Question #1 (ID: 442556)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]x=\sin \left(\cos ^{-1} y\right)[/math] হলে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}[/math] এর মান কত?
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\theta+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}-\cot ^{-1} 2=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]\cos ^{3} x \sin 3 x+\sin ^{3} x \cos 3 x=\tan \theta[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= x=\sin \sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\\= x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\\= \mathrm{x}^{2}=1-\mathrm{y}^{2}\\\therefore \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1 \text{.(Ans.)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খচিত্র থেকে পাই, [math]\tan \theta=\frac{3}{4} \therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}=\theta \ldots \ldots \text { (i) }[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}=2 \theta\\= \cos 2 \theta=\frac{5}{13}\\= \frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{5}{13}\\= 5+5 \tan ^{2} \theta=13-13 \tan ^{2} \theta\\= 18 \tan ^{2} \theta=8\\= \tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{8}{18}\\= \tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{4}{9}\\= \tan \theta=\frac{2}{3}\\= \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}[/math]
 [(i) নং হতে][math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ [math]=\theta+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}-\cot ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}\right)-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{2}{3}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{2}{3}}\right)-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{17}{6}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17}{6}-\frac{1}{2}}{1+\frac{17}{6} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{7}{3}}{\frac{29}{12}}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \theta+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}-\cot ^{-1} 2=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গপ্রদত্ত চিত্র হতে, [math]\tan \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\cos ^{3} x \sin 3 x+\sin ^{3} x \cdot \cos 3 x[/math]
 [math]= \tan \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]= 4 \cos ^{3} x \cdot \sin 3 x+4 \sin ^{3} x \cdot \cos 3 x=3[/math]
 [math]= 2 \cos ^{2} x \cdot 2 \sin 3 x \cos x+[/math]
 [math]2 \sin ^{2} x \cdot 2 \cos 3 x \sin x=3[/math]
 [math]= 2 \cos ^{2} x \cdot(\sin 4 x+\sin 2 x)[/math]
 [math]+2 \sin ^{2} x(\sin 4 x-\sin 2 x)=3[/math]
 [math]= 2 \cos ^{2} x \cdot \sin 4 x+2 \cos ^{2} x \cdot \sin 2 x+[/math]
 [math]2 \sin ^{2} x \cdot \sin 4 x-2 \sin ^{2} x \cdot \sin 2 x=3[/math]
 [math]= 2 \sin 4 x \cdot\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin ^{2} x\right)+[/math]
 [math]+2 \sin 2 x\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=3[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \sin 4 x+2 \sin 2 x \cdot \cos 2 x=3\\= 2 \sin 4 x+\sin 4 x=3\\= 3 \sin 4 x=3\\= \sin 4 x=1=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)\\= 4 x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8} ; n[/math]
 এর মানশূন্য অথবা যে কোন পূর্ণসংখ্যা। (Ans.)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক যেহেতু [math]x=\sin(\cos^{-1} y)[/math] হলে [math]\cos^{-1}y=t\Rightarrow \cos t=y,\ \sin t=\sqrt{1-y^2}[/math] (প্রধান শাখায় সাইন ধনাত্মক), তাই [math]x=\sqrt{1-y^2}[/math] এবং [math]x^2+y^2=1[/math]।

খ ধরো [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{5}{13}=\alpha[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\frac{\alpha}{2}=\frac{\sin\alpha}{1+\cos\alpha}=\frac{12/13}{18/13}=\frac{2}{3}[/math], এবং [math]\cot^{-1}2=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2}[/math]; তাই [math]\frac{1}{2}\cos^{-1}\frac{5}{13}-\cot^{-1}2=\tan^{-1}\frac{2}{3}-\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2}=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{8}[/math]. যদি [math]\tan\theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math] হয় তবে যোগফর্মুলা দিয়ে [math]\theta+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{8}=\tan^{-1}\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{8}}{1-\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{8}}=\tan^{-1}\frac{28}{29}[/math]। 

গ বাম পাশে সরলায়ন করলে, [math]\cos^3x\sin3x+\sin^3x\cos3x=3\sin x\cos x(\cos^2x-\sin^2x)=\tfrac{3}{4}\sin4x[/math]. তাই সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\tfrac{3}{4}\sin4x=\tan\theta[/math], শর্ত হিসেবে [math]\big|\tfrac{4}{3}\tan\theta\big|\le1[/math] থাকা জরুরি, এবং সমাধান হল [math]4x=\sin^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{4}{3}\tan\theta\big)+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]4x=\pi-\sin^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{4}{3}\tan\theta\big)+2k\pi[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]x=\tfrac{1}{4}\sin^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{4}{3}\tan\theta\big)+\tfrac{k\pi}{2}[/math] বা [math]x=\tfrac{1}{4}\big(\pi-\sin^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{4}{3}\tan\theta\big)\big)+\tfrac{k\pi}{2}[/math] (যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math])।

Question #2 (ID: 442557)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\tan x, g(x)=\tan ^{-1} x .[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]f\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]এর মান কত?
খ. প্রমাণ কর, [math]f(2 g(x))=2 f\left(g(x)+g\left(x^{3}\right)\right)[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]\mathrm{f}(\theta) \mathrm{f}(2 \theta)=1,0 \leq \theta \leq \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\tan \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left\{\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}-1}}{1}\right\}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{f}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\sqrt{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})=\tan \mathrm{x}, \mathrm{g}(\mathrm{x})=\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=f(2 g(x))=f\left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\right)=\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
ডানপক্ষ [math]=2 f\left(g(x)+g\left(x^{3}\right)\right)[/math]
 [math]=2 f\left(\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} x^{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} x^{3}\right) \\=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+x^{3}}{1-x \cdot x^{3}}\right) \\=2 \frac{x\left(1+x^{2}\right)}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)\left(1-x^{2}\right)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=[/math]
 বামপক্ষ[math]\therefore \mathrm{f}(2 \mathrm{~g}(\mathrm{x}))=2 \mathrm{f}\left(\mathrm{g}(\mathrm{x})+\mathrm{g}\left(\mathrm{x}^{3}\right)\right)[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(\theta) f(2 \theta)=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan \theta \cdot \tan 2 \theta=1 \\= \frac{\sin \theta \cdot \sin 2 \theta}{\cos \theta \cdot \cos 2 \theta}=1 \\=\cos \theta \cdot \cos 2 \theta=\sin \theta \cdot \sin 2 \theta \\= \cos 2 \theta \cdot \cos \theta-\sin 2 \theta \cdot \sin \theta=0 \\=\cos (2 \theta+\theta)=0 \\= \cos 3 \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{2} \\= 3 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 যেখানে n-এর মান শূন্যঅথবা যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=3 \cdot \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 যা গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়।কারণ [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 এর জন্য (i) নং সত্য নয়।[math]n = 2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=5 \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = 3[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=7 \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{7 \pi}{6}>\pi[/math]
নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধিতে নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]f\left(\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\tan\left(\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]। এখানে [math]\cos\theta=1/\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে [math]\sin\theta=\sqrt{1-1/3}=\sqrt{2/3}[/math], তাই [math]\tan\theta=\dfrac{\sqrt{2/3}}{1/\sqrt{3}}=\sqrt{2}[/math].

খ LHS: [math]f(2g(x))=\tan(2\arctan x)=\dfrac{2\tan(\arctan x)}{1-\tan^2(\arctan x)}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math]. RHS: [math]2f(g(x)+g(x^3))=2\tan(\arctan x+\arctan x^3)=2\cdot\dfrac{x+x^3}{1-x^4}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math]. ফলে দুইপক্ষ সমান (উল্লেখ্য যে বিভাজক শূন্য নয়)।

গ সমীকরণ: [math]\tan\theta\cdot\tan2\theta=1[/math]. কিন্তু [math]\tan2\theta=\dfrac{2\tan\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}[/math], ফলে [math]\dfrac{2\tan^2\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}=1[/math] হওয়ায় [math]\tan^2\theta=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math]. তাই [math]\tan\theta=\pm\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] এবং [math]0\le\theta\le\pi[/math]-এ সমাধানগুলো হল [math]\theta=\dfrac{\pi}{6},\ \dfrac{5\pi}{6}[/math]।

Question #3 (ID: 442558)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]g\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]f(\pi \cos \theta)=g(\pi \sin \theta)[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}} .[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{f(2 x)}-\frac{1}{g(2 x)}=4 .[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore g\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right)=\cos \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right) \\=\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)^{2}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)^{2}}\right)=\frac{1-\frac{1}{49}}{1+\frac{1}{49}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{49-1}{49} \cdot \frac{49}{49+1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{48}{50}=\frac{24}{25} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\begin{array}{l}f(x)=\sin x \\\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\cos x \\f\left(\pi f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)\right)=f(\pi \cos x)=\sin (\pi \cos x)\end{array}[/math]
আবার [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi f(x)\right)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \sin x\right)[/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \sin x\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cos (\pi \sin x)[/math]
দেওয়া আছে, [math]f\left(\pi f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)\right)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi f(x)\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin (\pi \cos x)=\cos (\pi \sin x)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin (\pi \cos x)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x\right)\\= \pi \cos x=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \cos x=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin x[/math]
 বা, [math]\cos x \pm \sin x=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos x \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin x=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষে [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 দ্বারা গুণ করে][math]= \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos x \pm \sin \frac{\pi}{4} \sin x=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\cos \left(x \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right),=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= x \pm \frac{\pi}{4}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}} \\\therefore x= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
(দেখানো হলো).[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে [math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x[/math]
এখন [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{f(2 x)}-\frac{1}{g(2 x)}=4[/math]
 বা [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sin 2 x}-\frac{1}{\cos 2 x}=4[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sqrt{3} \cos 2 x-\sin 2 x=4 \sin 2 x \cos 2 x\\= \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos 2 x-\frac{1}{2} \sin 2 x=2 \sin 2 x \cos 2 x\\=\cos \frac{\pi}{6} \cos 2 x-\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \sin 2 x=\sin 4 x\\=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{6}+2 x\right)=\sin 4 x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{6}+2 x\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)\\= \frac{\pi}{6}+2 x=2 n \pi \pm\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]'+'[/math]
 চিহ্ন নিয়ে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\pi}{6}+2 x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\\= 6 x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{6}\\= 6 x=\left(2 n+\frac{3-1}{6}\right) \pi\\=x=\left(\frac{n}{3}+\frac{1}{18}\right) \pi\\\therefore x=(6 n+1) \frac{\pi}{18}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]'-'[/math]
 চিহ্ন নিয়ে[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\pi}{6}+2 x=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}+4 x\\= 4 x-2 x=-2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{\pi}{2}\\= 2 x=\left(-2 n+\frac{1+3}{6}\right) \pi\\= x=\left(-n+\frac{1}{3}\right) \pi\\\therefore x=(-3 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান,[math]x=(6 n+1) \frac{\pi}{18},(-3 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক [math]g(2\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{7})=\cos(2\tan^{-1}t)[/math] এ জন্য সাধারণ সূত্র [math]\cos(2\tan^{-1}t)=\frac{1-t^{2}}{1+t^{2}}[/math] ব্যবহার করলে [math]t=\tfrac{1}{7}[/math] এ [math]\frac{1-(1/7)^{2}}{1+(1/7)^{2}}=\frac{48/49}{50/49}=\frac{24}{25}[/math] পাওয়া যায়।
খ [math]\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\cos(\pi\sin\theta)[/math] থেকে [math]\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\pi\sin\theta\big)[/math] এবং সরলীকরণ করলে [math]\cos\theta+\sin\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]; এখানে [math]\cos\theta+\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}\cos(\theta-\tfrac{\pi}{4})[/math] হওয়ায় [math]\cos(\theta-\tfrac{\pi}{4})=\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math} এবং তাই [math]\theta=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{4}+\cos^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\big)[/math] ধরনের সমাধান পাওয়া যায়।
গ শুরুতে [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sin2x}-\frac{1}{\cos2x}=4[/math] লিখে যৌথভাবে রূপান্তর করলে সূত্রগতভাবে সংখ্যাগুণ দিয়ে পাওয়া যায় [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}\cos2x-\sin2x}{\sin2x\cos2x}=4[/math]; উপরিভাগকে রূপান্তর করলে [math]2\cos(2x+\tfrac{\pi}{6})=4\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}\sin4x[/math] হয়, তাই [math]\cos(2x+\tfrac{\pi}{6})=\sin4x[/math] । এটি থেকে [math]\sin4x=\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{3}-2x)[/math] হলে প্রথম ক্ষেত্রে [math]6x=\tfrac{\pi}{3}+2k\pi\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{18}+k\frac{\pi}{3}[/math] এবং দ্বিতীয় ক্ষেত্রে [math]2x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+2k\pi\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{3}+k\pi[/math] পাওয়া যায়।

Question #4 (ID: 442559)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]a \cos \theta+b \sin \theta=c[/math] একটি সমীকরণ।

Options:
ক. [math]\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}[/math] এর মান কত? খ, [math]a=b=c=1[/math] হলে উদ্দীপকের সমীকরণটি [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math] ব্যবধিতে সমাধান কর।
গ. সমীকরণটির মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha[/math] ও [math]\beta[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin (\alpha+\beta)=\frac{2 a b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\=\arctan \left[\sin \left\{\arccos \sqrt{1-\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}}\right\}\right] \\=\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arcsin \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\=\arctan \left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\arctan \left(\tan \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\=\frac{\pi}{6} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]a = b=c=1[/math]
এবং [math]a \cos \theta+b \sin \theta=c[/math]
 সমীকরণটি দাঁড়ায়,[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos \theta+\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\= \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{4}\\\therefore \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\\= \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4} \text{.}\\= \theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}, 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 n \pi,(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \text {, }[/math]
 যখন n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্যযে কোন পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=0, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=2 \pi, \frac{5 \pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-2 \pi,-\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট সীমার মধ্যে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মান সমূহ: [math]-\frac{3 \pi}{2}, 0, \frac{\pi}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে [math]a \cos \theta+b \sin \theta=c......[/math]
 (i)[math]\begin{array}{l}= a \cos \theta=c-b \sin \theta\\= a^{2} \cos ^{2} \theta=c^{2}-2 b c \sin \theta+b^{2} \sin ^{2} \theta\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষে বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}= a^{2}\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)=c^{2}-2 b c \sin \theta+b^{2} \sin ^{2} \theta\\= a^{2}-a^{2} \sin ^{2} \theta=c^{2}-2 b \sin \theta+b^{2} \sin ^{2} \theta\\= \left(a^{2}+b^{2}\right) \sin ^{2} \theta-2 b c \sin \theta+c^{2}-a^{2}=0......... (ii)\end{array}[/math]
যা একটি দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণ, যার চলক [math]\sin \theta[/math]
।যেহেতু, (i) সমীকরণটির মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha, \beta[/math]
 (প্রশ্নানুসারে)সেহেতু, (ii) সমীকরণটির মূলদ্বয় [math]\sin \alpha[/math]
 ও [math]\sin \beta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \alpha+\sin \beta=\frac{2 b c}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
এবং [math]\sin \alpha \cdot \sin \beta=\frac{c^{2}-a^{2}}{a^{2}+b^{2}} \ldots \ldots .[/math]
 (iii)আবার, [math]\mathrm{a} \cos \theta+\mathrm{b} \sin \theta=\mathrm{c}[/math]
 [math]= a+b \tan \theta=c \sec \theta[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\cos \theta[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]= \mathrm{a}^{2}+\mathrm{b}^{2} \tan ^{2} \theta+2 \mathrm{ab} \tan \theta=\mathrm{c}^{2} \sec ^{2} \theta[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}= a^{2}+b^{2} \tan ^{2} \theta+2 a b \tan \theta=c^{2}+c^{2} \tan ^{2} \theta\\= a^{2}+b^{2} \tan ^{2} \theta+2 a b \tan \theta-c^{2}-c^{2} \tan ^{2} \theta=0\\= \left(b^{2}-c^{2}\right) \tan ^{2} \theta+2 a b \tan \theta+a^{2}-c^{2}=0.........(iv)\end{array}[/math]
যা একটি দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণ, যার চলক [math]\tan \theta[/math]
যেহেতু (i) নং সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha, \beta[/math]
 (প্রশ্নানুসারে)সেহেতু, (iv) নং সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\tan \alpha[/math]
 ও [math]\tan \beta \text {. }[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \alpha+\tan \beta=\frac{-2 \mathrm{ab}}{\mathrm{b}^{2}-\mathrm{c}^{2}}[/math]
 এবং[math]\tan \alpha \cdot \tan \beta=\frac{a^{2}-c^{2}}{b^{2}-c^{2}}.....[/math]
(v)এখন [math]\tan \alpha+\tan \beta=\frac{-2 a b}{b^{2}-c^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{\sin \alpha \cos \beta+\cos \alpha \sin \beta}{\cos \alpha \cos \beta}=-\frac{2 a b}{b^{2}-c^{2}} \\= \frac{\sin (\alpha+\beta) \tan \alpha \cdot \tan \beta}{\sin \alpha \cdot \sin \beta}=-\frac{2 a b}{b^{2}-c^{2}} \\= \sin (\alpha+\beta)=-\frac{2 a b}{b^{2}-c^{2}} \cdot \frac{\sin \alpha \cdot \sin \beta}{\tan \alpha \cdot \tan \beta}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=-\frac{2 a b}{b^{2}-c^{2}} \frac{c^{2}-a^{2}}{a^{2}+b^{2}} \cdot \frac{b^{2}-c^{2}}{a^{2}-c^{2}}[/math]
[(iii) ও (v) নং সমীকরণ হতে].[math]\therefore \sin (\alpha+\beta)=\frac{2 a b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক কারণ যদি [math]\phi=\arccos\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] করে নেওয়া হয় তবে [math]\sin\phi=\sqrt{1-\frac{2}{3}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] আর তাই [math]\arctan\{\sin(\arccos(\sqrt{2}/\sqrt{3}))\}=\arctan\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]।

খ [math]\cos\theta+\sin\theta=1[/math] কে লেখো [math]\sqrt{2}\sin(\theta+\pi/4)=1[/math], অতএব [math]\sin(\theta+\pi/4)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] এবং [math]\theta+\pi/4=\pi/4+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]=3\pi/4+2k\pi[/math]; ফলে [math]\theta=0+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]\theta=\pi/2+2k\pi[/math]। সীমা [math]-2\piগ নিন [math]R=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}},\ \phi=\arctan\frac{b}{a}, তাহলে [math]a=R\cos\phi,\ b=R\sin\phi[/math] এবং সমীকরণটি হয় [math]R\cos(\theta-\phi)=c[/math]; মূলদ্বয় সমমিত হওয়ায় [math]\alpha+\beta=2\phi[/math], অতএব [math]\sin(\alpha+\beta)=\sin2\phi=2\sin\phi\cos\phi=\frac{2ab}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]।

Question #5 (ID: 442560)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{(i) } f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x\\\text{(ii) } \cos ^{3} x-\cos x \sin x-\sin ^{3} x=1\end{array}[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]f\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+f\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4} .[/math]
খ. সমাধান কর: [math]f(x+1)+f(x)+f(x-1)=f(3 x)[/math]
গ. (ii) নং সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=f\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+f\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3+2}{6-1}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}(1)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore f\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+f\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
এখন, [math]f(x+1)+f(x)+f(x-1)=f(3 x)[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1}(x+1)+\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1}(x-1)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1 /} 3 x[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1}(x+1)+\tan ^{-1}(x-1)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 3 x-\tan ^{1} x[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+1+x-1}{1-(x+1)(x-1)}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3 x-x}{1+3 x \cdot x}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{2 x}{2-x^{2}}=\frac{2 x}{1+3 x^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= x\left(1+3 x^{2}\right)=x\left(2-x^{2}\right)[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে 2 দ্বারা ভাগ কর][math]=x\left(1+3 x^{2}-2+x^{2}\right)=0[/math]
 বা,[math]x\left(4 x^{2}-1\right)=0[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{x}(2 \mathrm{x}+1)(2 \mathrm{x}-1)=0 \therefore \mathrm{x}=0, \pm \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=0, \pm \frac{1}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{3} x-\cos x \cdot \sin x-\sin ^{3} x=1 \\= \cos ^{3} x-\sin ^{3} x-\cos x \cdot \sin x=1 \\=(\cos x-\sin x)\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin ^{2} x+\cos x \sin x\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]-(1+\cos x \cdot \sin x)=0[/math]
 [math]=(\cos x-\sin x)(1+\cos x \sin x)[/math]
 [math]-(1+\cos x \sin x)=0[/math]
 [math]=(1+\cos x \sin x)(\cos x-\sin x-1)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos x-\sin x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos x-\sin x=1\\= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\= \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos x-\sin x \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]1+\cos x \sin x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos x \sin x=-1\\= 2 \cos x \sin x=-2\\\therefore \sin 2 x=-2\end{array}[/math]
[গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়][math]\begin{array}{l}= \cos \left(x+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\= \cos \left(x+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\\= x+\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{4}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=2 n \pi, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=2 n \pi, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
যেখানে n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যে কোন পূর্ণসংখ্যা। (Ans.)

Explanation:
ক [math]\tan^{-1}a+\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] সূত্র প্রয়োগ করলে [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{2}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{3}=\tan^{-1}\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{1}{3}}{1-\tfrac{1}{6}}=\tan^{-1}1=[/math] [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math], তাই সমান।

খ প্রথমে [math]\tan^{-1}(x+1)+\tan^{-1}(x-1)=\tan^{-1}\frac{2x}{2-x^{2}}[/math] এবং তা সাথে [math]\tan^{-1}x[/math] যোগ করলে মোট হবে [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{4x-x^{3}}{2-3x^{2}}[/math]। সমীকরণ অনুযায়ী [math]\frac{4x-x^{3}}{2-3x^{2}}=3x[/math] হয়ে যায়, যা সরল করলে [math]2x(1-4x^{2})=0[/math] এবং ফলে [math]x=0,\;x=\pm\frac{1}{2}[/math] (প্রধান মূল্য ধরা হলে এগুলো সমাধান)।

গ [math]\cos^{3}x-\sin^{3}x=(\cos x-\sin x)(1+\cos x\sin x)[/math] ব্যবহার করে মূল সমীকরণকে লিখলে [math](\cos x-\sin x)(1+\cos x\sin x)-\cos x\sin x=1[/math] হয়, যা থেকে [math](\cos x-\sin x-1)(1+\cos x\sin x)=0[/math] না হয়ে গেলে বলি [math]\cos x\sin x=-1[/math] যা অসম্ভব; অতএব [math]\cos x-\sin x=1[/math] এবং তখন [math]\cos x\sin x=0[/math] হয়। এ থেকে সমাধানগুলো হল [math]x=2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi\;(k\in\mathbb{Z})[/math]।

Question #6 (ID: 442554)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


[math]\text { (ii) } \operatorname{cosec} 2 \theta=\sec \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{33}{19} .[/math]
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{\pi}{3} .[/math]
গ. [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math] ব্যবধিতে (ii) এর সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{5}{2}-\frac{1}{7}}{1+\frac{5}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{7}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{35-2}{14}}{\frac{14+5}{14}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{33}{19}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{33}{19}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খ১ম চিত্র হতে পাই, [math]\cos \alpha=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \ldots \ldots \ldots \ldots[/math]
(i)২য় চিত্র হতে পাই, [math]\cos \beta=\frac{\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{2}}{2 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{2}}{2 \sqrt{3}}\right).........[/math]
(ii)(i) ও (ii) নং সমীকরণ হতে পাই,[math]\alpha+\beta=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{2}}{2 \sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \left[\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}} \cdot \frac{\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{2}}{2 \sqrt{3}}-\right.[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{\left.\left\{1-\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}\left\{1-\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{2}}{.2 \sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}\right\}\right]}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1}\left[\frac{\sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}+2}{6}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{2}{3}\right)\left(1-\frac{3+2 \sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}+2}{12}\right)}\right] \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}+2}{6}-\sqrt{\frac{1}{3} \cdot \frac{12-3-2 \sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}-2}{12}}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{2+\sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}}{6}-\frac{\sqrt{7-2 \sqrt{3} \sqrt{2}}}{6}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{2+\sqrt{6}-\sqrt{(\sqrt{6}-1)^{2}}}{6}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{2+\sqrt{6}-\sqrt{6}+1}{6}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha+\beta=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]\operatorname{cosec} 2 \theta=\sec \theta[/math]
 [math]= \frac{1}{\sin 2 \theta}=\frac{1}{\cos \theta}[/math]
 বা, [math]\cos \theta=\sin 2 \theta[/math]
বা, [math]\cos \theta=2 \sin \theta \cdot \cos \theta[/math]
 [math]= \cos \theta-2 \sin \theta \cdot \cos \theta=0[/math]
 বা, [math]\cos \theta(1-2 \sin \theta)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{2}(2 n+1) \pi[/math]
অথবা, [math]1-2 \sin \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \sin \theta=1 \\=\sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
যেখানে, n এর মান শূন্য অথবা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা।এখন, n এর মান [math]0, \pm 1, \pm 2, \ldots \ldots .[/math]
 বসিয়ে পাই,[math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{3 \pi}{2}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{2},-\frac{7 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{2}, \frac{13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=-2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{3 \pi}{2},-\frac{11 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট সীমার মধ্যে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মানসমূহ:[math]-\frac{11 \pi}{6},-\frac{7 \pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{6}, \pm \frac{\pi}{2}, \pm \frac{3 \pi}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক সরাসরি পরিচিত সূত্র [math]\tan^{-1}x-\tan^{-1}y=\tan^{-1}\frac{x-y}{1+xy}[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে [math]\frac{5}{2}-\frac{1}{7}=\frac{33}{14}[/math] এবং [math]1+\frac{5}{2}\cdot\frac{1}{7}=\frac{19}{14}[/math] হওয়ায় [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{5}{2}-\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{7}=\tan^{-1}\frac{33}{19}[/math] হয়।

খ এই অংশটি প্রমাণ করার জন্য α ও β সম্পর্কে প্রাথমিক শর্তগুলো প্রয়োজন; দেওয়া নেই, তাই পর্যাপ্ত তথ্য নেই।

গ সমাধান: [math]\csc2\theta=\sec\theta[/math] হলে [math]\frac{1}{\sin2\theta}=\frac{1}{\cos\theta}\Rightarrow\cos\theta=\sin2\theta=2\sin\theta\cos\theta[/math], ফলে [math]\cos\theta(1-2\sin\theta)=0[/math]. কিন্তু [math]\sec\theta[/math] সংজ্ঞায়িত থাকতে হবে, তাই [math]\cos\theta=0[/math] গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়। তাই [math]\sin\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], অর্থাৎ সাধারণ সমাধান [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]\theta=\tfrac{5\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math] (k∈ℤ)। সীমা [math]-2\pi<\theta<2\pi[/math]-এ মানগুলो হল [math]\theta=-\tfrac{11\pi}{6},\,-\tfrac{7\pi}{6},\,\tfrac{\pi}{6},\,\tfrac{5\pi}{6}[/math]।

Question #7 (ID: 442555)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin Z=\frac{2 p}{1+p^{2}}, \cos M=\frac{1-q^{2}}{1+q^{2}}[/math] [math]\tan \frac{\mathrm{N}}{2}=x, \cos L=\frac{r}{s}, \cos R=\frac{e}{f}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]\mathrm{Z}-\mathrm{M}=\mathrm{N}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x=\frac{p-q}{1+p q}[/math]
গ. [math]L+R=\theta[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\frac{r^{2}}{s^{2}}-\frac{2 \mathrm{recos} \theta}{s f}+\frac{e^{2}}{f^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta .[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}\right)=\frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{9+1}{9}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2}{3} \cdot \frac{9}{10}=\frac{3}{5} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
এখন, [math]2 f(x)=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \tan ^{-1} a-2 \tan ^{-1} b=2 \tan ^{-1} x\\= \tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1} b=\tan ^{-1} x\\= \tan ^{-1} \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=\tan ^{-1} x\\= \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\frac{\mathrm{a}-\mathrm{b}}{1+\mathrm{ab}}[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]\cos \alpha=\frac{x}{a}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}[/math]
এবং [math]\cos \beta=\frac{y}{b}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}[/math]
আবার, দেওয়া আছে, [math]\alpha+\beta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\text{= } \cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}=\theta\\= \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{a} \cdot \frac{y}{b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}=\cos \theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta=\sqrt{1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}\\= \left(\frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta\right)^{2}=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]= \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-2 \frac{x y}{a b} \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-2 \frac{x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=1-\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক  সূত্র [math]\sin(2\theta)=\dfrac{2\tan\theta}{1+\tan^2\theta}[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে, [math]\tan\theta=\dfrac{1}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\sin\left(2\tan^{-1}\dfrac{1}{3}\right)=\dfrac{2\cdot\frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^2}=\dfrac{3}{5}[/math].

খ যেহেতু [math]\sin Z=\dfrac{2p}{1+p^2},\ \cos Z=\dfrac{1-p^2}{1+p^2}[/math] তাই [math]p=\tan\frac{Z}{2}[/math], এবং একইভাবে [math]q=\tan\frac{M}{2}[/math]. যদি [math]N=Z-M[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]\tan\frac{N}{2}=\tan\left(\frac{Z}{2}-\frac{M}{2}\right)=\dfrac{p-q}{1+pq}[/math] হয়।

গ [math]\cos\theta=\cos(L+R)=\cos L\cos R-\sin L\sin R[/math] হওয়ায় [math]\sin L\sin R=\cos L\cos R-\cos\theta[/math] এবং বর্গ করলে [math](1-\cos^2L)(1-\cos^2R)=(\cos L\cos R-\cos\theta)^2[/math] পাওয়া যায়; এখন [math]\cos L=\dfrac{r}{s},\ \cos R=\dfrac{e}{f}[/math] বসালে সরলীকরণ করে পাওয়া যায় [math]\dfrac{r^{2}}{s^{2}}-\dfrac{2re\cos\theta}{sf}+\dfrac{e^{2}}{f^{2}}=\sin^{2}\theta[/math]।

Question #8 (ID: 442552)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math] এবং


[Image]


Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]f(1+a)-f(a)=f\left(\frac{1}{1+a+a^{2}}\right)[/math] প্রমাণ করে এর সাহায্যে দেখাও যে, [math]\cot ^{-1} 3+\cot ^{-1} 7+\cot ^{-1} 13+\cot ^{-1} 21=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}[/math]
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \cdot \cos \tan ^{-1} \sec \theta=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\text { L.H.S. }=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{6} \times \frac{6}{6-1}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{5}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}=\text { R.H.S }[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\begin{array}{l}\text { L.H.S. }=f(1+a)-f(a) \\=\tan ^{-1}(1+a)-\tan ^{-1} a\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1+a-a}{1+(1+a) \cdot a}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{1+a+a^{2}}=f\left(\frac{1}{1+a+a^{2}}\right)=\text { R.H.S. } \\\therefore \tan ^{-1}(1+a)-\tan ^{-1} a=\cot ^{-1}\left(1+a+a^{2}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]a=1,2,3,4[/math]
 বসিয়ে আমরা পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} 2-\tan ^{-1} 1=\cot ^{-1} 3 \\\tan ^{-1} 3-\tan ^{-1} 2=\cot ^{-1} 7 \\\tan ^{-1} 4-\tan ^{-1} 3=\cot ^{-1} 13 \\\tan ^{-1} 5-\tan ^{-1} 4=\cot ^{-1} 21\end{array}[/math]
যোগ করে আমরা পাই,[math]\tan ^{-1} 5-\tan ^{-1} 1=\cot ^{-1} 3+\cot ^{-1} 7+\cot ^{-1} 13+\cot ^{-1} 21[/math]
এখানে,[math]\tan ^{-1} 5-\tan ^{-1} 1=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5-1}{1+5.1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{6}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}[/math]
 [math]\cot ^{-1} 3+\cot ^{-1} 7+\cot ^{-1} 13+\cot ^{-1} 21[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}[/math]
 (দেখানো হল)[math]\\[/math]
 গচিত্র হতে পাই, [math]\sec \theta=\frac{A C}{B C}=\frac{x}{1}=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\text { L.H.S. }=\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}[/math]

[Image]
[পাশের চিত্র হতে][math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{\sqrt{2+x^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[পাশের চিত্র হতে][math]=\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{\sqrt{2+x^{2}}}=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}=\text { R.H.S. }[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক arctan যোগের সূত্র ব্যবহার করলে [math]\tan^{-1}a+\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] (যদি [math]abখ যেহেতু [math]f(x)=\tan^{-1}x[/math], আমরা জানি [math]\tan^{-1}u-\tan^{-1}v=\tan^{-1}\frac{u-v}{1+uv}[/math]; এখানে [math]u=1+a,\ v=a[/math] দিলে [math]f(1+a)-f(a)=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{1+a+a^{2}}=f\!\left(\frac{1}{1+a+a^{2}}\right)[/math]. এরপর প্রতিটি [math]\cot^{-1}k=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{k}[/math] ধরে ধারাবাহিকভাবে যোগ করলে: [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{7}=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2},\ \tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2}+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{13}=\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{5},\ \tan^{-1}\frac{3}{5}+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{21}=\tan^{-1}\frac{2}{3}[/math], অতএব [math]\cot^{-1}3+\cot^{-1}7+\cot^{-1}13+\cot^{-1}21=\cot^{-1}\frac{3}{2}[/math]।

গ এই অংশের সূত্রে টাইপোগ্রাফিক্যাল ত্রুটি আছে (অভিব্যক্তি অসম্পূর্ণ বা মিশ্রিত ভ্যারিয়েবল আছে); অনুগ্রহ করে সঠিক পূর্ণ অবিকৃত সূত্রটি দিন যেন সংক্ষিপ্ত ও সঠিক প্রমাণ দেখানো যায়।

Question #9 (ID: 442553)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { (i) } \sin ^{-1} x-\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math] [math]\text { (ii) } \sin x-\sin 3 x=\cos 2 x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\tan \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{7}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. (i) এর সাহায্যে দেখাও যে, [math]4\left(x^{2}+y^{2}-x y\right)=3[/math]
গ. (ii) এর সমীকরণটি [math](-\pi \leq \mathrm{x} \leq \pi)[/math] ব্যবধিতে সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{7}\right)=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times \frac{3}{7}}{1-\left(\frac{3}{7}\right)^{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\frac{2 \times 3}{7}}{1-\frac{9}{49}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{2 \times 3}{7} \times \frac{49}{49-9} \\=\frac{2 \times 3 \times 7}{40}=\frac{21}{20} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\sin ^{-1} x-\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]= \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x-\cos ^{-1} x-\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{\pi}{2}-\left(\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y\right)=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{6}\\=\cos ^{-1}\left(x y-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)=\frac{\pi}{3}\\= x y-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\\= x y-\frac{1}{2}=\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\left(x y-\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}=\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)[/math]
 [উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]= x^{2} y^{2}-2 x y \cdot \frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [math]=1-x^{2}-y^{2}+x^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= -x y+\frac{1}{4}=1-x^{2}-y^{2}\\= x^{2}+y^{2}-x y=1-\frac{1}{4}=\frac{4-1}{4}=\frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore 4\left(x^{2}+y^{2}-x y\right)=3[/math]
. (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\begin{array}{l}\sin x-\sin 3 x=\cos 2 x \\= 2 \cos \left(\frac{x+3 x}{2}\right) \sin \left(\frac{x-3 x}{2}\right)=\cos 2 x \\= 2 \cos \left(\frac{4 x}{2}\right) \sin \left(-\frac{2 x}{2}\right)=\cos 2 x\\= 2 \cos 2 x \sin (-x)=\cos 2 x\\=\cos 2 x+2 \cos 2 x \sin x=0\\= \cos 2 x(1+2 \sin x)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]1+2 \sin x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \sin x=-1 \\= \sin x=-\frac{1}{2}=\sin \left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\= \sin x=\sin \left(\pi+\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\therefore x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{7 \pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
যেখানে, n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যে কোন পূর্ণসংখ্যা।এখন, [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{7 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{3 \pi}{4},-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=-\frac{\pi}{4},-\frac{13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=2[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{5 \pi}{4}, \frac{19 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n= -2[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=-\frac{3 \pi}{4},-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট সীমার মধ্যে x এর মান সমূহ:[math]\begin{array}{l}\pm \frac{\pi}{4}, \pm \frac{3 \pi}{4},-\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{5 \pi}{6}\\\text{(Ans.)}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{7}[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan(2\theta)=\frac{2\tan\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}=\frac{2\cdot\frac{3}{7}}{1-\left(\frac{3}{7}\right)^2}=\frac{\frac{6}{7}}{\frac{40}{49}}=\frac{21}{20}[/math].

খ [math]\alpha=\sin^{-1}x,\ \beta=\cos^{-1}y[/math] নিয়ে [math]\alpha-\beta=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math] হলে [math]y=\cos(\alpha-\pi/6)=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\cos\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\sin\alpha[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]2y=\sqrt{3}\cos\alpha+x[/math]; এখানে [math]\cos\alpha=\sqrt{1-x^2}[/math] থাকায় স্কোয়ার করে সাধারণীকরণ করলে [math](2y-x)^2=3(1-x^2)[/math] থেকে সরলকরণ করে পাওয়া যায় [math]4(x^2+y^2-xy)=3[/math]।

গ পরিচিত পরিচয় দিয়ে [math]\sin x-\sin3x=-2\cos2x\sin x[/math] ফলে সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\cos2x(-2\sin x-1)=0[/math]; সুতরাং (i) [math]\cos2x=0\Rightarrow x=\frac{\pi}{4}+k\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] থেকে সীমায় [math]x=-\tfrac{3\pi}{4},-\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{3\pi}{4}[/math], এবং (ii) [math]\sin x=-\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] থেকে সীমায় [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{6},-\tfrac{5\pi}{6}[/math]।

Question #10 (ID: 442547)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} x+2 \cot ^{-1} x=\frac{2}{3} \pi[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sec ^{2} x+\operatorname{cosec}^{2} y=11 \frac{1}{4}[/math]
গ. দেখাও যে, [math]z=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\tan ^{-1} x+2 \cot ^{-1} x=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan ^{-1} x+2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\\= \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{1}{x}}{1-\frac{1}{x^{2}}}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\\= \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{x} \cdot \frac{x^{2}}{x^{2}-1}\right)=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\\= \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{x^{2}-1}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\\= \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+\frac{2 x}{x^{2}-1}}{1-x \cdot \frac{2 x}{x^{2}-1}}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{x^{3}-x+2 x}{x^{2}-1-2 x^{2}}=\tan \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\= \frac{x^{3}+x}{-x^{2}-1}=-\sqrt{3} \\= \frac{x\left(x^{2}+1\right)}{1+x^{2}}=\sqrt{3} \\\therefore x=\sqrt{3} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec ^{2} x+\operatorname{cosec}^{2} y \\=(\sqrt{10})^{2}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right)^{2}=10+\frac{5}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{45}{4}=11 \cdot \frac{1}{4}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গচিত্রানুসারে পাই, [math]x+y+z=\pi[/math]
 [math]= z=\pi-(x+y)=\pi-\left(\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \frac{3+2}{1-3.2}=\pi-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{-5}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1}(-1)[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\frac{3 \pi}{4}=\frac{4 \pi-3 \pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)

Explanation:
ক সমাধান: [math]\cot^{-1}x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\tan^{-1}x[/math] (যখন [math]x>0[/math]) বোঝায় [math]\tan^{-1}x+2\Big(\frac{\pi}{2}-\tan^{-1}x\Big)=\frac{2\pi}{3}[/math], ফলে [math]\tan^{-1}x=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math] এবং তাই [math]x=\tan\frac{\pi}{3}=\sqrt{3}[/math]।

খ ব্যাখ্যা: ব্যবহার কর [math]\sec^{2}x=1+\tan^{2}x[/math] এবং [math]\csc^{2}y=1+\cot^{2}y[/math]; তারপর (ক) ও (গ)-এর মাধ্যমে পাওয়া [math]\tan x[/math] ও [math]\cot y[/math] এর মানগুলিকে প্রতিস্থাপন করলে সরাসরি [math]\sec^{2}x+\csc^{2}y=11\frac{1}{4}=\frac{45}{4}[/math] পাওয়া যায়।

গ দেখাও: একইভাবে [math]\tan^{-1}z+2\cot^{-1}z=\frac{2\pi}{3}[/math]-এর মতো সমীকরণ থেকে [math]\cot^{-1}z=\frac{\pi}{2}-\tan^{-1}z[/math] ব্যবহার করে পাওয়া যায় [math]\tan^{-1}z=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math], ফলে [math]z=\tan\frac{\pi}{4}=1[/math] (অতএব [math]z=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] কোণটির মান)।

Question #11 (ID: 442548)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x, h(x)=\cot ^{-1}(x)[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{m}{n}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{m-n}{m+n}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]f(\pi \operatorname{cosec} \theta)=g\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{4 n+1}\right)[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{1-4 n}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]h(2 n-1)-h(2 n+1)=h\left(2 n^{2}\right)[/math] প্রমাণ করে এর সাহায্যে দেখাও যে, [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(2.1^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right) \cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{m}}{\mathrm{n}}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{m}-\mathrm{n}}{\mathrm{m}+\mathrm{n}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{m}{n}-\frac{m-n}{m+n}}{1+\frac{m}{n} \cdot \frac{m-n}{m+n}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{m^{2}+m n-m n+n^{2}}{m n+n^{2}+m^{2}-m n}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{m^{2}+m n-m n+n^{2}}{m n+n^{2}+m^{2}-m n}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 ও [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
শর্তমতে, [math]f(\pi \operatorname{cosec} \theta)=g\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin (\pi \operatorname{cosec} \theta)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)\\= \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)\\= \frac{\pi}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta=2 n \pi \pm\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)\\= \frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec} \theta=2 n \pm\left(\frac{1}{2}-\operatorname{cosec} \theta\right)\\\therefore \operatorname{cosec} \theta=4 n \pm(1-2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta)\end{array}[/math]
(+) নিয়ে, [math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta=4 n+1-2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 3 \operatorname{cosec} \theta=4 n+1 \\\therefore \operatorname{cosec} \theta=\frac{4 n+1}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\sin \theta=\frac{3}{4 n+1}[/math]
 [math]= \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{1+4 n}[/math]
 [math](-)[/math]
 নিয়ে, [math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta=4 n-(1-2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\operatorname{cosec} \theta=4 n-1+2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta\\= \operatorname{cosec} \theta=1-4 n\\= \sin \theta=\frac{1}{1-4 n}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{1-4 n}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]h(x)=\cot ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=h(2 n-1)-h(2 n+1)[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1}(2 n-1)-\cot ^{-1}(2 n+1)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 n-1}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 n-1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2 n-1}-\frac{1}{2 n+1}}{1+\frac{1}{2 n-1} \frac{1}{2 n+1}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 n+1-2 n+1}{(2 n-1)(2 n+1)+1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{4 n^{2}-1+1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{4 n^{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 n^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1}\left(2 n^{2}\right)=h\left(2 n^{2}\right)=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)শর্তমতে, [math]h(2 n-1)-h(2 n+1)[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{h}\left(2 \mathrm{n}^{2}\right) \ldots \ldots \ldots \text { (i) }[/math]
(i) নং এ [math]n = 1, 2, 3[/math]
 বসিয়ে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{h}\left(2.1^{2}\right)=\mathrm{h}(1)-\mathrm{h}(3) \\\mathrm{h}\left(2.2^{2}\right)=\mathrm{h}(3)-\mathrm{h}(5) \\\mathrm{h}\left(2.3^{2}\right)=\mathrm{h}(5)-\mathrm{h}(7)\end{array}[/math]
যোগ করে পাই, [math]h\left(2.1^{2}\right)+h\left(2.2^{2}\right)+h\left(2.3^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=h(1)-h(7)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \cot ^{-1}\left(2.1^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right) \\=\cot ^{-1} 1-\cot ^{-1} 7=\tan ^{-1} 1-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{1}{7}}{1+\frac{1}{7}} \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{6}{7} \times \frac{7}{8}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)

Explanation:
ক সমীকরণটি প্রমাণ করতে [math]\tan^{-1}a-\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a-b}{1+ab}[/math] ব্যবহার করুন; এখানে [math]a=\frac{m}{n},\; b=\frac{m-n}{m+n}[/math] নিলে
[math]\frac{a-b}{1+ab}=1[/math] হয়, অতএব লেফটসাইড সমান [math]\tan^{-1}1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]।

খ ধরা আছে [math]\sin(\pi\csc\theta)=\cos\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}\csc\theta\bigr)[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin A=\sin\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-B\bigr)[/math] যেখানে [math]A=\pi\csc\theta,\;B=\tfrac{\pi}{2}\csc\theta[/math]; তাই
[math]\pi\csc\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\tfrac{\pi}{2}\csc\theta+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\pi\csc\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+\tfrac{\pi}{2}\csc\theta+2k\pi[/math] হওয়া প্রয়োজন। প্রথম থেকে [math]\csc\theta=\frac{4k+1}{3}[/math] এবং দ্বিতীয় থেকে [math]\csc\theta=1+4k[/math] পাওয়া যায়; কণিক সূচক পরিবর্তন করে এগুলোকে যথাক্রমে [math]\theta=\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{4n+1}[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\sin^{-1}\frac{1}{1-4n}[/math] লেখা যায় (n পূর্ণসংখ্যা)।

গ সাধারণভাবে [math]\cot^{-1}u-\cot^{-1}v=\cot^{-1}\frac{uv+1}{v-u}[/math] হলে [math]u=2n-1,\;v=2n+1[/math] বসালে [math]\frac{uv+1}{v-u}=\frac{4n^{2}}{2}=2n^{2}[/math], ফলে [math]\cot^{-1}(2n-1)-\cot^{-1}(2n+1)=\cot^{-1}(2n^{2})[/math]। n=1,2,3 নিয়ে যোগ করলে টেলিস্কোপিং হয়ে [math]\sum_{n=1}^{3}\cot^{-1}(2n^{2})=\cot^{-1}1-\cot^{-1}7=\cot^{-1}\frac{4}{3}[/math]।

Question #12 (ID: 442549)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{array}{l}P=\tan (\theta-\alpha) \tan (\theta-\beta) \\Q=2 \sqrt{3} \cos ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta-2 \sqrt{3}\end{array}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]-1-\sqrt{3} i[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]Q=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]P=\tan ^{2} \theta[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \sin \alpha \sin \beta}{\sin (\alpha+\beta)}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]-1-\sqrt{3} i[/math]
 এর আর্গুমেন্ট[math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{-\sqrt{3}}{-1}=-\pi+\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{3} \\=-\pi+\frac{\pi}{3}=-\frac{2 \pi}{3} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]Q = 0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore 2 \sqrt{3} \cos ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta-2 \sqrt{3}=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sqrt{3} \cos ^{2} \theta+4 \cos \theta-3 \cos \theta-2 \sqrt{3}=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta(\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+2)-\sqrt{3}(\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+2) \\\Rightarrow(\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+2)(2 \cos \theta-\sqrt{3})=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+2=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\cos \theta=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \alpha[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \alpha,[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\cos \alpha=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
আবার, [math]2 \cos \theta-\sqrt{3}=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\cos \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \alpha, \cos \alpha=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
অথবা [math]\theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]P=\tan ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan (\theta-\alpha) \tan (\theta-\beta)=\tan ^{2} \theta \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin (\theta-\alpha) \sin (\theta-\beta)}{\cos (\theta-\alpha) \cos (\theta-\beta)}=\tan ^{2} \theta \\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos (\theta-\alpha) \cos (\theta-\beta)}{\sin (\theta-\alpha) \sin (\theta-\beta)}=\frac{1}{\tan ^{2} \theta}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\cos (\theta-\alpha) \cos (\theta-\beta)+\sin (\theta-\alpha) \sin (\theta-\beta)}{\cos (\theta-\alpha) \cos (\theta-\beta)-\sin (\theta-\alpha) \sin (\theta-\beta)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} .[/math]
[যোজন-বিয়োজন করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\cos (\theta-\alpha-\theta+\beta)}{\cos (\theta-\alpha+\theta-\beta)}=\frac{1}{\cos 2 \theta} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos (\alpha-\beta)}{\cos \{2 \theta-(\alpha+\beta)\}}=\frac{1}{\cos 2 \theta}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta \cos (\alpha+\beta)+\sin 2 \theta \sin (\alpha+\beta)[/math]
 [math]=\cos 2 \theta \cos (\alpha-\beta)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta \sin (\alpha+\beta)=\cos 2 \theta\{\cos (\alpha-\beta)-\cos (\alpha+\beta)\} \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta \sin (\alpha+\beta)=\cos 2 \theta \cdot 2 \sin \alpha \sin \beta \\\Rightarrow \tan 2 \theta=\frac{2 \sin \alpha \sin \beta}{\sin (\alpha+\beta)} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\tan ^{-1}-\frac{2 \sin \alpha \sin \beta}{\sin (\alpha+\beta)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \sin \alpha \sin \beta}{\sin (\alpha+\beta)}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]-1-\sqrt{3}\,i[/math] বিন্দুটি তৃতীয় কোয়াড্রান্টে (real খ [math]Q=2\sqrt{3}\cos^2\theta+\cos\theta-2\sqrt{3}=0[/math] হলে [math]x=\cos\theta[/math] ধরে [math]2\sqrt{3}x^2+x-2\sqrt{3}=0[/math]; ডিসক্রিমিন্যান্ট [math]\Delta=49[/math] হওয়ায় [math]x=\sqrt{3}/2[/math] বা [math]x=-2/\sqrt{3}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, কিন্তু দ্বিতীয়টি অবৈধ (|x|>1), সুতরাং [math]\cos\theta=\sqrt{3}/2\Rightarrow \theta=2k\pi\pm\frac{\pi}{6},\;k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

গ ধরুন [math]t=\tan\theta,\;a=\tan\alpha,\;b=\tan\beta[/math]; [math]\tan(\theta-\alpha)\tan(\theta-\beta)=\tan^2\theta[/math] থেকে সরলীকরণ করলে (গুণন এবং গুণাঙ্ক নবায়ন করে) পাওয়া যায় [math]\sin(\alpha+\beta)\,t=\sin\alpha\sin\beta\,(1-t^2)[/math], ফলে [math]\tan2\theta=\dfrac{2t}{1-t^2}=\dfrac{2\sin\alpha\sin\beta}{\sin(\alpha+\beta)}[/math] এবং তাই [math]\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\tan^{-1}\!\dfrac{2\sin\alpha\sin\beta}{\sin(\alpha+\beta)}[/math]।

Question #13 (ID: 442550)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=6 x^{2}-x-1[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]f(x)=2[/math] সমীকরণের মূলের প্রকৃতি নির্ণয় কর ।
খ. [math]\mathrm{f}(\sin \theta)=0[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\frac{1}{\mathrm{f}(\mathbf{x})}[/math] এর বিস্তৃতিতে [math]x^{r}[/math] এর সহগ নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে,[math]f(x)=2 \Rightarrow 6 x^{2}-x-1=2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 6 x^{2}-x-3=0[/math]
সমীকরণটির পৃথায়ক [math]=(-1)^{2}-4.6(-3)=1+72=73[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 সমীকরণটির মূল দুইটি অমূলদ ও অসমান।[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{f}(\sin \theta)=0 \\\Rightarrow 6 \sin ^{2} \theta-\sin \theta-1=0 \\\Rightarrow 6 \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \sin \theta+2 \sin \theta-1=0 \\\Rightarrow 3 \sin \theta(2 \sin \theta-1)+1(2 \sin \theta-1)=0 \\\Rightarrow(2 \sin \theta-1)(3 \sin \theta+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]2 \sin \theta-1=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6} \text {, }[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]3 \sin \theta+1=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\sin \theta=-\frac{1}{3}=\sin \alpha[/math]
 (ধরি)[math]\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \alpha, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\alpha=\sin ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6} \text {, }[/math]
 [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \alpha[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\sin \alpha=-\frac{1}{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=6 x^{2}-x-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{1}{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})}=\frac{1}{6 \mathrm{x}^{2}-\mathrm{x}-1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{6 x^{2}-3 x+2 x-1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{(3 x+1)(2 x-1)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{(3 x+1)\left(2 \cdot \frac{-1}{3}-1\right)}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{1}{(2 x-1)\left(3 \frac{1}{2}+1\right)}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{-3}{5(3 x+1)}+\frac{2}{5(2 x-1)} \\=\frac{1}{5}\left\{2(2 x-1)^{-1}-3(3 x+1)^{-1}\right\} \\=\frac{1}{5}\left\{-2(1-2 x)^{-1}-3(1+3 x)^{-1}\right\} \\=\frac{1}{5}\left\{-2\left(1+2 x+2^{2} x^{2}+\ldots+2^{r} x^{r}+\ldots\right)\right.\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\left.-3\left(1-3 x+3^{2} x^{2}-\ldots+(-1)^{r} 3^{r} x^{r}+\ldots\right)\right\}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}^{\mathrm{r}}[/math]
 এর সহগ [math]=\frac{1}{5}\left\{-2 \cdot 2^{\mathrm{r}}-3 \cdot(-1)^{\mathrm{r}} 3^{\mathrm{r}}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{1}{5}\left\{-2^{\mathrm{r}+1}-(-1)^{\mathrm{r}} 3^{\mathrm{r}+1}\right\} \\=\frac{1}{5}\left\{(-1)^{\mathrm{r}+1}, 3^{\mathrm{r}+1}-2^{\mathrm{r}+1}\right\} \\=\frac{1}{5}\left\{(-3)^{\mathrm{r}+1}-2^{\mathrm{r}+1}\right\} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]f(x)=2 \Rightarrow 6x^{2}-x-3=0[/math]; ডিস্ক্রিমিনেন্ট [math]D=(-1)^{2}-4\cdot6\cdot(-3)=73>0[/math], তাই দুটি বিশিষ্ট বাস্তব মূল আছে এবং [math]D[/math] বর্গপূর্ণ না হওয়ায় মূলগুলো Irrational।

খ [math]f(\sin\theta)=0\Rightarrow 6\sin^{2}\theta-\sin\theta-1=0[/math], [math]\sin\theta=y[/math] ধরে সমাধান থেকে [math]y=\tfrac{1}{2},\ -\tfrac{1}{3}[/math]; তাই [math]\sin\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2k\pi,\ \tfrac{5\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math] এবং [math]\sin\theta=-\tfrac{1}{3}\Rightarrow \theta=-\arcsin\tfrac{1}{3}+2k\pi,\ \theta=\pi+\arcsin\tfrac{1}{3}+2k\pi[/math] (অখণ্ড সংখ্যার জন্য [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math])।

গ কারকরণ দিয়ে [math]6x^{2}-x-1=(3x+1)(2x-1)[/math] এবং পার্শ্বভঙ্গি করলে [math]\frac{1}{f(x)}=\frac{-3/5}{1+3x}+\frac{-2/5}{1-2x}[/math]; জ্যামিতিক ধারা ছড়িয়ে coef. নির্ণয়ে [math]x^{r}[/math] এর সহগ হচ্ছে [math]a_{r}=\frac{(-1)^{r+1}3^{\,r+1}-2^{\,r+1}}{5}\quad (r\ge0)[/math]।

Question #14 (ID: 442551)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}\right)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] হলে x এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]\mathrm{f}(\sin \theta)=0[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]f(x)=0[/math] সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha[/math] ও [math]\beta[/math] হলে [math]\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}[/math] ও [math]\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}[/math] মূলবিশিষ্ট সমীকরণ নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}}{1-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3} \cdot \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{11}{3 \sqrt{2}}}{1-1}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]= \frac{\pi}{2}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 বা, [math]\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=x \therefore x=1 \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(\sin \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0\\= \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta-\sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0\\= \sqrt{2} \sin \theta(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})-1(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})=0\\= (\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
কিন্তু [math]\sin \theta-\sqrt{2} 
eq 0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1=0 \\= \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \\\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{4} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{4}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}[/math]
শর্তমতে, [math]f(x) = 0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}=0 \ldots \ldots \text { (i) }[/math]
(i) নং সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha[/math]
 ও [math]\beta[/math]
 হলে পাই,[math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 এবং [math]\alpha \beta=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{2}}=1[/math]
এখন, [math]\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}=(\alpha+\beta)^{2}-2 \alpha \beta[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}-2.1=\frac{9}{2}-2=\frac{5}{2}[/math]
এবং [math]\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}=(\alpha+\beta)^{3}-3 \alpha \beta(\alpha+\beta)[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{3}-3.1 \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{27}{2 \sqrt{2}}-\frac{9}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{9}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমীকরণ, [math]x^{2}-\left(\frac{5}{2}+\frac{9}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right) x+\frac{5}{2} \cdot \frac{9}{2 \sqrt{2}}=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore 4 \sqrt{2} x^{2}-(10 \sqrt{2}+18) x+45=0 \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\tan^{-1}a+\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] সূত্রে [math]a=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3},\;b=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] হলে [math]ab=1[/math] তাই যোগফল [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]; অতএব [math]\sin^{-1}x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] থেকে [math]x=1[/math]।

খ [math]f(x)=0[/math] থেকে মূলদ্বয় = [math]\frac{3\pm1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sqrt{2}[/math] ও [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]; কিন্তু [math]\sin\theta[/math] এর মান ≤1 হওয়ায় গ্রহণযোগ্য কেবল [math]\sin\theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math], তাই [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]\theta=\frac{3\pi}{4}+2k\pi,\;k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

গ [math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}},\;\alpha\beta=1[/math]; তাই [math]\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}=(\alpha+\beta)^{2}-2\alpha\beta=\frac{5}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}=(\alpha+\beta)^{3}-3\alpha\beta(\alpha+\beta)=\frac{9}{2\sqrt{2}}=\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{4}[/math]; সংশ্লিষ্ট(simple) সমীকরণগুলো হল [math]x-\frac{5}{2}=0[/math] ও [math]x-\frac{9}{2\sqrt{2}}=0[/math]।

Question #15 (ID: 442546)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\mathrm{P}=2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\tan \frac{\alpha}{2} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\}[/math] এবং


[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]x=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan x=\frac{1}{3}[/math] অথবা [math]\tan x=3[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{A B}{A C}+\frac{A C}{A B}=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]P=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\sin \beta+\cos \alpha}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]x=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \Rightarrow 2 x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin 2 x=\frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan x}{1+\tan ^{2} x}=\frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} x+3=10 \tan x \\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} x-10 \tan x+3=0 \\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} x-9 \tan x-\tan x+3=0 \\\Rightarrow 3 \tan x(\tan x-3)-1(\tan x-3)=0 \\\Rightarrow(\tan x-3)(3 \tan x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\tan x=3[/math]
 অথবা, [math]\tan x=\frac{1}{3}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খচিত্র হতে, [math]\frac{\mathrm{AB}}{\mathrm{AC}}=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\operatorname{cosec} \theta \\\therefore \frac{\mathrm{AB}}{\mathrm{AC}}+\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
হতে পাই, [math]\sin \theta+\operatorname{cosec} \theta=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta+\frac{1}{\sin \theta}=\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta+\sqrt{2}=3 \sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta-\sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin \theta(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})-1(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})=0 \\\Rightarrow(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \theta-\sqrt{2}=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
কিন্তু [math]\sqrt{2}>1[/math]
 বলে [math]\sin \theta 
eq \sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin ^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{4}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=0+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\pi+(-1) \frac{\pi}{4}=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}>\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
এখানে [math]xগ [math]\begin{array}{l}\text { L.H.S. }=\mathbf{P}\\=2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\tan \frac{\alpha}{2} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}}{1-\frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}[/math]
এখন [math]2 \sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \sin 2\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\beta\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \cos \beta[/math]
এবং [math]\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\left\{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)+\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\} \\\left\{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\} \\\cos \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \cdot \cos \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}-\frac{\beta}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\=\cos \left\{\frac{\alpha}{2}+\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\} \cdot \cos \left\{\frac{\alpha}{2}-\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\} \\=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\cos 2 \cdot \frac{\alpha}{2}+\cos 2 \cdot\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\} \\=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\cos \alpha+\cos \left(\beta-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)\right\} \\=\frac{1}{2}\{\cos \alpha+\sin \beta\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { L.H.S }=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\frac{1}{2}(\sin \beta+\cos \alpha)}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\sin \beta+\cos \alpha}[/math]
 [math]=\text { R.H.S. }[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]x=\frac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{5}[/math] হলে [math]\sin 2x=\frac{3}{5}[/math]; সঙ্গে [math]\sin 2x=\dfrac{2\tan x}{1+\tan^2 x}[/math] বসালে [math]\dfrac{2t}{1+t^2}=\dfrac{3}{5}[/math] যেখানে [math]t=\tan x[/math]; এটি সরল করলে [math]3t^2-10t+3=0[/math] হয় এবং সমাধান দিয়ে [math]t=3[/math] অথবা [math]t=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] পাওয়া যায়।

খ ধরা যাক [math]k=\dfrac{AB}{AC}[/math], তখন [math]k+\dfrac{1}{k}=\dfrac{3}{\sqrt2}[/math] থেকে [math]k^2-\dfrac{3}{\sqrt2}k+1=0[/math] হয়; সমাধানে [math]k=\sqrt2[/math] অথবা [math]k=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt2}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, সুতরাং যদি [math]k=\tan\theta[/math] ধরা হয় তবে [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}(\sqrt2)[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{1}{\sqrt2}\big)[/math]।

গ নিন [math]u=\tan\frac{\alpha}{2}\tan\!\big(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\big)[/math]; তাহলে [math]P=2\arctan u[/math] এবং [math]\tan P=\dfrac{2u}{1-u^2}[/math] ব্যবহার করে অর্ধকোণ সূত্রসমূহ [math]\tan\frac{\alpha}{2}=\dfrac{\sin\alpha}{1+\cos\alpha}[/math] এবং [math]\tan\frac{\beta}{2}=\dfrac{\sin\beta}{1+\cos\beta}[/math] বসিয়ে সরলীকরণ করলে সরাসরি পাওয়া যায় [math]\tan P=\dfrac{\sin\alpha\cos\beta}{\sin\beta+\cos\alpha}[/math], তাই [math]P=\tan^{-1}\dfrac{\sin\alpha\cos\beta}{\sin\beta+\cos\alpha}[/math]।

Question #16 (ID: 442545)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]\cot ^{2} \theta+\operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta=3[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{BD}}{\mathrm{AB}}\right) +\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{BD}}{\mathrm{AB}}\right)=\frac{2 \mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}[/math]
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{A C}{B C}\right)=\sqrt{1+\sin ^{2} \alpha}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{2} \theta+\operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta=3\\= \cot ^{2} \theta+1+\cot ^{2} \theta=3\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= 2 \cot ^{2} \theta=2[/math]
 বা, [math]\cot ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 বা, [math]\tan ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 [math]= \tan \theta= \pm 1[/math]
 বা, [math]\tan \theta=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
. নির্ণেয় সমাধানঃ [math]\theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যখন n এর মান শূন্যঅথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]\\[/math]
 খ
[Image]
চিত্রানুসারে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\mathrm{BD}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\cos \alpha \\\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}=\sec \alpha\end{array}[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{BD}}{\mathrm{AB}}\right)[/math]
 [math]+\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{BD}}{\mathrm{AB}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \cos \alpha\right)+\tan[/math]
 [math]\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \cos \alpha\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)+\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\tan \frac{\pi}{4}+\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}{1-\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}+\frac{\tan \frac{\pi}{4}-\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}{1+\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1+\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}{1-\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}+\frac{1-\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}{1+\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\left(1+\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}\right)^{2}+\left(1-\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1-\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}\right)\left(1+\tan \frac{\alpha}{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2\left(1+\tan ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}\right)}{1-\tan ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}=\frac{2 \sec ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}{1-\frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}} \times \frac{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2}{\cos \alpha}=2 \sec \alpha=2 \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গএখন, [math]\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{A C}{B C}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \operatorname{cosec}\left(90^{\circ}-\alpha\right) \\=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \sec (\alpha) \\=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan (\alpha)\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{A D}{B D}\right) \\=\sin \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{A B}{B D}\right) \\=\frac{A B}{B D} \\=\sec \alpha \\=\sqrt{\sec ^{2} \alpha}=\sqrt{1+\tan ^{2} \alpha}\end{array}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\mathrm{P}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{AD}}{\mathrm{BD}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos P=\frac{A D}{B D}[/math]
 [math]\left.\therefore \sin P=\frac{A B}{B D} \Rightarrow P=\sin ^{-1} \frac{A B}{B D}\right][/math]

[Image]
তাহলে, [math]\sqrt{1+\tan ^{2} \alpha}=\sqrt{1+\sin ^{2} \alpha}[/math]
 হবেযদি [math]\alpha=0[/math]
 অথবা [math]\pi[/math]
 এর যেকোন গুণিতক হয়,অর্থাৎ [math]\sin \alpha=\tan \alpha[/math]
 হয়।অতএব, [math]\alpha[/math]
 এর মান 0 (শূন্য) অথবা [math]\pi[/math]
 এর যেকোন গুণিতকের জন্য[math]\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{A C}{B C}\right)=\sqrt{1+\sin ^{2} \alpha}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:

Question #17 (ID: 442544)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(1-a+a^{2}\right)=\tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1}(a-1)[/math]
খ. [math]f(x)=0[/math] সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha, \beta[/math] হলে [math]\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}[/math] ও [math]\alpha^{-2} \beta^{-2}[/math] মূলবিশিষ্ট সমীকরণ নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}-\frac{\mathrm{AD}}{\mathrm{DE}}=\frac{4}{3}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1}(a-1)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{a-a+1}{1+a(a-1)}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{1-a+a^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1}\left(1-a+a^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot ^{-1}\left(1-a+a^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1}(a-1)[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]f(x)=0 \Rightarrow 5 x^{2}-2 x+3=0[/math]
সমীকরণের মূলদ্বয় [math]\alpha, \beta[/math]
 হলে,[math]\alpha+\beta=-\frac{-2}{5}=\frac{2}{5}, \alpha \beta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]
এখন নির্ণেয় সমীকরণের মূল দুইটির সমষ্টি।[math]\begin{array}{l}=\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}+\alpha^{-2} \beta^{-2} \\=(\alpha+\beta)^{3}-3 \alpha \beta(\alpha+\beta)^{3}+\frac{1}{(\alpha \beta)^{2}} \\=\left(\frac{2}{5}\right)^{3}-3 \times \frac{3}{5} \times \frac{2}{5}+\frac{1}{\left(\frac{3}{5}\right)^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{8}{125}-\frac{18}{25}+\frac{25}{9}=\frac{72-810+3125}{125 \times 9}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2387}{1125}[/math]
এবং গুণফল [math]=\left(\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}\right) \alpha^{-2} \beta^{-2}[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{8}{125}-\frac{18}{25}\right) \frac{25}{9}=\frac{8-90}{125} \times \frac{25}{9}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-82}{5} \times \frac{1}{9}=\frac{-82}{45}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমীকরণ, [math]x^{2}-\frac{2387}{1125} x+\left(\frac{-82}{45}\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore 1125 \mathrm{x}^{2}-2387 \mathrm{x}-2050=0 \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}-\frac{\mathrm{AD}}{\mathrm{DE}}=\frac{4}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sec \theta-\operatorname{cosec} \theta=\frac{4}{3}[/math]
 [চিত্র হতে][math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos \theta}-\frac{1}{\sin \theta}=\frac{4}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta-\cos \theta}{\sin \theta \cos \theta}=\frac{4}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 3(\sin \theta-\cos \theta)=2 \sin 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow 9\left(\sin ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta\right)=4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 9(1-\sin 2 \theta)=4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta+9 \sin 2 \theta-9=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta+12 \sin 2 \theta-3 \sin 2 \theta-9=0 \\\Rightarrow 4 \sin 2 \theta(\sin 2 \theta+3)-3(\sin 2 \theta+3)=0 \\\Rightarrow(\sin 2 \theta+3)(4 \sin 2 \theta-3)=0\end{array}[/math]
এখানে, [math]\sin 2 \theta 
eq-3[\because-1 \leq \sin \theta \leq 1][/math]
 [math]\therefore 4 \sin 2 \theta-3=0 \Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক তফাৎ সূত্র [math]\tan^{-1}x-\tan^{-1}y=\tan^{-1}\frac{x-y}{1+xy}[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে RHS হয় [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{a-(a-1)}{1+a(a-1)}=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{1-a+a^{2}}[/math]; আর [math]\cot^{-1}t=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{t}[/math] হলে উভয় পাক্ষিক সমান হয়।

খ ধরা হোক [math]s_{1}=\alpha+\beta,\;s_{2}=\alpha\beta[/math]. তাহলে [math]\alpha^{3}+\beta^{3}=s_{1}^{3}-3s_{1}s_{2}[/math] এবং [math]\alpha^{-2}\beta^{-2}=1/s_{2}^{2}[/math]. দুইটি মূল হলে সমীকরণ হবে [math]x^{2}-(r_{1}+r_{2})x+r_{1}r_{2}=0[/math] যেখানে [math]r_{1}=s_{1}^{3}-3s_{1}s_{2},\;r_{2}=1/s_{2}^{2}[/math], ফলে [math]x^{2}-\Big(s_{1}^{3}-3s_{1}s_{2}+\dfrac{1}{s_{2}^{2}}\Big)x+\dfrac{s_{1}^{3}-3s_{1}s_{2}}{s_{2}^{2}}=0[/math]।

গ প্রদত্ত অনুপাতে সম্বন্ধীয় ত্রিকোণমিতিক সম্পর্ক প্রয়োগ করলে সহজভাবে পাওয়া যায় [math]\sin 2\theta=\dfrac{3}{4}[/math], সুতরাং [math]\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\dfrac{3}{4}[/math]।
