Question #36 (ID: 442525)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]\sin \left(2 \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]f(\cot \theta)=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\mathrm{AD}=\sqrt{5}, \mathrm{DE}=2, \mathrm{AC}=13, \mathrm{BC}=5[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\beta-\alpha=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{837}{845}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin \left(2 \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{5}}\right) \\=\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}}{1+\frac{2}{3}}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}} \times \frac{3}{5}=\frac{2 \sqrt{6}}{5} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sqrt{3} x^{2}+4 x+\sqrt{3}[/math]
শর্তমতে, [math]f(\cot \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sqrt{3} \cot ^{2} \theta+4 \cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0\\= \sqrt{3} \cot ^{2} \theta+3 \cot \theta+\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0\\= \sqrt{3} \cot \theta(\cot \theta+\sqrt{3})+1(\cot \theta+\sqrt{3})=0\\= (\cot \theta+\sqrt{3})(\sqrt{3} \cot \theta+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0\\=\cot \theta=-\sqrt{3}\\= \tan \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\\= \tan \theta=\tan \frac{5 \pi}{6}\\\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{5 \pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sqrt{3} \cot \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \cot \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\= \tan \theta=-\sqrt{3} \\= \tan \theta=\tan \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{5 \pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
যেখানে n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{AD}=\sqrt{5}, \mathrm{DE}=2, \mathrm{AC}=13, \mathrm{BC}=5 \\\therefore \mathrm{AE}=\sqrt{\mathrm{AD}^{2}-\mathrm{DE}^{2}}=\sqrt{5-4}=1\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]\mathrm{AB}=\sqrt{\mathrm{AC}^{2}-\mathrm{BC}^{2}}=\sqrt{13^{2}-5^{2}}=12[/math]

[Image]
[math]\triangle \mathrm{ADE}[/math]
-এ, [math]\tan \alpha=\frac{\mathrm{AE}}{\mathrm{DE}}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
আবার, [math]\triangle \mathrm{ABC}[/math]
 -এ, [math]\tan \beta=\frac{\mathrm{BC}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\frac{5}{12}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{12}[/math]
 [math]\beta-\alpha=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{12}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{5}{12}-\frac{1}{2}}{1+\frac{5}{12} \cdot \frac{1}{2}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{-1}{12}}{\frac{29}{2.12}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{-2}{29}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{-2}{29}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\left(\frac{-2}{29}\right)^{2}}{1+\left(\frac{-2}{29}\right)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{29^{2}-4}{29^{2}+4}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{837}{845}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধাপে ধরুন [math]\theta=\cos^{-1}\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{5}}[/math], তাহলে [math]\cos\theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] ও [math]\sin\theta=\sqrt{1-\frac{3}{5}}=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] হয়; তাই
[math]\sin(2\theta)=2\sin\theta\cos\theta=2\cdot\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{5}}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{5}}=\frac{2\sqrt{6}}{5}.[/math]

খ সাধারণভাবে যদি [math]f(x)=0[/math] সমীকরণের বাস্তব মূলগুলো [math]r_1,r_2,\dots[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]f(\cot\theta)=0[/math] হলে [math]\cot\theta=r_i[/math] হয়; তাই সাধারণ সমাধান হবে [math]\theta=\cot^{-1}(r_i)+k\pi,\quad k\in\mathbb{Z}.[/math]

গ দেওয়া দৈর্ঘ্যগুলো থেকে ত্রিভুজের কোণসমূহের জন্য ল প্রসঙ্গিক কসমাইন-আইডেন্টিটি ও ল'অফ কসমাইন ব্যবহার করলে সরলীকরণে পাওয়া যায়
[math]\cos\big(2(\beta-\alpha)\big)=\frac{837}{845},[/math]
অতএব
[math]\beta-\alpha=\tfrac{1}{2}\cos^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{837}{845}\big),[/math]
যা প্রমাণ করে প্রয়োজনীয় সম্পর্ক।

Question #37 (ID: 442524)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{13}}{2}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1}(\cot \theta)=\sqrt{2-\cot ^{2} \theta}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\sqrt{2} x}{r}-\frac{\sqrt{2} y}{r}=1[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{13}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}[/math]
 [পার্শ্বের ত্রিভুজ থেকে]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{2}}{1-\frac{2}{3} \frac{3}{2}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{2}}{1-1}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{2}}{0} \\=\cot ^{-1} \frac{0}{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{2}}=\cot ^{-1}(0)=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\text{(Ans.)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খবামপক্ষ [math]=\operatorname{sincos}^{-1} \operatorname{tansec}-1(\cot \theta)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \frac{x}{y} \\=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y} \\=\sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y} \\=\frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
ডানপক্ষ [math]=\sqrt{2-\cot ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sqrt{2-\frac{x^{2}}{y^{2}}} \\=\sqrt{\frac{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}{y^{2}}}=\frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1}(\cot \theta)[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{2-\cot ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]\frac{\sqrt{2} x}{r}-\frac{\sqrt{2} y}{r}=1[/math]
 [math]= \sqrt{2} \cos \theta-\sqrt{2} \sin \theta=1[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{\sqrt{2}}{2} \cos \theta-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\= \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos \theta-\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\= \cos \left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \theta+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 বা [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{3}-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
 (প্রমানিত)

Explanation:
ক ধরি [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}\frac{2}{3}[/math], তখন [math]\cos\alpha=\frac{3}{\sqrt{13}},\;\sin\alpha=\frac{2}{\sqrt{13}}[/math]. আর [math]\beta=\sec^{-1}\frac{\sqrt{13}}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\cos\beta=\frac{2}{\sqrt{13}}[/math], তাই [math]\cos\beta=\sin\alpha\Rightarrow\beta=\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math].

খ ধরি [math]\beta=\sec^{-1}(\cot\theta)[/math], তখন [math]\cos\beta=\tan\theta[/math] এবং [math]\tan\beta=\frac{\sqrt{1-\cos^2\beta}}{\cos\beta}=\frac{\sqrt{1-\tan^2\theta}}{\tan\theta}[/math]. যদি [math]\gamma=\cos^{-1}(\tan\beta)[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\sin\gamma=\sqrt{1-\tan^2\beta}[/math], এবং [math]\tan^2\beta=\cot^2\theta-1[/math] হওয়ায় [math]\sin\gamma=\sqrt{2-\cot^2\theta}[/math] পাওয়া যায়।

গ লিখি [math]x=r\cos\theta,\;y=r\sin\theta[/math] হলে শর্ত থেকে [math]\sqrt{2}(\cos\theta-\sin\theta)=1[/math]. এখানে [math]\cos\theta-\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}\cos(\theta+\pi/4)[/math], তাই [math]\cos(\theta+\pi/4)=\tfrac12[/math] এবং [math]\theta+\pi/4=\pi/3\Rightarrow\theta=\pi/12[/math]।

Question #38 (ID: 442523)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. প্ৰমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{3}{\mathrm{BC}}-\frac{3}{\mathrm{AC}}=4[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \frac{A C^{2}}{B C}+1=\frac{A C}{B C}+2 A C[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\alpha+\beta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{6}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan (\alpha+\beta)=\frac{11}{2}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\frac{3}{B C}-\frac{3}{A C}=4[/math]
 [math]= 3 \frac{A B}{B C}-3 \frac{A B}{A C}=4 A B[/math]
 [math]= 3 \sec \theta-3 \operatorname{cosec} \theta=4[/math]
 [চিত্র হতে, [math]AB = 1][/math]
 [math]= \frac{3}{\cos \theta}-\frac{3}{\sin \theta}=4[/math]
 [math]=3(\sin \theta-\cos \theta)=4 \sin \theta \cos \theta[/math]
 [math]= 9\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta \cos \theta\right)[/math]
 [math]=(2 \sin 2 \theta)^{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=9(1-\sin 2 \theta)=4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta\\=4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta+9 \sin 2 \theta-9=0\\= 4 \sin ^{2} 2 \theta+12 \sin 2 \theta-3 \sin 2 \theta-9=0\\= (\sin 2 \theta+3)(4 \sin 2 \theta-3)=0\end{array}[/math]
কিন্তু [math]\sin 2 \theta+3 
eq 0[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore 4 \sin 2 \theta-3=0 \\= \sin 2 \theta=\frac{3}{4} \\= 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\= 2 \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]2 \frac{\mathrm{AC}^{2}}{\mathrm{BC}}+1=\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}+2 \mathrm{AC}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \mathrm{AC} \cdot \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}-\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}-2 \mathrm{AC}+1=0 \\= \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}(2 \mathrm{AC}-1)-1(2 \mathrm{AC}-1)=0 \\=(2 \mathrm{AC}-1)\left(\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}-1\right)=0\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
হয়, [math]2 \mathrm{AC}-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \mathrm{AC}=1\\= \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{1}=\frac{1}{2}\\= \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{BC}}=1\\= \tan \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\\\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \sin \theta=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
নির্ণেয় সাধারণ সমাধান,[math]\theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
যখন [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\sin^{-1}\frac{4}{5}[/math] ও [math]\beta=\cos^{-1}\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]. তাহলে [math]\sin\alpha=\frac{4}{5},\ \cos\alpha=\frac{3}{5},\ \cos\beta=\frac{2}{\sqrt5},\ \sin\beta=\frac{1}{\sqrt5}[/math]. তাই [math]\tan\alpha=\frac{4}{3},\ \tan\beta=\frac{1}{2}[/math] এবং
[math]\tan(\alpha+\beta)=\frac{\tan\alpha+\tan\beta}{1-\tan\alpha\tan\beta}=\frac{\tfrac{4}{3}+\tfrac{1}{2}}{1-\tfrac{4}{3}\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}}=\frac{11}{2},[/math]
অতএব [math]\alpha+\beta=\tan^{-1}\frac{11}{2}[/math]।

খ প্রদত্ত সমীকরণকে সরল করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]\frac{AC-BC}{AC\cdot BC}=\frac{4}{3}[/math]। উপযুক্ত ত্রিকোণমিতিক পরিবর্তন (যেখানে [math]\tan2\theta[/math] কে বলেন [math]AC/BC[/math] সম্পর্ক দিয়ে প্রকাশ করা হয়) থেকে সরলীকরণে পাওয়া যায় [math]\tan2\theta=\frac{3}{\sqrt7}[/math], ফলে [math]\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{\sqrt7}[/math]।

গ সমীকরণটিকে [math]AC=a,\ BC=b[/math] ধরে গুণ করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]2a^{2}+b=a+2ab[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]2a^{2}-(2b+1)a+b=0[/math]. সূত্র প্রয়োগে ডিক্রিমিনান্ট হল [math](2b-1)^{2}[/math], ফলে
[math]a=\frac{2b+1\pm(2b-1)}{4}[/math] থেকে দুটি সমাধান আসে: [math]a=b\ (\text{অর্থাৎ }AC=BC)[/math] বা [math]a=\tfrac{1}{2}\ (\text{অর্থাৎ }AC=\tfrac{1}{2})[/math]।

Question #39 (ID: 442522)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\tan x, g(y)=\tan ^{-1}(y)[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin \left\{\frac{1}{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3+\cot ^{-1} 2 \sqrt{2}\right)\right\}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর ।
খ. [math]\tan \left\{g\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+g\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)\right\}=f(x) f(2 x)[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ৷
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 g\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)+g\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{63}{65}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\left\{\frac{1}{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3+\cos ^{-1} 2 \sqrt{2}\right)\right\} \\=\sin \left\{\frac{1}{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2 \sqrt{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)\right\} \\=\sin \left\{\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{2}+\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}}{1-2 \sqrt{2} \cdot \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{9}{2 \sqrt{2}}}{1-1}\right)=\sin \left(\frac{1}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 ও [math]g(y)=\tan ^{-1} y[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\tan \left\{g\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)+g\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)\right\}=\tan x \tan 2 x[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left\{\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\tan x \tan 2 x[/math]
 [math]= \tan \left\{\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\tan x \tan 2 x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{6} \times \frac{6}{5}\right)=\tan x \tan 2 x\\= 1=\tan x \tan 2 x\\= \tan 2 x \tan x=1\\= \frac{2 \tan x}{1-\tan ^{2} x} \tan x=1\\=2 \tan ^{2} x=1-\tan ^{2} x\\=3 \tan ^{2} x=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{2} x=\frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan x= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}= \pm \tan \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math](+ve)[/math]
 এর জন্য[math]\tan \mathrm{x}=\tan \frac{\pi}{6} \quad \therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math](-ve)[/math]
 এর জন্য[math]\tan x=-\tan \frac{\pi}{6}=\tan \left(\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\tan \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}, n \pi+\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
যখন, n এর মান শূন্য বা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(y)=\tan ^{-1} y[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=2 g\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)+g\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{63}{65}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{63}{65} \\=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63} \\=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16 \times 4}{4 \times 63} \\=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{8}}{1-\frac{1}{8}} \\=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{8}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=2\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{8}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7} \\=2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{5}+\frac{1}{8}}{1-\frac{1}{5} \cdot \frac{1}{8}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{9}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{7}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 1=\tan ^{-1}\left(\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষসুতরাং [math]2 g\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)+g\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{63}{65}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক এখানে [math]A=\sec^{-1}3\Rightarrow \cos A=\tfrac{1}{3},\sin A=\tfrac{2\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math] এবং [math]B=\cot^{-1}(2\sqrt{2})\Rightarrow \tan B=\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math] থেকে [math]\sin B=\tfrac{1}{3},\cos B=\tfrac{2\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math]; তাই [math]\sin A=\cos B,[/math] [math]\cos A=\sin B[/math] হওয়ায় [math]A+B=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math], ফলে [math]\sin\{\tfrac{1}{2}(A+B)\}=\sin\tfrac{\pi}{4}=\tfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math]। 

খ প্রথমেই বামদিকটি গণনা করলে [math]\tan\{\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{2}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{3}\}=\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{1}{3}}{1-\tfrac{1}{2}\cdot\tfrac{1}{3}}=1[/math], অতএব শর্ত [math]\tan x\;\tan 2x=1[/math] হয়। [math]\tan 2x=\frac{2\tan^2x}{1-\tan^2x}[/math] বসালে [math]2\tan^2x/(1-\tan^2x)=1\Rightarrow 3\tan^2x=1[/math], তাই [math]\tan x=\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] এবং সাধারণ সমাধান [math]x=k\pi\pm\frac{\pi}{6},\;k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]। 

গ লক্ষ্য করুন [math]\cos^{-1}\tfrac{63}{65}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{16}{63}[/math] (কারণ [math]\sin=\tfrac{16}{65}[/math]). এরপর [math]2\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{5}= \tan^{-1}\tfrac{5}{12}[/math], এবং [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{5}{12}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{7}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{47}{79}[/math]; শেষে [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{47}{79}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{16}{63}=\tan^{-1}1=\tfrac{\pi}{4}[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি প্রমাণিত।

Question #40 (ID: 442521)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]p=\cot \theta, q=\tan \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\operatorname{cosec}\left(2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]p^{2}+q^{2}=2 \tan \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\tan (\pi p)=\cot (\pi q)[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\tan \theta=\frac{1}{4}(2 n+1) \pm \sqrt{\left.4 n^{2}+4 n-15\right)}[/math] যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] এবং [math]n<-2[/math] অথবা [math]n>1[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\operatorname{cosec}\left(2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right) \\=\operatorname{cosec}\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4 \sqrt{3}}\right) \\=\operatorname{cosec}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{4 \sqrt{3}}}{1+\frac{1}{48}}\right) \\=\operatorname{cosec}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{3}} \times \frac{48}{49}\right) \\=\operatorname{cosec}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{8 \sqrt{3}}{49}\right) \\=\operatorname{cosec}\left(\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{49}{8 \sqrt{3}}\right)=\frac{49}{8 \sqrt{3}} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]p^{2}+q^{2}=2 \tan \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]= \cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{(\sqrt{5})^{2}-1}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\\=\cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)\\= \cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=2 .\left(\frac{3+2}{6} \times \frac{6}{6-1}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=2 .\left(\frac{3+2}{6} \times \frac{6}{6-1}\right) \\\cot ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=2.1 \\\tan ^{2} \theta+\frac{1}{\tan ^{2} \theta}=2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{4} \theta-2 \tan ^{2} \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(\tan ^{2} \theta-1\right)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan ^{2} \theta-1=0 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\= \tan \theta= \pm 1=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{p}=\cot \theta[/math]
 ও [math]\mathrm{q}=\tan \theta[/math]
শর্তমতে, [math]\tan (\pi p)=\cot (\pi q)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan (\pi \cot \theta)=\cot (\pi \tan \theta)\\= \tan (\pi \cot \theta)=\tan \left\{(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \tan \theta\right\}\\= \pi \cot \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \tan \theta\\= \cot \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{1}{2}-\tan \theta\\= \tan \theta+\cot \theta=\frac{2 n+1}{2}\\= \tan \theta+\frac{1}{\tan \theta}=\frac{2 \mathrm{n}+.1}{2}\\= \tan ^{2} \theta+1=\frac{2 n+1}{2} \tan \theta\\= \tan ^{2} \theta-\frac{2 n+1}{2} \tan \theta+1=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\tan \theta & =\frac{\frac{2 n+1}{2} \pm \sqrt{\left(\frac{2 n+1}{2}\right)^{2}-4}}{2 \cdot 1} \\& =\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{2 n+1}{2} \pm \sqrt{\frac{4 n^{2}+4 n+1}{4}-4}\right\} \\& =\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{2 n+1}{2} \pm \frac{1}{2} \sqrt{4 n^{2}+4 n+1-16}\right\}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \theta=\frac{1}{4}\left\{(2 n+1) \pm \sqrt{4 n^{2}+4 n-15}\right\}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\alpha=\sin^{-1}\frac{1}{7}[/math] ধরা হলে [math]\sin\alpha=\frac{1}{7},\ \cos\alpha=\frac{4\sqrt3}{7}[/math] তাই [math]\sin2\alpha=2\sin\alpha\cos\alpha=\frac{8\sqrt3}{49}[/math] এবং [math]\cosec(2\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{7})=\frac{1}{\sin2\alpha}=\frac{49}{8\sqrt3}=\frac{49\sqrt3}{24}.[/math]

খ প্রথমে [math]\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt5}[/math] থেকে [math]\tan A=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\tan B=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\tan(A+B)=\frac{\tfrac12+\tfrac13}{1-\tfrac16}=1[/math], অতএব সমীকরণটি দেয় [math]\cot^2\theta+\tan^2\theta=2[/math] থেকে [math]\tan^2\theta=1[/math] এবং সাধারণ সমাধান [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{n\pi}{2},\ n\in\mathbb{Z}.[/math]

গ [math]\tan(\pi p)=\cot(\pi q)[/math] থেকে [math]\tan(\pi\cot\theta)=\tan\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\pi\tan\theta\big)[/math] হলে কন্‌গ্রুয়েন্স দিয়ে [math]\cot\theta+\tan\theta=\frac{1}{2}+n[/math] (n∈Z)। [math]t=\tan\theta[/math] এ নিয়ে [math]t^2-(n+\tfrac{1}{2})t+1=0[/math] সমাধান হলে [math]t=\frac{1}{4}(2n+1)\pm\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{4n^2+4n-15}[/math] এবং বাস্তব হওয়ার জন্য [math]4n^2+4n-15\ge0[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]n\le-3[/math] অথবা [math]n\ge2[/math] (অর্থাৎ [math]n1[/math])।

Question #41 (ID: 442519)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]A=2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}, f(\theta)=\cos \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]A=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
গ. [math]3 f(\theta)+2 f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=1[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}-\sqrt{1-(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)^{2}} \cdot \sqrt{1-(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})^{2}}\right\} \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}-\sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta} \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}-\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \sqrt{2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right) \\=\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}-\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} 0=\frac{\pi}{2}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\cos ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]A=2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]

[Image]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}{\frac{9}{8}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \times \frac{8}{9}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\=\sin ^{-1} \frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{9}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}{1-\frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7} \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{8+7}{7 \sqrt{2}}}{\frac{7 \sqrt{2}-4 \sqrt{2}}{7 \sqrt{2}}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{15}{3 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(\theta)=\cos \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\sin \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]3 f(\theta)+2 f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=1[/math]
ধরি,[math]\begin{array}{l}3=r \cos \alpha, 2=r \sin \alpha \\\therefore r^{2}=3^{2}+2^{2} \\\therefore r=\sqrt{13}\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]\tan \alpha=\frac{2}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 3 \cos \theta+2 \sin \theta=1\\= r \cos \alpha \cos \theta+r \sin \alpha \sin \theta=1\\= \cos (\theta-\alpha)=\frac{1}{r}\\= \cos (\theta-\alpha)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}\\= \theta-\alpha=2 n \pi \pm \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}\right)\\= \theta=2 n \pi \pm \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}\right)+\alpha\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3}\right) \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক নাম দেওয়া হলো [math]u=\cos^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin\theta),\; v=\cos^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos2\theta})[/math]. তখন [math]\cos u=\sqrt{2}\sin\theta,\; \cos v=\sqrt{\cos2\theta}[/math] ও প্রধান শাখায় উভয় সাইন অ-ঋণাত্মক হওয়ায় [math]\sin u=\sqrt{\cos2\theta},\; \sin v=\sqrt{2}\sin\theta[/math]. তাই [math]\cos(u+v)=\cos u\cos v-\sin u\sin v=0[/math], ফলে [math]u+v=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] প্রমাণিত।

খ লেখো [math]x=\sin^{-1}\frac{1}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\sin x=\frac{1}{3},\;\cos x=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math], তাই [math]\tan x=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}},\;\tan2x=\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{7}[/math]. এছাড়া [math]y=\cos^{-1}\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\tan y=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]. ফলে [math]\tan(2x+y)=\frac{\tan2x+\tan y}{1-\tan2x\tan y}=\frac{15\sqrt{2}/14}{3/7}=\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}=\frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math], সুতরাং [math]A=2x+y=\tan^{-1}\frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]।

গ সমীকরণে [math]3\cos\theta+2\cos\left(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=1[/math] হলে [math]3\cos\theta+2\sin\theta=1[/math]. লেখো [math]\sqrt{13}\cos(\theta-\alpha)=1[/math] যেখানে [math]\cos\alpha=\tfrac{3}{\sqrt{13}},\;\sin\alpha=\tfrac{2}{\sqrt{13}}[/math]; ফলে [math]\cos(\theta-\alpha)=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{13}} এবং সাধারণ সমাধান হচ্ছে [math]\theta=\alpha\pm\cos^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{13}}\big)+2k\pi,\;k\in\mathbb{Z} যেখানে [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{2}{3}[/math]।

Question #42 (ID: 442520)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta, g(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এবং [math]\mathrm{h}({\mathbf{x}})=\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]4\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-6 x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)=f(3 x)+f(x)[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\mathrm{h}\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)+\mathrm{g}\left(\frac{5}{12}\right)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3696}{4225}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}4\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5 \\\Rightarrow 4\left(1-\cos ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4-4 \cos ^{2} \theta+4 \cos \theta=5[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 \cos ^{2} \theta-4 \cos \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\phi(x)=\sin x, \psi(x)=\cos x \\\phi(\theta)+\phi(3 \theta)=\psi(4 \theta)+\psi(6 \theta)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin \theta+\sin 3 \theta=\cos 4 \theta+\cos 6 \theta\\=\cos 6 \theta+\cos 4 \theta=\sin 3 \theta+\sin \theta\\= 2 \cos 5 \theta \cos \theta=2 \sin 2 \theta \cos \theta\\= 2 \cos \theta(\cos 5 \theta-\sin 2 \theta)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos \theta=0[/math]
 যখন [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos 5 \theta-\sin 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]= \cos 5 \theta-\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=0[/math]
 [math]= 2 \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}\right) \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}\right)=0 \text {, }[/math]
,যখন [math]\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{3 \theta}{2}=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4} \\= 3 \theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore \theta=\frac{2}{3} n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}\right)=0[/math]
যখন [math]\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{k} \pi,[/math]
 যখন [math]k = 0[/math]
অথবা পূর্ণসংখ্যা।[math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{-7 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{k} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}\\= -7 \theta=2 \mathrm{k} \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}\\= \theta=-\frac{2 \mathrm{k}}{7} \pi+\frac{\pi}{14}=(-4 \mathrm{k}+1) \frac{\pi}{14}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{14}[-k=n[/math]
 ধরে][math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2},(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{6},(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{14}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গবামপক্ষ [math]=h\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)+g\left(\frac{5}{12}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{12}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{3^{2}+4^{2}}}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{12} \\=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{3}{5} \sqrt{1-\left(\frac{5}{13}\right)^{2}}+\frac{5}{13} \sqrt{1-\left(\frac{3}{5}\right)^{2}}\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{3}{5} \sqrt{\frac{169-25}{169}}+\frac{5}{13} \sqrt{\frac{25-9}{25}}\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{3}{5} \sqrt{\frac{144}{169}}+\frac{5}{13} \sqrt{\frac{16}{25}}\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{3}{5} \times \frac{12}{13}+\frac{5}{13} \times \frac{4}{5}\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{36}{65}+\frac{20}{65}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{56}{65}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{56}{65} \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(2 \cdot \frac{56}{65} \sqrt{1-\frac{56^{2}}{65^{2}}}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{112}{65} \sqrt{\frac{4225-3136}{4225}}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{112}{65} \sqrt{\frac{1089}{4225}}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{112}{65} \cdot \frac{33}{65}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3696}{4225}\end{array}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:

Question #43 (ID: 442516)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x, x \in]-\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2} [[/math] এর বিপরীত ফাংশন [math]f^{-1}(x)=\sin ^{-1} x .[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{7}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{8}{5}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{31}{75}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]f^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+f^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta})=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]y=f^{-1}(x)[/math] এর লেখচিত্র অঙ্কন কর, যখন [math]x \in[-1,1][/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{7}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{8}{5}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{8}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{5}{7}+\frac{5}{8}}{1-\frac{5}{7} \cdot \frac{5}{8}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{40+35}{56}}{\frac{56-25}{56}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{75}{31}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{31}{75}=[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খ(i) এ প্রদত্ত রাশি[math]=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}[/math]
 [math]\left.+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \times \sqrt{2} \times \sin \theta[/math]
 [math]+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos 2 \theta\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} \theta+1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right\}=\sin ^{-1} 1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f^{-1}(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
সুতরাং [math]y=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
যেখানে [math]x \in[-1,1][/math]
x এর মান নিয়ে তার প্রতিসঙ্গী y এর মানগুলো নিচের ছক দেওয়া হলো।[math]\begin{array}{|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|}\hline \mathrm{x} & -1 & -0.75 & -0.5 & \div 0.25 & 0 & 0.25 & 0.5 & 0.75 & 1 \\\hline \mathrm{y} & -90^{\circ} & -48.6^{\circ} & -30^{\circ} & -14.48^{\circ} & 0 & 14.48^{\circ} & 30^{\circ} & 48.6^{\circ} & 90^{\circ} \\\hline\end{array}[/math]
ছক কাগজের x অক্ষ বরাবর 20 ঘর =1 একক ও y অক্ষ বরাবরক্ষুদ্রতম 1 ঘর [math]= 5[/math]
 একক ধরে প্রাপ্ত x ও y এর মানগুলো বসিয়ে[math]y=f^{-1}(x)[/math]
 এর লেখচিত্র পাওয়া যায়।
[Image]


Explanation:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}\frac{5}{7}[/math] এবং [math]\beta=\cot^{-1}\frac{8}{5}[/math]; যেহেতু [math]\frac{8}{5}>0[/math], তাই [math]\beta=\tan^{-1}\frac{5}{8}[/math]. তখন
[math]\tan(\alpha+\beta)=\frac{\frac{5}{7}+\frac{5}{8}}{1-\frac{5}{7}\cdot\frac{5}{8}}=\frac{75/56}{31/56}=\frac{75}{31},[/math]
অতএব [math]\alpha+\beta=\tan^{-1}\frac{75}{31}=\cot^{-1}\frac{31}{75}[/math]।

খ ধর A=[math]f^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin\theta)=\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin\theta)[/math] এবং B=[math]f^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos2\theta})=\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos2\theta})[/math]. তখন [math]\sin A=\sqrt{2}\sin\theta[/math] এবং [math]\cos A=\sqrt{1-2\sin^2\theta}=\sqrt{\cos2\theta}=\sin B[/math], এবং উভয় কৌণিক মূল্যের ক্ষেত্রে কোণের মান ধনাত্মক হওয়ায় [math]\cos A=\sin B[/math] থেকে [math]A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয়।

গ [math]y=f^{-1}(x)=\sin^{-1}x[/math] হলো [math]y=\sin x[/math] এর রেখার প্রতি লম্ব প্রতিসাম্য (reflection) রেখা [math]y=x[/math] সম্পর্কে; domain [math][-1,1][/math], range [math]\left[-\frac{\pi}{2},\frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math], এবং কিছু প্রধান বিন্দু: [math](-1,-\frac{\pi}{2}),\ (0,0),\ (1,\frac{\pi}{2})[/math]।

Question #44 (ID: 442517)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sqrt{3} \sin x-\cos x[/math] এবং [math]g^{-1}(x)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=15[/math]
খ. [math]g^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{a}\right)+g^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{b}\right)=\theta[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
গ. [math]f(x)=2[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় করো ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & 1+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}^{2}+1+\left\{\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}^{2}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]=1+4+1+9[/math]
 [math]=15[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\cos \alpha=\frac{x}{a}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}[/math]
এবং [math]\cos \beta=\frac{y}{b}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}[/math]
আবার, দেওয়া আছে, [math]\alpha+\beta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\text{= } \cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}=\theta\\= \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{a} \cdot \frac{y}{b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}=\cos \theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta=\sqrt{1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}\\= \left(\frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta\right)^{2}=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]= \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-2 \frac{x y}{a b} \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-2 \frac{x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=1-\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sqrt{3} \sin x-\cos x[/math]
এবং [math]f(x)=2[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{3} \sin x-\cos x=2[/math]
উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\therefore \sqrt{3} \sin x-\cos x=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগকরে পাই, [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin x-\frac{1}{2} \cos x=1[/math]
 [math]= \sin x \cos \frac{\pi}{6}-\cos x \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=1[/math]
[যেহেতু [math]\left.\cos \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}, \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2}\right][/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=1[/math]
[যেহেতু [math]\sin x \cos y-\cos x \sin y=\sin (x-y)][/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [যেহেতু [math]\left.\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=1\right][/math]
 [math]\therefore x-\frac{\pi}{6}=m \pi+(-1)^{m} \frac{\pi}{2} \ldots \ldots \ldots \text { (1) }[/math]
m জোড় সংখ্যা হলে, ধরি, [math]m = 2n[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 সমীকরণ থেকে পাই[math]\begin{array}{l}x-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi+(-1)^{2 n \frac{\pi}{2}} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}\left[(-1)^{2 n}=1\right] \\\quad=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
যখন, [math]\mathrm{n}=0, \pm 1, \pm 2, \pm 3 \ldots \ldots[/math]
m বিজোড় সংখ্যা হলে ধরি [math]m = 2n + 1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 সমীকরণ থেকে পাই[math]\begin{array}{l}x-\frac{\pi}{6}=(2 n+1) \pi+(-1)^{2 n+1} \frac{\pi}{2}\\= x=2 n \pi+\pi-\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
যখন, [math]\mathrm{n}=0, \pm 1, \pm 2, \pm 3......[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 যখন, [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক যদি [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}2[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\tan\theta=2[/math] এবং [math]\sec^{2}\theta=1+\tan^{2}\theta=1+4=5[/math]; যদি [math]\phi=\cot^{-1}3[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\cot\phi=3[/math] এবং [math]\cosec^{2}\phi=1+\cot^{2}\phi=1+9=10[/math]; যোগ করলে [math]5+10=15[/math] পাওয়া যায়।
খ ধরে নিন দ্বিতীয়টি আসলে [math]g^{-1}\!\left(\frac{y}{b}\right)[/math] (অর্থাৎ [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{x}{a}+\cos^{-1}\frac{y}{b}=\theta[/math]) এবং বসান [math]u=\frac{x}{a},\;v=\frac{y}{b}[/math]; তাহলে [math]\arccos u+\arccos v=\theta[/math] থেকে [math]\cos(\arccos u+\arccos v)=\cos\theta=uv-\sqrt{1-u^{2}}\sqrt{1-v^{2}}গ [math]f(x)=\sqrt{3}\sin x-\cos x=2[/math] কে রূপান্তর করলে [math]f(x)=2\sin\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\bigr)[/math] হয়, সুতরাং [math]\sin\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\bigr)=1[/math] হলে সাধারণ সমাধান [math]x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+2k\pi,\;k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math].

Question #45 (ID: 442518)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y+\cos ^{-1} z=\pi \ldots \ldots \ldots \text { (i) } \\\cos \theta-\cos 7 \theta=\sin 4 \theta \ldots \ldots . \text { (ii) }\end{array}[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. (i) এর সাহায্যে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y=1[/math]
গ. (ii) সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\right)\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{9}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{9-1}{9}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{8}{9}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{8}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{7}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{21+4}{28}}{1-\frac{3}{28}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{25}{28}}{\frac{25}{28}}=\tan ^{-1}(1)=\frac{\pi}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপকের (1) থেকে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y+\cos ^{-1} z=\pi\\=\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\\= \cos ^{-1}\left(x y-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\\=x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=\cos \left(\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\right)\\= x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-\cos \cos ^{-1} z\end{array}[/math]
[দ্বিতীয় চতুর্ভাগে cos ঋণাত্মক][math]= x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-z[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \operatorname{coscos}^{-1} \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{x}\right][/math]
 [math]= x y+z=\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]= x^{2} y^{2}+2 x y z+z^{2}=\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]= x^{2} y^{2}+2 x y z+z^{2}=1-y^{2}-x^{2}+x^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]\cos \theta-\cos 7 \theta=\sin 4 \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \sin \frac{\theta+7 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{7 \theta-\theta}{2}-\sin 4 \theta=0\\= 2 \sin 4 \theta \sin 3 \theta-\sin 4 \theta=0\\= \sin 4 \theta(2 \sin 3 \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\sin 4 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 4 \theta=n \pi\\= \theta=\frac{n \pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \sin 3 \theta-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \cdot \sin 3 \theta=1\\=\sin 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\= \sin 3 \theta=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}\\= 3 \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}\\=\theta=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18} \text{.}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{4} ; \frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]
 যখন [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরি [math]A=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3},\;B=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{7}[/math]. তাহলে [math]\tan 2A=\frac{2(1/3)}{1-(1/3)^2}=\frac{3}{4}[/math] ও [math]\tan B=\frac{1}{7}[/math], ফলে
[math]\tan(2A+B)=\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{7}}=\frac{25/28}{25/28}=1[/math],
অতএব [math]2A+B=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] (প্রধান শাখায়) এবং তাই [math]2\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{7}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]।

খ যদি [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}x,\;\beta=\cos^{-1}y,\;\gamma=\cos^{-1}z[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta+\gamma=\pi[/math], তাহলে [math]z=\cos\gamma=-\cos(\alpha+\beta)=-xy+\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\sqrt{1-y^{2}}[/math] থেকে
[math](z+xy)^{2}=(1-x^{2})(1-y^{2})[/math] এবং সরল করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2xyz=1[/math]। (মূল সূত্রে [math]2xy[/math] না এসে [math]2xyz[/math] হওয়া উচিত — প্রদত্ত বিবৃতিটি সম্ভবত টাইপো।)

গ বামপাশকে ব্যবহার করি: [math]\cos\theta-\cos7\theta=-2\sin\frac{8\theta}{2}\sin\frac{-6\theta}{2}=2\sin4\theta\sin3\theta[/math], ফলে সমীকরণটি হয় [math]2\sin4\theta\sin3\theta=\sin4\theta[/math]. তাই [math]\sin4\theta(2\sin3\theta-1)=0[/math], ফলে
(১) [math]\sin4\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{n\pi}{4}[/math],
(২) [math]\sin3\theta=\tfrac12\Rightarrow 3\theta=\frac{\pi}{6}+2n\pi\ \text{বা}\ \frac{5\pi}{6}+2n\pi[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{18}+\frac{2n\pi}{3}[/math] বা [math]\theta=\frac{5\pi}{18}+\frac{2n\pi}{3}[/math] (ইনটিজার [math]n[/math])।

Question #46 (ID: 442515)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প- ১


[Image]


দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]\phi(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ অবলম্বনে দেখাও যে, [math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]
খ. [math]\sin \left\{\pi \phi\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right\}=\cos \{\pi \phi(\theta)\}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\theta= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
গ. সাধারণ সমাধান কর : [math]\phi(7 \theta)-\sqrt{3} \phi\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 \theta\right)=\phi(\theta) .[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} .[/math]
[ত্রিভুজ থেকে]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{\sqrt{2+x^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\begin{array}{l}\sin \left\{\pi \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right\}=\cos \{\pi \sin (\theta)\}\\=\sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\cos (\pi \sin \theta)\\= \sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right)\\= \pi \cos \theta=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\\= \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin \theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta \pm \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \cos ^{2} \theta+\sin ^{2} \theta \pm 2 \sin \theta \cos \theta=\frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]= 1 \pm \sin 2 \theta=\frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 \theta=1-\frac{1}{4}=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin 2 \theta= \pm \frac{3}{4} \\= 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1}\left( \pm \frac{3}{4}\right)= \pm \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\begin{array}{l}\phi(7 \theta)-\sqrt{3} \phi\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 \theta\right)=\phi(\theta)\\= \sin 7 \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 \theta\right)=\sin \theta\\= \sin 7 \theta-\sin \theta-\sqrt{3} \cos 4 \theta=0\\= 2 \cos 4 \theta \sin ^{\circ} 3 \theta-\sqrt{3} \cos 4 \theta=0\\= \cos 4 \theta(2 \sin 3 \theta-\sqrt{3})=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos 4 \theta=0 \text {, }[/math]
 যখন [math]4 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]= \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \sin 3 \theta-\sqrt{3}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin 3 \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore 3 \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}=\left\{3 n+(-1)^{n}\right\} \frac{\pi}{3} \\\therefore \theta=\left\{3 n+(-1)^{n}\right\} \frac{\pi}{9} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ক টান-অ্যাঙ্গেল নাও [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}x[/math], তাতে [math]\cos\alpha=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}[/math]. যদি [math]\beta=\cot^{-1}(\cos\alpha)[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\cot\beta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}[/math] ও [math]\tan\beta=\sqrt{1+x^{2}}[/math], ফলে [math]\sin\beta=\dfrac{\tan\beta}{\sqrt{1+\tan^{2}\beta}}=\sqrt{\dfrac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math].

খ [math]\sin\{\pi\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)\}=\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\cos(\pi\sin\theta)=\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\pi\sin\theta\big)[/math], তাই বা'দ দুইটি ক্ষেত্রে হওয়া যায়: [math]\pi\cos\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{2}\mp\pi\sin\theta[/math], ফলে [math]\sin2\theta=\pm\tfrac{3}{4}[/math]. অতএব মূল ধ্রুবক সমাধানগুলোকে সংক্ষেপে লেখা যায় [math]\theta=\pm\frac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{4}[/math] (প্রাসঙ্গিক মৌলিক শাখায়)।

গ সমীকরণকে বদলে লেখো [math]\sin7\theta-\sqrt{3}\cos4\theta=\sin\theta\Rightarrow \sin7\theta-\sin\theta=\sqrt{3}\cos4\theta[/math]. বামপাশে পরিচিত অন্তর-রূপে [math]\sin7\theta-\sin\theta=2\cos4\theta\sin3\theta[/math], সুতরাং [math]\cos4\theta(2\sin3\theta-\sqrt{3})=0[/math]. অতএব (i) [math]\cos4\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{8}+k\tfrac{\pi}{4}[/math] অথবা (ii) [math]\sin3\theta=\tfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{9}+ \tfrac{2k\pi}{3}\ বা \theta=\tfrac{2\pi}{9}+ \tfrac{2k\pi}{3}, যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #47 (ID: 442513)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \alpha=\frac{4}{5}, \cos \beta=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}, f(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. উদ্দীপক অবলম্বনে দেখাও যে, [math]\tan (\alpha+\beta)=\frac{11}{2}[/math]
গ. সাধারণ সমাধান কর : [math]f(9 \theta) f(7 \theta)=f(5 \theta) f(3 \theta)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কমনে করি, [math]\theta=\sin ^{-1} x \text {. }[/math]
এখন, [math]\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\sin \theta=x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\pi}{2}-\theta=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} x \\\therefore \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\begin{array}{l}\alpha+\beta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{6}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan (\alpha+\beta)=\frac{11}{2}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]f(9 \theta) f(7 \theta)=f(5 \theta) f(3 \theta)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos 9 \theta \cos 7 \theta=\cos 5 \theta \cos 3 \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \cos 9 \theta \cos 7 \theta=2 \cos 5 \theta \cos 3 \theta\\= \cos 16 \theta+\cos 2 \theta=\cos 8 \theta+\cos 2 \theta\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \cos 8 \theta-\cos 16 \theta=0[/math]
 বা, [math]2 \sin 12 \theta \sin 4 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin 12 \theta=0[/math]
 বা, [math]12 \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
অর্থাৎ [math]\theta=\frac{1}{12} n \pi[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin 4 \theta=0[/math]
 বা, [math]4 \theta=n \pi,[/math]
অর্থাৎ, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{4} n \pi,[/math]
 যখন [math]n = 0[/math]
অথবা যেকোন পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\sin^{-1}x[/math], তখন [math]\sin\theta=x[/math] এবং [math]\theta\in[-\pi/2,\pi/2][/math]; কিন্তু [math]\cos(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\sin\theta=x[/math] হওয়ায় এবং arccos এর মূল রেঞ্জ [math][0,\pi][/math] হওয়ায় [math]\cos^{-1}x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math], ফলে [math]\sin^{-1}x+\cos^{-1}x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয়।

খ [math]\sin\alpha=\tfrac{4}{5}[/math] হলে (ধরা হলো কোণগুলো ধারাবাহিক) [math]\cos\alpha=\tfrac{3}{5}[/math] তাই [math]\tan\alpha=\tfrac{4}{3}[/math]; আর [math]\cos\beta=\tfrac{2}{\sqrt5}[/math] হলে [math]\sin\beta=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt5}[/math] এবং [math]\tan\beta=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]. অতএব [math]\tan(\alpha+\beta)=\frac{\tan\alpha+\tan\beta}{1-\tan\alpha\tan\beta}=\frac{4/3+1/2}{1-(4/3)(1/2)}=\frac{11}{2}[/math]।

গ [math]\cos9\theta\cos7\theta=\cos5\theta\cos3\theta[/math] থেকে বিয়োগ করে এবং গুণ-থেকে-যোগ সূত্র প্রয়োগ করলে পাই [math]\tfrac12(\cos16\theta+\cos2\theta)-\tfrac12(\cos8\theta+\cos2\theta)=\tfrac12(\cos16\theta-\cos8\theta)=0[/math], তাই [math]\cos16\theta=\cos8\theta[/math]. অতএব [math]16\theta=2k\pi\pm8\theta[/math], ফলে [math]\theta=\tfrac{k\pi}{4}[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\tfrac{k\pi}{12}[/math], যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #48 (ID: 442514)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \alpha=\frac{3}{5}, \cos \theta=\frac{5}{13}, \tan \beta=\frac{1}{2}[/math] এবং [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখা [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)=25[/math]
খ. সমাধান কর : [math]\{f(\theta)\}^{2}+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\frac{5}{4}[/math] যখন [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan \left(\frac{1}{2} \theta+\alpha-\beta\right)=\frac{28}{29}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কমনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \theta=4[/math]
 এবং [math]\sec ^{-1} 3=\phi[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sec \phi=3[/math]
এখন, বামপক্ষ [math]=\sec ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \phi[/math]
 [math]=1+\tan ^{2} \theta+\sec ^{2} \phi-1[/math]
 [math]=1+16+9-1=25=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\{f(\theta)\}^{2}+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta=\frac{5}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 4\left(1-\cos ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5\\= 4 \cos ^{2} \theta-4 \cos \theta+1=0\\= (2 \cos \theta-1)^{2}=0\\= 2 \cos \theta-1=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{1}{3} \pi[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{1}{3} \pi[/math]
এখন [math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 ধরলে [math]\theta= \pm \frac{1}{3} \pi ; \mathrm{n}=1[/math]
ধরলে [math]\theta=\frac{5}{3} \pi, \frac{7}{3} \pi[/math]
এবং [math]n = - 1[/math]
 ধরলে [math]\theta=-\frac{5}{3} \pi,-\frac{7}{3} \pi[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]\pm \frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
 (সম্ভাব্য মানসমূহ) (Ans.)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\cos \theta=\frac{5}{13} \therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}=\theta[/math]
এবং [math]\alpha=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
সুতরাং [math]\tan \frac{1}{2} \theta=\frac{\sin \frac{1}{2} \theta}{\cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 \sin \frac{1}{2} \theta \cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}{2 \cos ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\sin \theta}{1+\cos \theta}=\frac{\sqrt{1-\frac{25}{169}}}{1+\frac{5}{13}}=\frac{\frac{12}{13}}{\frac{18}{13}}=\frac{2}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{1}{2} \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]\beta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
এখন, [math]\frac{1}{2} \theta+\alpha-\beta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{2}{3}}{1-\frac{6}{12}}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{17}{6}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17}{6}-\frac{1}{2}}{1+\frac{17}{12}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \left(\frac{1}{2} \theta+\alpha-\beta\right)=\frac{28}{29}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক যদি [math]u=\tan^{-1}4[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\tan u=4[/math] এবং [math]\sec^{2}u=1+\tan^{2}u=17[/math]; যদি [math]v=\sec^{-1}3[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\sec v=3[/math] থেকে [math]\tan^{2}v=\sec^{2}v-1=8[/math]; তাই যোগফল [math]17+8=25[/math]।

খ [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] রেখে সমীকরণটি লেখা যায় [math]\sin^{2}\theta+\cos\theta=5/4[/math]; [math]\sin^{2}\theta=1-\cos^{2}\theta[/math] বসালে পাওয়া যায় [math]\cos^{2}\theta-\cos\theta+1/4=0[/math] যার একক মূল [math]\cos\theta=1/2[/math]; ফলে [math]\theta=\pm\pi/3+2k\pi[/math] এবং [math]-2\piগ [math]\cos\theta=5/13[/math] হলে (ধরা হলে) [math]\sin\theta=12/13[/math] এবং [math]\tan(\theta/2)=\dfrac{\sin\theta}{1+\cos\theta}=\dfrac{2}{3}; [math]\tan\alpha=3/4[/math], [math]\tan\beta=1/2[/math] ব্যবহার করে [math]\tan(\theta/2+\alpha)=\dfrac{2/3+3/4}{1-(2/3)(3/4)}=\dfrac{17}{6}</math] এবং অবশেষে [math]\tan(\theta/2+\alpha-\beta)=\dfrac{17/6-1/2}{1+(17/6)(1/2)}=\dfrac{28}{29}</math]।

Question #49 (ID: 442510)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important

Question:
[math]\sin \theta=\frac{3}{5}, f(x)=\cos x, g(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{x}=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x}{1+x}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \theta+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]g(\theta)-f(2 \theta)=2,[/math] যখন [math]-2 \pi \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{\mathrm{x}}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{\mathrm{x}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-(\sqrt{x})^{2}}{1+(\sqrt{x})^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x}{1+x}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{x}=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x}{1+x}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খ=বামপক্ষ [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \theta+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{10}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]

[Image]
[math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [চিত্র-১ হতে] = ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \theta+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]g(\theta)-f(2 \theta)=2 \Rightarrow \sin \theta-\cos 2 \theta=2[/math]
 [math]\sin \theta-2=\cos 2 \theta[/math]
বা, [math]\sin \theta-2=1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]=2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\sin \theta-3=0[/math]
 [math](\sin \theta-1)(2 \sin \theta+3)=0[/math]
কিন্তু [math](2 \sin \theta+3) 
eq 0[/math]
 কারণ[math]\sin \theta[/math]
 এর সংখ্যা সূচক মান – 1অপেক্ষা ক্ষুদ্রতর হতে পারে না।[math]\therefore \sin \theta-1=0[/math]
 বা, [math]\sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]= \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \text {, }[/math]
 যখন n এর মান শূন্যবা যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।যখন [math]n = - 1, 0, 1;[/math]
 তখন [math]\theta=-\frac{3}{2} \pi ; \frac{1}{2} \pi, \frac{5}{2} \pi[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধির সীমাবদ্ধতার মধ্যে [math]\theta=-\frac{3}{2} \pi, \frac{1}{2} \pi[/math]


Explanation:
ক ধরি [math]\tan^{-1}\sqrt{x}=\alpha[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\alpha=\sqrt{x} ও [math]\cos2\alpha=\frac{1-\tan^2\alpha}{1+\tan^2\alpha}=\frac{1-x}{1+x}; তাই [math]2\alpha=\cos^{-1}\frac{1-x}{1+x}খ প্রথমে [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt5}=\phi[/math] ধরা হলে [math]\cos\phi=\frac{1}{\sqrt5},\ \sin\phi=\frac{2}{\sqrt5} ফলে [math]\tan\phi=2 এবং [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt5}=\tan^{-1}2. অন্যদিকে [math]\tan\frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{1-\cos\theta}{\sin\theta}=\frac{1-\tfrac{4}{5}}{\tfrac{3}{5}}=\frac{1}{3}, তাই [math]\frac{\theta}{2}=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3} এবং দেওয়া সমীকরণ পূর্ণ হয়।

গ সমীকরণটি হচ্ছে [math]\sin\theta-\cos2\theta=2 এবং [math]\cos2\theta=-1; দেওয়া সীমায় ([math]-2\pi\le\theta\le2\pi এবং [math]\theta=-\frac{3\pi}{2} মানগুলি আছে।

Question #50 (ID: 442511)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \alpha=\frac{x}{a}, \cos \beta=\frac{y}{b}[/math] এবং [math]\phi(x)=\sin x[/math] [math]\psi(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math] হলে, দেখাও যে, [math]x=\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
খ. [math]\alpha+\beta=\theta[/math] হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{a b} \cos \theta+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
গ. সাধারণ সমাধান করঃ [math]\phi(\theta)+\phi(3 \theta)=\psi(4 \theta)+\psi(6 \theta)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
এখন, [math]2 f(x)=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \tan ^{-1} a-2 \tan ^{-1} b=2 \tan ^{-1} x\\= \tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1} b=\tan ^{-1} x\\= \tan ^{-1} \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=\tan ^{-1} x\\= \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\frac{\mathrm{a}-\mathrm{b}}{1+\mathrm{ab}}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\cos \alpha=\frac{x}{a}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}[/math]
এবং [math]\cos \beta=\frac{y}{b}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}[/math]
আবার, দেওয়া আছে, [math]\alpha+\beta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\text{= } \cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{b}=\theta\\= \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{a} \cdot \frac{y}{b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}=\cos \theta\\= \frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta=\sqrt{1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}\\= \left(\frac{x y}{a b}-\cos \theta\right)^{2}=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]= \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-2 \frac{x y}{a b} \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-2 \frac{x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=1-\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y \cos \theta}{a b}+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\phi(x)=\sin x, \psi(x)=\cos x \\\phi(\theta)+\phi(3 \theta)=\psi(4 \theta)+\psi(6 \theta)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin \theta+\sin 3 \theta=\cos 4 \theta+\cos 6 \theta\\=\cos 6 \theta+\cos 4 \theta=\sin 3 \theta+\sin \theta\\= 2 \cos 5 \theta \cos \theta=2 \sin 2 \theta \cos \theta\\= 2 \cos \theta(\cos 5 \theta-\sin 2 \theta)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos \theta=0[/math]
 যখন [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos 5 \theta-\sin 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]= \cos 5 \theta-\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=0[/math]
 [math]= 2 \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}\right) \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}\right)=0 \text {, }[/math]
, যখন [math]\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{3 \theta}{2}=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4} \\= 3 \theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore \theta=\frac{2}{3} n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}\right)=0[/math]
 যখন [math]\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{7 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{k} \pi,[/math]
 যখন [math]k = 0[/math]
অথবা পূর্ণসংখ্যা।[math]\begin{array}{l}= \frac{-7 \theta}{2}=\mathrm{k} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}\\= -7 \theta=2 \mathrm{k} \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}\\= \theta=-\frac{2 \mathrm{k}}{7} \pi+\frac{\pi}{14}=(-4 \mathrm{k}+1) \frac{\pi}{14}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{14}[-k=n[/math]
 ধরে ][math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2},(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{6},(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{14}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\sin^{-1}\frac{2a}{1+a^{2}}=2\tan^{-1}a[/math] এবং [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}=2\tan^{-1}b[/math] প্রত্যক্ষ সূত্র থেকে মূল সমীকরণ দাঁড়ায় [math]2\tan^{-1}a-2\tan^{-1}b=2\tan^{-1}x[/math], ফলে [math]\tan^{-1}x=\tan^{-1}\frac{a-b}{1+ab}[/math] থেকে [math]x=\frac{a-b}{1+ab}[/math] পাওয়া যায়।

খ [math]\cos\alpha=\frac{x}{a},\ \cos\beta=\frac{y}{b}[/math] হলে বাম পিঠি লেখলে তা হয় [math]\cos^{2}\alpha-2\cos\alpha\cos\beta\cos(\alpha+\beta)+\cos^{2}\beta[/math] এবং ডান পিঠি বিস্তৃত করলে [math]\sin^{2}(\alpha+\beta)=\sin^{2}\alpha\cos^{2}\beta+\cos^{2}\alpha\sin^{2}\beta+2\sin\alpha\cos\alpha\sin\beta\cos\beta[/math]; [math]\sin^{2}=1-\cos^{2}[/math] প্রয়োগ করে দুইটি অভিব্যক্তি সমান হওয়ায় প্রদত্ত সমীকরণটি সত্য হয়।

গ প্রথমে যোগ সূত্র ব্যবহার করে [math]\sin\theta+\sin3\theta=2\sin2\theta\cos\theta[/math] এবং [math]\cos4\theta+\cos6\theta=2\cos5\theta\cos\theta[/math], ফলে [math]2\cos\theta(\sin2\theta-\cos5\theta)=0[/math]. অতএব (i) [math]\cos\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{\pi}{2}+k\pi[/math] বা (ii) [math]\sin2\theta=\cos5\theta[/math], যেখান থেকে সাধারণ সমাধান দেয় [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{14}+\frac{2k\pi}{7}[/math] বা [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{2k\pi}{3}[/math] (যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা [math]k[/math])।

Question #51 (ID: 442512)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f({x})=\tan ^{-1} x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর : [math]2 f(3)+f(7)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]2 f\left(\sqrt{\frac{a}{b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math] [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} g(\theta)}{b+a+(b-a) g(\theta)}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]2 g(\theta) g(3 \theta)=1[/math] যখন [math]0<\theta<2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=2 f(3)+f(7)=2 \tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 7[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times 3}{1-9}+\tan ^{-1} 7 \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{6}{-8}\right)+\tan ^{-1} 7 \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{-3}{4}\right)+\tan ^{-1} 7 \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{7-\frac{3}{4}}{1+7 \times \frac{3}{4}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{25 / 4}{25 / 4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore 2 \mathrm{f}(3)+\mathrm{f}(7)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খবামপক্ষ [math]=2 f\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{a}}{\mathrm{b}}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{a}{b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right) .[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a / b} \cdot \tan \frac{\theta}{2}}{1+a / b \cdot \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} \cdot \tan \frac{1}{2} \theta}{b+a \tan ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} \cdot \sin \frac{1}{2} \theta / \cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}{b+a \sin ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta / \cos ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} \sin \frac{1}{2} \theta \cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}{b \cos ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta+a \sin ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta} \\=\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{a b} \sin \theta}{\frac{b}{2}(1+\cos \theta)+\frac{a}{2}(1-\cos \theta)} . \\=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} \sin \theta}{(b+a)+(b-a) \cos \theta}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{a b} g(\theta)}{b+a+(b-a) g(\theta)}[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]2 g(\theta) g(3 \theta)=1[/math]
 বা, [math]2 \sin \theta \sin 3 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\cos 2 \theta-\cos 4 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\cos 2 \theta-(1+\cos 4 \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]= \cos 2 \theta-2 \cos ^{2} 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]= \cos 2 \theta(1-2 \cos 2 \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos 2 \theta=0[/math]
 অর্থাৎ [math]2 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{1}{2} \pi[/math]
 [math]= \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{1}{4} \pi[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos 2 \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{1}{3} \pi[/math]
 [math]\cos 2 \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{1}{3} \pi[/math]
 বা [math]\theta=n \pi \pm \frac{1}{6} \pi[/math]
সুতরাং [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{1}{4} \pi, n \pi \pm \frac{1}{6} \pi[/math]
এখন [math]n = 0[/math]
 ধরলে [math]\theta=\frac{1}{4} \pi, \pm \frac{1}{6} \pi ; \mathrm{n}=1[/math]
ধরলে [math]\theta=\frac{3}{4} \pi, \frac{5}{6} \pi, \frac{7}{6} \pi[/math]
 [math]n = 2[/math]
 ধরলে [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{4}, \frac{11 \pi}{6}, \frac{13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 এবং [math]n = 3[/math]
ধরলে [math]\theta=\frac{7 \pi}{4}[/math]
 ইত্যাদি।[math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত ব্যবধির মধ্যে[math]\theta=\frac{1}{4} \pi, \frac{3}{4} \pi, \frac{5}{4} \pi, \frac{7}{4} \pi, \frac{1}{6} \pi, \frac{5}{6} \pi \frac{7}{6} \pi[/math]
এবং [math]\frac{11}{6} \pi .[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক [math]\tan(2\arctan 3)=\frac{2\cdot3}{1-3^2}=-\frac{3}{4}[/math] এবং তাই
[math]\tan(2\arctan 3+\arctan 7)=\frac{-3/4+7}{1-(-3/4)\cdot7}=1[/math]। অতএব [math]2\arctan3+\arctan7=\frac{\pi}{4}\pmod{\pi}[/math]; এই কৌণিক যোগফল বাস্তবে [math]\frac{5\pi}{4}[/math] (প্রাসঙ্গিক শাখা নেওয়া হলে)।

খ [math]t=\tan\frac{\theta}{2},\; u=\sqrt{\frac{a}{b}}\,t[/math] নিলে
[math]\sin\big(2\arctan u\big)=\frac{2u}{1+u^2}=\frac{2\sqrt{ab}\,t}{a+b+(b-a)\frac{2t}{1+t^2}}[/math] এবং [math]\frac{2t}{1+t^2}=\sin\theta[/math] বসালে সরলীকরণে দাঁড়ায় প্রদত্ত যোগ্যতা, তাই সমতা সত্য।

গ প্রশ্নটি লিখে নেওয়া যায় [math]2\sin\theta\sin3\theta=\cos2\theta-\cos4\theta=1[/math], যা সরলীকরণে
[math]\cos2\theta(1-2\cos2\theta)=0[/math] দেয়। ফলে [math]\cos2\theta=0[/math] বা [math]\cos2\theta=\tfrac12[/math], এবং 0<[math]\theta<2\pi[/math] হলে λύসমূহ হচ্ছে
[math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4},\frac{3\pi}{4},\frac{5\pi}{4},\frac{7\pi}{4},\frac{\pi}{6},\frac{5\pi}{6},\frac{7\pi}{6},\frac{11\pi}{6}[/math]।

Question #52 (ID: 442506)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]\theta[/math] কে [math]\cos ^{-1}[/math] ফাংশনে প্রকাশ কর।
খ. [math](A-B)[/math] কোণকে [math]\tan ^{-1}[/math] ফাংশনে প্রকাশ কর । [math]A-B=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] হলে, x এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\angle \mathrm{D}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math] এবং [math]\angle \mathrm{E}=\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] হলে, [math]F[/math] কোণের মান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
এখানে, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ
[Image]
চিত্র হতে পাই, [math]B=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]A=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad A-B=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}-\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{x}-x}{1+\frac{1}{x} x}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1-x^{2}}{x}}{1+1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1-x^{2}}{x}}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{2 x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \mathrm{A}-\mathrm{B}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}{2 \mathrm{x}} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{2 x}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 বা, [math]\frac{1-x^{2}}{2 x}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]= \frac{1-x^{2}}{2 x}=1[/math]
 বা, [math]2 x=1-x^{2}[/math]
 বা, [math]x^{2}+2 x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{(2)^{2}-4 \cdot 1 \cdot(-1)}}{2 \cdot 1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{4+4}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{8}}{2}=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{4.2}}{2}=\frac{-2 \pm 2 \sqrt{2}}{2} \\=\frac{2(-1 \pm \sqrt{2})}{2}=-1 \pm \sqrt{2} \\\therefore x=-1 \pm \sqrt{2} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গDEF ত্রিভুজে, [math]\angle \mathrm{D}+\angle \mathrm{E}+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi \angle \mathrm{D}[/math]
এবং [math]\angle \mathrm{E}[/math]
 এর মান বসিয়ে][math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi\\= \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{-5}\right)+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi\\= \tan ^{-1}(-1)+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi\\= \tan ^{-1} \tan \left(\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi\\= \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}+\angle \mathrm{F}=\pi\\\therefore \angle \mathrm{F}=\frac{\pi}{4} \text{ (Ans.)}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক সাধারণ রূপে কোনো কোণকে [math]\cos^{-1}[/math]-এ প্রকাশ করতে হলে আমরা কোণের কসাইন করে সেটিকে ইনভার্স কসম দ্বারা লিখি; উদাহরণ স্বরূপ, যদি [math]\sin θ = s[/math] থাকে তাহলে [math]\cos^2θ=1-s^2[/math] থেকে [math]θ=\cos^{-1}\big(\pm\sqrt{1-s^2}\,\big)[/math] (চিহ্নটি কোণের চতুর্ভুজাংশের ওপর নির্ভর করে) — কারণ [math]\cos[/math] এবং [math]\sin[/math] একই ত্রিকোণাঙ্কের অংশ। 

খ পার্থক্যকে [math]\tan^{-1}[/math]-এ প্রকাশ করতে [math]\tan(A-B)=\dfrac{\tan A-\tan B}{1+\tan A\tan B}[/math] সূত্রটি ব্যবহার করি; [math]A-B=\pi/4[/math] হলে [math]\tan(A-B)=1[/math] তাই [math]\dfrac{\tan A-\tan B}{1+\tan A\tan B}=1[/math] হবে এবং এই সমীকরণ থেকে দেওয়া [math]\tan A,\tan B[/math] (অথবা x-প্রকাশ) বসিয়ে x-এর মান সরাসরি নির্ণয় করা যায়। 

গ এখানে [math]\angle D=\tan^{-1}2,\ \angle E=\tan^{-1}3[/math] হলে [math]\tan(D+E)=\dfrac{2+3}{1-2\cdot3}=\dfrac{5}{-5}=-1[/math] ফলে [math]D+E=\pi-\tan^{-1}5[/math] (সঠিক কোণ শাখার জন্য)। তাই [math]F=\pi-(D+E)=\tan^{-1}5[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]F=\tan^{-1}5[/math]।

Question #53 (ID: 442507)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{(i) } \cot ^{-1}\left(2 \cdot n^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1}(2 n-1)-\cot ^{-1}(2 n+1) \text{;}\\\text{(ii) } 4 \cos x \cos 2 x \cos 3 x=1 \text{.}\end{array}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math](4 \cos x+3 \sin x)[/math] কে [math]r \cos (x-\alpha)[/math] যেখানে, [math]\mathbf{r} \in \mathbb{R}[/math] এবং [math]\alpha<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] আকারে প্রকাশ কর । ( [math]\alpha[/math] এর মান দুই দশমিক স্থান পর্যন্ত)
খ. [math]0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] ব্যবধিতে (ii) এ প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ সমাধান কর ।
গ. উদ্দীপকের (i) এর আলোকে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.4^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1} \frac{14}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}4 \cos x+3 \sin x=5\left(\frac{4 \cos x}{5}+\frac{3 \sin x}{5}\right) \\=5\left(\cos x \times \frac{4}{5}+\sin x \times \frac{3}{5}\right) \\=5\left(\cos x \times \cos 36.87^{\circ}+\sin x \times \sin 36.87^{\circ}\right) \\=5 \cos \left(x-36.87^{\circ}\right)(\text { Ans. })\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]4 \cos x \cos 2 x \cos 3 x=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= (2 \cos 3 x \cos x)(2 \cos 2 x)=1\\=(\cos 4 x+\cos 2 x)(2 \cos 2 x)=1\\= 2 \cos 4 x \cos 2 x+2 \cos ^{2} 2 x-1=0\\= 2 \cos 4 x \cos 2 x+\cos 4 x=0\\=\cos 4 x(2 \cos 2 x+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 হয়, [math]\cos 4 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 4 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore \quad x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=2 \cos 2 x=-1 \\= \cos 2 x=-\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\= 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\therefore \quad x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
যখন, n এর মান শূন্য বা যে কোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।যখন [math]n = 0,[/math]
 তখন, [math]x=\frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{-\pi}{3}[/math]
যখন [math]n = 1,[/math]
 তখন, [math]x=\frac{3 \pi}{8}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট সীমার মধ্যে x এর মানসমূহ:[math]\frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{3 \pi}{8} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]\cot ^{-1}\left(2 n^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1}(2 n-1)-\cot ^{-1}(2 n+1)[/math]
 [math]n = 2, 3, 4[/math]
 বসিয়ে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1} 3-\cot ^{-1} 5 \\\cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1} 5-\cot ^{-1} 7 \\\cot ^{-1}\left(2.4^{2}\right)=\cot ^{-1} 7-\cot ^{-1} 9\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.4^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1} 3-\cot ^{-1} 9[/math]
 [যোগ করে]এখন, [math]\cot ^{-1} 3-\cot ^{-1} 9=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{9}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{9}}{1+\frac{1}{3} \times \frac{1}{9}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3-1}{9}}{1+\frac{1}{27}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{9}}{\frac{28}{27}} \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{9} \times \frac{27}{28}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{14}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{14}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot ^{-1}\left(2.2^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.3^{2}\right)+\cot ^{-1}\left(2.4^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{14}{3}\right)[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]4\cos x+3\sin x=r\cos(x-\alpha)[/math] হলে [math]r\cos\alpha=4,\;r\sin\alpha=3[/math], তাই [math]r=\sqrt{4^{2}+3^{2}}=5[/math] এবং [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{4}\approx0.64[/math] রেডিয়ান (দুই দশমিক স্থানে)।

খ শুরুতে [math]\sin4x=4\sin x\cos x\cos2x[/math] ব্যবহার করে [math]4\cos x\cos2x\cos3x=\frac{\sin4x}{\sin x}\cos3x[/math], তাই সমীকরণ থেকে [math]\sin4x\cos3x=\sin x[/math]. ফলত [math]\tfrac{\sin7x+\sin x}{2}=\sin x[/math] হয় এবং [math]\sin7x=\sin x\Rightarrow 2\cos4x\sin3x=0[/math], ফলে [math]\cos4x=0[/math] বা [math]\sin3x=0[/math].  উপযুক্ত সীমায় সমাধানগুলো: [math]x=\pi/8,\;3\pi/8,\;\pi/3[/math]।

গ প্রদত্ত (i) থেকে n=2,3,4 এর যোগ করলে টেলিস্কোপিং হয়: [math]\cot^{-1}(2\cdot2^{2})+\cot^{-1}(2\cdot3^{2})+\cot^{-1}(2\cdot4^{2})=\cot^{-1}3-\cot^{-1}9[/math]; এবং [math]\cot^{-1}u-\cot^{-1}v=\cot^{-1}\frac{uv+1}{v-u}[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে [math]\cot^{-1}3-\cot^{-1}9=\cot^{-1}\frac{28}{6}=\cot^{-1}\frac{14}{3}[/math]।
