Question #93 (ID: 442469)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]P(x)=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]P(x)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]P\left(\sqrt{\frac{a-b}{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a \cos \theta}{a+b \cos \theta}[/math]
গ. দেখাও যে, [math]\tan (P(x))=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} x^{3}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]P(x)=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
মনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}=\theta \quad \therefore \tan \theta=\mathrm{x}[/math]
আমরা জানি, [math]\tan 2 \theta=\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}} \\\therefore \quad 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 খ(i) বামপক্ষ [math]=2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{a-b^{}}{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{r}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{a-b}{a+b} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{1+\frac{a-b}{a+b} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \\=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{(a-b)}{(a+b)} \cdot \frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}{1+\frac{(a-b)}{(a+b)} \cdot \frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{(a+b) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-(a-b) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{(a+b) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+(a-b) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \\=\cos ^{-1} \frac{a \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-a \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{a \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+a \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-b \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \\=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+a\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)}{a\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+b\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a \cos \theta}{a+b \cos \theta}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \cos 2 \theta=\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta\right][/math]
= ডানপক্ষ [cos20 = cos20 - sin20 ][math]\therefore 2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{a-b}{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a \cos \theta}{a+b \cos \theta}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan \left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
ডানপক্ষ [math]=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} x^{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+x^{3}}{1-x^{4}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{x\left(1+x^{2}\right)}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)\left(1-x^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ = ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক দুই পাশে [math]\tan[/math] নিলে [math]\tan(2\arctan x)=\dfrac{2\tan(\arctan x)}{1-\tan^2(\arctan x)}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই [math]2\arctan x=\arctan\frac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math] (প্রয়োগযোগ্য প্রধান মান ধরে)। 

খ [math]P(x)=2\arctan x[/math] হলে [math]\cos P(x)=\cos(2\arctan x)=\dfrac{1-x^2}{1+x^2}[/math]; এখানে [math]x^2=\dfrac{a-b}{a+b}\tan^2\frac{\theta}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\tan^2\frac{\theta}{2}=\dfrac{1-\cos\theta}{1+\cos\theta}[/math] বসালে সরলীকরণ করে [math]\cos P(x)=\dfrac{b+a\cos\theta}{a+b\cos\theta}[/math], সুতরাং [math]P(x)=\arccos\frac{b+a\cos\theta}{a+b\cos\theta}[/math] (প্রধান শাখা ধরে)। 

গ বামে [math]\tan(P(x))=\tan(2\arctan x)=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math] আর ডানে [math]2\tan(\arctan x+\arctan x^3)=2\cdot\dfrac{x+x^3}{1-x^4}=2x\cdot\dfrac{1+x^2}{(1-x^2)(1+x^2)}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math], তাই একই রকম।

Question #94 (ID: 442466)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]\tan ^{-1}(x+p)+\tan ^{-1}(x-p)=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২:


[Image]


Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{27}{11}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{P}=\frac{1}{3}[/math] হলে x এর মান নির্ণয় কর ।
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]\mathrm{AC}+\mathrm{BC}=\sqrt{2} \mathrm{AB}(-\pi<\theta<\pi)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [চিত্র হতে][math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{3}{5}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{3}{5}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{15+12}{20}}{\frac{20-9}{20}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{27}{11}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ 11[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{27}{11}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\tan ^{-1}(x+p)+\tan ^{-1}(x-p)=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(x+\frac{1}{3}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(x-\frac{1}{3}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\left[\because p=\frac{1}{3}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+\frac{1}{3}+x-\frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(x+\frac{1}{3}\right)\left(x-\frac{1}{3}\right)}=\tan ^{-1} 2\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-\left(x^{2}-\frac{1}{9}\right)}=\tan ^{-1} 2\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{\frac{10}{9}-x^{2}}=\tan ^{-1} 2\\=\frac{2 x}{\frac{10}{9}-x^{2}}=2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 2 x=\frac{20}{9}-2 x^{2}\\= 2 x^{2}+2 x-\frac{20}{9}=0\\= x^{2}+x-\frac{10}{9}=0\\=9 x^{2}+9 x-10=0 \text{.}\\= 9 x^{2}+15 x-6 x-10=0\\= 3 x(3 x+5)-2(3 x+5)=0\\= (3 x+5)(3 x-2)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]3 x+5=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 3 x=-5 \\\therefore \quad x=-\frac{5}{3}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]3 x-2=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=3 x=2\\\therefore \quad x=\frac{2}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় মান, [math]x=-\frac{5}{3}, \frac{2}{3}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গ [math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{AC}+\mathrm{BC}=\sqrt{2} \mathrm{AB} \\= \frac{\mathrm{AC}}{\mathrm{AB}}+\frac{\mathrm{BC}}{\mathrm{AB}}=\sqrt{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= \quad \sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [চিত্র হতে]
[Image]
[math]= \sin \theta \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\cos \theta \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=1[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin \theta \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos \theta \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1\\= \sin \left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1\\= \theta+\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}\\= \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\\= \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\\\therefore \quad \theta=(8 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
যেখানে, n এর মান শূন্য বা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।এখন, [math]n=0, \pm 1, \pm 2, \ldots \ldots[/math]
 বসিয়ে,[math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n = -1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{7 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মান: [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}\frac{4}{5}[/math] এবং [math]\beta=\cot^{-1}\frac{5}{3}[/math], তখন [math]\tan\alpha=\frac{3}{4}[/math] এবং [math]\tan\beta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]; তাই
[math]\tan(\alpha+\beta)=\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{3}{5}}{1-\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{3}{5}}=\frac{27}{11}[/math],
অতএব [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{4}{5}+\cot^{-1}\frac{5}{3}=\tan^{-1}\frac{27}{11}[/math]।

খ সাধারণভাবে [math]\tan^{-1}(x+p)+\tan^{-1}(x-p)=\tan^{-1}2[/math] হলে
[math]\frac{2x}{1-x^2+p^2}=2[/math] পাওয়া যায়, অর্থাৎ [math]x^2+x-(1+p^2)=0[/math]।
[math]p=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] দিলে [math]x^2+x-\tfrac{10}{9}=0[/math] বা [math]9x^2+9x-10=0[/math], ফলাফল [math]x=\tfrac{2}{3}[/math] অথবা [math]x=-\tfrac{5}{3}[/math]।

গ A ও B কে যথাক্রমে [math](1,0)[/math] ও [math](-1,0)[/math] ধরে ধরে এবং C কে [math](\cos\theta,\sin\theta)[/math] ধরলে
[math]AC=2\sin\frac{\theta}{2},\;BC=2\cos\frac{\theta}{2}[/math] হয়; শর্ত অনুযায়ী
[math]2\big(\sin\frac{\theta}{2}+\cos\frac{\theta}{2}\big)=\sqrt{2}\cdot 2[/math], ফলে [math]\sin\frac{\theta}{2}+\cos\frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2}[/math] যা দেয় [math]\frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] এবং সুতরাং [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] (প্রদত্ত সীমায়)।

Question #95 (ID: 442467)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]tan^{-1}(-√3)[/math] এর মূখ্যমান নির্ণয় কর ।
খ. [math]4\left[\{f(x)\}^{2}+\sqrt{1-\{f(x)\}^{2}}\right]=5 ;(-2 \pi
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(x))+\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-f(2(x))^{2}}}}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan ^{-1}(-\sqrt{3})=\tan ^{-1}\left\{\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)\right\}=-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan ^{-1}(-\sqrt{3})[/math]
 এর মুখ্যমান [math]-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x) = sinx[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে, [math]\left.4\left[\{f(x)\}^{2}+\sqrt{1-\{f(x)}\right\}^{2}\right]=5[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=4\left(\sin ^{2} x+\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} x_{e}}\right)=5\\= 4\left(\sin ^{2} x+\cos x\right)=5\\= 4 \sin ^{2} x+4 \cos x-5=0\\= 4\left(1-\cos ^{2} x\right)+4 \cos x-5=0\\= 4 \cos ^{2} x-4 \cos x+5-4=0\\=4 \cos ^{2} x-4 \cos x+1=0\\= (2 \cos x-1)^{2}=0\\=2 \cos x-1=0\\= \cos x=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]= x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} \text {; }[/math]
 যখন n এর মান শূন্যবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]n = 0[/math]
, হলে, [math]x= \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
, হলে, [math]x=\frac{5 \pi}{3}, \frac{7 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]n=-1,[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=-\frac{5 \pi}{3},-\frac{7 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধিতে x এর মানসমূহ:[math]\pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \pm \frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x) = sinx[/math]
বামপক্ষ[math]=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(x))+\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-(f(2 x))^{2}}}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin x)+\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{1 \cdot-\sin ^{2} 2 x}}} \\=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin x)+\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1}{\cos 2 x}} \\=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin x)+\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 x})\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin x \sqrt{1-\cos 2 x}[/math]
 [math]\left.+\sqrt{\cos 2 x} \cdot \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} x}\right\}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin x \times \sqrt{2} \sin x[/math]
 [math]+\sqrt{\cos 2 x} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 x}\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} x+\cos 2 x\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} x+1-2 \sin ^{2} x\right\}=\sin ^{-1} 1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\tan^{-1}(-\sqrt{3})[/math] এর মূলমান হল [math]-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math], কারণ [math]\tan\left(-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=-\sqrt{3}[/math] এবং আরকট্যাংজেন্টের প্রধান শৃঙ্খল হল [math]\left(-\frac{\pi}{2},\frac{\pi}{2}\right)[/math]।

খ সমীকরণকে লিখি: [math]4\big[\sin^2 x+\sqrt{1-\sin^2 x}\big]=5[/math], যেখানে [math]\sqrt{1-\sin^2 x}=|\cos x|[/math] হলে [math]1-|\cos x|^2+|\cos x|=\tfrac{5}{4}[/math] বা [math]|\cos x|=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]. তাই [math]\cos x=\pm\frac{1}{2}[/math], সাধারণ সূত্রে [math]x=2k\pi\pm\frac{\pi}{3}[/math] বা [math]x=2k\pi\pm\frac{2\pi}{3}[/math] (সকল পূর্ণসংখ্যা [math]k[/math])।

গ লক্ষ্য করুন [math]\csc^{-1}y=\sin^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{1}{y}\big)[/math] (প্রধান শর্তে)। এখানে [math]\sqrt{1-f(2x)^2}=\sqrt{1-\sin^2 2x}=| \cos 2x|[/math], ফলে অভ্যন্তরীণটি হচ্ছে [math]\;s=\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{|\cos 2x|}}\;(=1/\sqrt{\cos 2x}\ \text{যদি }\cos2x\ge0)[/math]. তখন [math]\csc^{-1}s=\sin^{-1}\!\big(\sqrt{\cos 2x}\,\big)[/math] এবং [math]\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin x)+\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2x})=\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin x)+\cos^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin x)=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math], কেবলমাত্র যেখানে ডোমেইন শর্ত পূরণ হয় (যেমন [math]|\sin x|\le1/\sqrt{2}[/math] এবং [math]\cos2x\ge0[/math])।

Question #96 (ID: 442465)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]\sec 2 P=\frac{1+b^{2}}{d-b^{2}}, \operatorname{cosec} 2 Q=\frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২: [math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]P+Q=\pi-\tan ^{-1} a[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]a+b+c=a b c[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}^{3} \mathrm{~g}(3 \mathrm{x})+\{\mathrm{g}(\mathrm{x})\}^{3} \mathrm{f}(3 \mathrm{x})=\frac{3}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]=\cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [চিত্র-১]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\cot \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\=\cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\frac{3}{4}(\text { Ans. })\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec 2 P=\frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}} \\= 2 P=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}} \\\therefore P=\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]\operatorname{cosec} 2 Q=\frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}[/math]
এবং [math]2 Q=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}[/math]
 [math]\therefore Q=\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে, [math]P+Q=\pi-\tan ^{-1} a[/math]
 [math]= \frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} a+\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}=\pi[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} a+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 c}{1+c^{2}}=\pi[/math]
 [math]= \tan ^{-1} a+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} b+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} c=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{~b}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{c}=\pi\\= \tan ^{-1} \frac{a+b+c-a b c}{1-b c-c a-a b}=\pi\\= \frac{a+b+c-a b c}{1-b c-c a-a b}=\tan \pi\\= \frac{a+b+c-a b c}{1-b c-c a-c b}=0\\= a+b+c-a b c=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad a+b+c=a b c[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\{f(x)\}^{3} g(3 x)+\{g(x)\}^{3} f(3 x)=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= \sin ^{3} x \cdot \cos 3 x+\cos ^{3} x \sin 3 x=\frac{3}{4}\\= \cos ^{3} x \sin 3 x+\sin ^{3} x \cos 3 x=\frac{3}{4}\\= 4 \cos ^{3} x \sin 3 x+4 \sin ^{3} x \cos 3 x=3\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=(\cos 3 x+3 \cos x) \sin 3 x+[/math]
 [math](3 \sin x-\sin 3 x) \cos 3 x=3[/math]
 [math]= \sin 3 x \cos 3 x+3 \sin 3 x \cos x+3 \cos 3 x \sin x[/math]
 [math]-\sin 3 x \cos 3 x=3[/math]
 [math]= 3(\sin 3 x \cos x+\cos 3 x \sin x)=3[/math]
 [math]3 \sin (3 x+x)=3[/math]
 বা, [math]\sin 4 x=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}= 4 x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8},\end{array}[/math]
যখন n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক সমাধান: [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{4}=\theta[/math] ধরলে [math]\sin\theta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]; তাই [math]\cos^{-1}(\sin\theta)=\phi[/math] যেখানে [math]\cos\phi=\frac{3}{5}[/math] ও [math]\sin\phi=\frac{4}{5}[/math]; ফলে [math]\cot\phi=\frac{3/5}{4/5}=\frac{3}{4}[/math].

খ সমাধান: প্রদত্ত [math]\sec2P=\frac{1+b^2}{1-b^2}[/math] ও [math]\cosec2Q=\frac{1+c^2}{2c}[/math] থেকে [math]\tan P=b,\,\tan Q=c[/math] ধরা যায়; এরপর [math]P+Q=\pi-\tan^{-1}a[/math] হলে [math]\tan(P+Q)=-a[/math] এবং [math]\tan(P+Q)=\frac{b+c}{1-bc}[/math]; তাই [math]\frac{b+c}{1-bc}=-a\Rightarrow a+b+c=abc[/math]।

গ সমাধান: [math]\sin^3x\cos3x+\cos^3x\sin3x[/math] এ ট্রিপল-এঙ্গেল ব্যবহার করলে সরলীকরণে [math]\frac{3}{4}\sin4x[/math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই [math]\frac{3}{4}\sin4x=\frac{3}{4}\Rightarrow \sin4x=1[/math] এবং সমাধান: [math]x=\frac{\pi}{8}+\frac{k\pi}{2},\ k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #97 (ID: 442463)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]A=\cos \theta, B=\sin \theta, C=\cos 2 \theta, D=\sin 2 \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. মান নির্ণয় কর : [math]\tan ^{-1} \sin \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]
খ. [math]A+\sqrt{3} B=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর ।
গ. [math]A + B = C + D[/math] হলে, সমীকরণটির [math]\left[0, \frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math] ব্যবধিতে সমাধান আছে কি-না যাচাই কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]=\tan ^{-1} \sin \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\frac{\pi}{6} .[/math]

[Image]
খএখানে, [math]\mathrm{A}=\cos \theta, \mathrm{B}=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}A+\sqrt{3} B=\sqrt{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \text{.}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{12}\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]{\theta}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
 গএখানে, [math]A=\cos \theta, B=\sin \theta, C=\cos 2 \theta, D=\sin 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B}=\mathrm{C}+\mathrm{D}\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta+\sin \theta=\cos 2 \theta+\sin 2 \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\cos 2 \theta-(\sin 2 \theta-\sin \theta)=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}-2 \cos \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2}\left(\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}-\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}\right)=0\\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\theta}{2}\left(\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}-\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}\right)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\sin \frac{\theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
অথবা [math]\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}-\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}=\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}}{\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}}=1 \Rightarrow \tan \frac{3 \theta}{2}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{3 \theta}{2}=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{2 n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}\Rightarrow \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে, [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=0[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=2 \pi[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-2 \pi[/math]
 [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে, [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{6} ; n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\left[0, \frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta=0, \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]\theta=0, \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 সমীকরণটির [math]\left[0, \frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]
 ব্যবধিতে সমাধান আছে।

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\cos^{-1}\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}[/math], তাহলে [math]\cos\theta=\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}[/math] ও [math]\sin\theta=\sqrt{1-\tfrac{2}{3}}=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math], সুতরাং [math]\tan^{-1}(\sin(\cos^{-1}\sqrt{2/3}))=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=[/math][math]\tfrac{\pi}{6}[/math]।

খ [math]\cos\theta+\sqrt{3}\sin\theta=2\cos(\theta-\pi/3)[/math] (কারণ [math]R=\sqrt{1+3}=2[/math] ও [math]\phi=\pi/3[/math]), তাই [math]2\cos(\theta-\pi/3)=\sqrt{2}\Rightarrow\cos(\theta-\pi/3)=\tfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math], ফলে [math]\theta-\pi/3=\pm\pi/4+2k\pi[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\pi/12+2k\pi\ বা \theta=7\pi/12+2k\pi[/math]।

গ ব্যবহার করুন [math]\cos\theta+\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}\cos(\theta-\pi/4)[/math] ও [math]\cos2\theta+\sin2\theta=\sqrt{2}\cos(2\theta-\pi/4)[/math]; সমীকরণে সুচেতন হলে [math]\cos(\theta-\pi/4)=\cos(2\theta-\pi/4)[/math], যা থেকে [math]\theta=0[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\pi/6[/math] মিলেযায়; অতএব [math][0,\tfrac{\pi}{2}][/math] খন্ডে সমাধান আছে (উপরোক্ত দুটো মান)।

Question #98 (ID: 442462)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board

Question:
[math]g(x)=p \sin ^{-1} x ; h(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]g(x)[/math] এর লেখচিত্র অঙ্কন কর, যখন [math]\mathrm{p}=\frac{1}{2},-1 \leq \mathrm{x} \leq 1[/math]
গ. [math]2\{\mathrm{~h}(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}+\{\mathrm{h}(2 \mathrm{x})\}^{2}=2[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ, সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ৷


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\end{array}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{2}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\frac{3}{4}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)খএখানে, [math]g(x)=p \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad g(x)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \mathrm{x}[/math]
 যখন [math]p=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
ধরি, [math]y=g(x)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]-1 \leq x \leq 1[/math]
 সীমার মধ্যে x এর বিভিন্ন মানের জন্য y এর সংশ্লিষ্ট মান নির্ণয় করি :[math]\begin{array}{|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|}\hline \mathrm{x} & -1 & -\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} & -\frac{1}{2} & 0 & \frac{1}{2} & \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} & 1 \\\hline \mathrm{y} & -\frac{\pi}{4} & -\frac{\pi}{6} & -\frac{\pi}{12} & 0 & \frac{\pi}{12} & \frac{\pi}{6} & \frac{\pi}{4} \\\hline\end{array}[/math]
ছক কাগজের X-অক্ষ বরাবর ক্ষুদ্রতম [math]10[/math]
 বর্গের দৈর্ঘ্য [math]= 1[/math]
 একক এবং Y- অক্ষ বরাবর ক্ষুদ্রতম 1 বর্গের বাহুর দৈর্ঘ্য [math]=\frac{\pi}{60}[/math]
 ধরে প্রাপ্ত বিন্দুগুলো স্থাপন 60 করি। স্থাপিত বিন্দুগুলি যোগ করে লেখচিত্র অঙ্কন করা হলো :
[Image]
গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}2\{\mathrm{~h}(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}+\{\mathrm{h}(2 \mathrm{x})\}^{2}=2\\\Rightarrow 2(\cos x)^{2}+(\cos 2 x)^{2}=2\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x+\cos ^{2} 2 x=2\\\Rightarrow 1+\cos 2 x+\cos ^{2} 2 x=2\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} 2 x+\cos 2 x-1=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1^{2}-4 \cdot 1 \cdot(-1)}}{2 \cdot 1}=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1+4}}{2}=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{5}}{2}\end{array}[/math]
যেহেতু [math]\cos 2 x=\frac{-1-\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
 হতে পারে না।সেহেতু [math]\cos 2 x=\frac{-1+\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
এখন, [math]\cos 2 x=\cos \alpha[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\cos \alpha=\frac{-1+\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad 2 \mathrm{x}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=n \pi \pm \frac{\alpha}{2}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{Z}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]x=n \pi \pm \frac{\alpha}{2}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]n \in Z[/math]
 এবং [math]\alpha=\cos ^{-1} \frac{-1+\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরি [math]A=\sec^{-1}\frac{\sqrt5}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\cos A=\frac{2}{\sqrt5},\ \sin A=\frac{1}{\sqrt5}[/math] তাই [math]\tan A=\frac{1}{2}[/math]; এবং [math]B=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\tan B=\frac{1}{2}[/math]. ফলে
[math]\tan(A+B)=\frac{\tan A+\tan B}{1-\tan A\tan B}=\frac{1/2+1/2}{1-1/4}=\frac{4}{3}[/math],
এবং [math]\cot^{-1}\frac{3}{4}[/math] এর কোণটির ট্যানে [math]4/3[/math] হওয়ায় [math]A+B=\cot^{-1}\frac{3}{4}[/math] (প্রধান শাখা মিলায়) — প্রদর্শিত।

খ [math]g(x)=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}x[/math] হলে ডোমেইন [math][-1,1][/math] এবং রেঞ্জ [math]\left[-\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{\pi}{4}\right][/math]; গাণিতিকভাবে এটি অমনুন্নত এবং বিজোড় (odd) ফাংশন, [math]g(-1)=-\tfrac{\pi}{4},\ g(0)=0,\ g(1)=\tfrac{\pi}{4}[/math], ত্বরণ [math]g'(x)=\tfrac{1}{2}/\sqrt{1-x^2}[/math] তাই ক্রমান্বয়ে বৃদ্ধি পায় এবং [math]x0[/math]-এ অববক্র; এসব বৈশিষ্ট্য দিয়ে লেখচিত্র অংকন করবে।

গ [math]2\cos^2x+\cos^2(2x)=2[/math] লিখে ট্রিগনোমেট্রিক পরিচয় প্রয়োগ করলে পাবো
[math]2\cos^2x+\cos^2(2x)=2\ \Rightarrow\ 2\cos2x+\cos4x=1[/math].
[math]\cos4x=2\cos^2 2x-1[/math] ব্যবহার করলে
[math]2\cos^2 2x+2\cos2x-2=0\Rightarrow \cos^2 2x+\cos2x-1=0.[math]
[math]y=\cos2x[/math] ধরলে [math]y^2+y-1=0[/math] থেকে [math]y=\dfrac{-1\pm\sqrt5}{2}[/math]; [math]y=\dfrac{\sqrt5-1}{2}[/math] গ্রহণযোগ্য (অপরটি মানসংগত নয়). তাই সাধারণ সমাধান:
[math]x=\pm\frac{1}{2}\cos^{-1}\!\left(\frac{\sqrt5-1}{2}\right)+k\pi,\quad k\in\mathbb{Z}.[/math]

Question #99 (ID: 442461)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board

Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]\cos \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}\right)=\frac{x^{2}-y^{2}}{x^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
খ. উদ্দীপকে [math]A+P=\varphi[/math] হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, \ [math]x^{2}-2 x y \cos \varphi+y^{2}=r^{2} \sin ^{2} \varphi[/math]
গ. [math]f(\theta)=\frac{\mathrm{r}}{\mathrm{x}}[/math] হলে, [math]-\pi \leq \theta \leq \pi[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]f(2 \theta)-f(\theta)=2[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\cos \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{y}}{\mathrm{x}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{x^{2}-y^{2}}{x^{2}+y^{2}}=\frac{x^{2}-y^{2}}{x^{2}+y}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \cos \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}\right)=\frac{x^{2}-y^{2}}{x^{2}+y} .[/math]
(দেখানো হলো )খ
[Image]
এখানে, [math]ABC[/math]
 সমকোণী ত্রিভুজে[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos A \frac{x}{r}\\\Rightarrow A=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{r}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]PQR[/math]
 সমকোণী ত্রিভুজে[math]\cos p=\frac{y}{r}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow p=\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{r}[/math]
 [math]\Lambda+P=\varphi[/math]
 হলে [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{r}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{r}=\varphi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{r} \cdot \frac{y}{r}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\varphi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{r^{2}}-\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}}=\cos \varphi\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{r^{2}}-\cos \varphi=\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}}\\\Rightarrow \left(\frac{x y}{r^{2}}-\cos \varphi\right)^{2}=1-\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}-2 \cdot \frac{x y}{r^{2}} \cdot \cos \varphi+\cos ^{2} \varphi=1-\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}-\frac{2 x y}{r^{2}} \cos \varphi+\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{r^{4}}=1-\cos ^{2} \varphi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{r^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{x^{2}} \cos \varphi+\frac{y^{2}}{r^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \varphi\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x^{2}-2 x y \cos \varphi+y^{2}=r^{2} \sin ^{2} \varphi .[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)গ [math]f(\theta)=\frac{r}{x}[/math]
 হলে [math]f(\theta)=\sec \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(2 \theta)=\sec 2 \theta[/math]
এখন, [math]f(2 \theta)-f(\theta)=2[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sec 2 \theta-\sec \theta=2\\\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\cos 2 \theta}-\frac{1}{\cos \theta}=2\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta-\cos 2 \theta}{\cos 2 \theta \cos \theta}=2\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\cos 2 \theta=2 \cos 2 \theta \cos \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\cos 2 \theta=\cos 3 \theta+\cos \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} 3 \theta+\cos \theta-\cos \theta+\cos 2 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow\cos 3 \theta+\cos 2 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow2 \cos \frac{5 \theta}{2} \cos \frac{\theta}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow\cos \frac{5 \theta}{2} \cos \frac{\theta}{2}=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\cos \frac{5 \theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{5 \theta}{2}=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \text {; }[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]n \in \mathbf{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
অথবা [math]\cos \frac{\theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \text {; }[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]
 [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে Z,[math]n=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\frac{3 \pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]n=2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\pi[/math]
 [math]n=-2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{3 \pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=3[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{7 \pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]n=-3[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\pi[/math]
 [math]n=4[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\frac{9 \pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]n=-4[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=-\frac{7 \pi}{5}[/math]
 [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে. [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\pi[/math]
 [math]\mathbf{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=3 \pi[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=-\pi[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=5 \pi[/math]
 [math]n=-2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=-3 \pi[/math]
 [math]-\pi \leq \theta \leq \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta=-\pi,-\frac{3 \pi}{5},-\frac{\pi}{5}, \frac{\pi}{5}, \frac{3 \pi}{5}, \pi[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]: \theta=-\pi,-\frac{3 \pi}{5},-\frac{\pi}{5}, \frac{\pi}{5}, \frac{3 \pi}{5}, \pi .[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরো [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\frac{y}{x}[/math], তখন [math]\tan\theta=\frac{y}{x}[/math] এবং [math]\cos2\theta=\frac{1-\tan^2\theta}{1+\tan^2\theta}=\frac{1-(y/x)^2}{1+(y/x)^2}=\frac{x^2-y^2}{x^2+y^2}[/math]। অতএব প্রদর্শিত সত্যি।

খ উদ্দীপকের চিত্র অনুযায়ী ধরে নাও [math]x=r\cos A[/math] এবং [math]y=r\cos P[/math] এবং [math]\varphi=A+P[/math]। তাহলে বামদিককে [math]/r^2[/math] দ্বারা ভাগ করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]\cos^2A-2\cos A\cos P\cos(A+P)+\cos^2P[/math] যা সরলীকরণ করে পরিচিত ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণ দিয়ে [math]\sin^2(A+P)=\sin^2\varphi[/math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই [math]x^{2}-2xy\cos\varphi+y^{2}=r^{2}\sin^{2}\varphi[/math]।

গ যেহেতু [math]x=r\cos\theta[/math], তাই [math]f(\theta)=\dfrac{r}{x}=\sec\theta[/math]. সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\sec2\theta-\sec\theta=2[/math]; [math]u=\sec\theta[/math] ধরলে [math]\dfrac{u^{2}}{2-u^{2}}-u=2[/math], যা সমাধান করলে [math]u=-1,\;u=-1\pm\sqrt{5}[/math] পাওয়া যায়. সুতরাং [math]\sec\theta=-1[/math] থেকে [math]\theta=\pm\pi[/math], [math]\sec\theta=-1+\sqrt5[/math] এবং [math]\sec\theta=-1-\sqrt5[/math] থেকে অনুক্রমে [math]\theta=\pm\arccos\frac{1}{-1+\sqrt5}[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\pm\arccos\frac{1}{-1-\sqrt5}[/math] (সবগুলো [math]-\pi\le\theta\le\pi[/math] এ গ্রহণযোগ্য)।

Question #100 (ID: 442459)

Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x, g(x)=\cos x, \sin \theta=\frac{4}{5}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{10}}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর। ..
খ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\frac{1}{2} \theta-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে সমাধান কর : [math]\sqrt{3} g(x)+f(x)=\sqrt{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{10}}{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3+2}{6}}{\frac{6-1}{6}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{5}{6}}{\frac{5}{6}}=\tan ^{-1} 1=\tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{10}}{3}=\frac{\pi}{4} .[/math]
[বি.দ্র. প্রশ্নে [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
 এর পরিবর্তে [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{10}}{3}[/math]
 হবে]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{10}}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\sin \theta=\frac{4}{5}[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan \frac{1}{2} \theta=\frac{\sin \frac{1}{2} \theta}{\cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{2 \sin \frac{1}{2} \theta \cdot \cos \frac{1}{2} \theta}{2 \cos ^{2} \frac{1}{2} \theta} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta}{1+\cos \theta} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta}{1+\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} \theta}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\frac{4}{5}}{1+\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{4}{5}\right)^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\frac{4}{5}}{1+\sqrt{1-\frac{16}{25}}}=\frac{\frac{4}{5}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{9}{25}}}=\frac{\frac{4}{5}}{1+\frac{3}{5}}=\frac{\frac{4}{5}}{\frac{8}{5}}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \frac{1}{2} \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]

[Image]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\frac{1}{2} \theta-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\frac{1}{2} \theta-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
এখন, [math]\sqrt{3} g(x)+f(x)+\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \cos x+\sin x=\sqrt{3}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos x+\frac{1}{2} \sin x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}+\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi, 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]x=2 n \pi, 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]

Question #101 (ID: 442460)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\cot ^{-1} y-\tan ^{-1} x.......[/math](i)

[math]\cos \theta-\cos 9 \theta=\sin 5 \theta......[/math](ii)

Options:
ক. [math]\sin \frac{x}{3}[/math] এর পর্যায়কাল কত ?
খ. [math]f(x)=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math] হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]x+y+\sqrt{3} x y=\sqrt{3}[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপক-২ এর সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কধরি, [math]g(x)=\sin \frac{x}{3}[/math]
এখন, [math]\sin \frac{x}{3}=\sin \left(2 \pi+\frac{x}{3}\right)=\sin \left\{\frac{1}{3}(6 \pi+x)\right\}=g(6 \pi+x)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad g(x)=g(6 \pi+x)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \sin \frac{x}{3}[/math]
 এর পর্যায় [math]6 \pi[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cot ^{-1} y-\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]f(x)=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 হলে [math]\cot ^{-1} y-\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{y}-\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{y}-x}{1+\frac{1}{y} \cdot x}=\frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1-x y}{y}}{\frac{y+x}{y}}=\frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1-x y}{x+y}=\frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1-x y}{x+y}=\tan \frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1-x y}{x+y}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\\\Rightarrow x+y=\sqrt{3}-\sqrt{3} x y\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x+y+\sqrt{3} x y=\sqrt{3} \text {. }[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]\cos \theta-\cos 9 \theta=\sin 5 \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin \left(\frac{\theta+9 \theta}{2}\right) \sin \left(\frac{9 \theta-\theta}{2}\right)=\sin 5 \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin 5 \theta \cdot \sin 4 \theta=\sin 5 \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin 5 \theta \cdot \sin 4 \theta-\sin 5 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \sin 5 \theta(2 \sin 4 \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় , [math]\sin 5 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 5 \theta=n \pi[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]n \in Z[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{n \pi}{5}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \sin 4 \theta-1=0 .[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin 4 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow\sin 4 \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow\sin 4 \theta=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbf{z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{n \pi}{4}+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{24}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]\theta=\frac{n \pi}{5}, \frac{n \pi}{4}+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{24}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{z}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\sin\frac{x}{3}[/math] হল [math]\sin(kx)[/math] ধরনের যেখানে [math]k=\frac{1}{3}[/math]; তাই পর্যায়কাল [math]T=\frac{2\pi}{|k|}=6\pi[/math]।

খ ধরে নিন [math]\alpha=\cot^{-1}y,\ \beta=\tan^{-1}x[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\alpha=\frac{1}{y},\ \tan\beta=x[/math] এবং [math]\alpha-\beta=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]; তাই [math]\tan(\alpha-\beta)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{\tan\alpha-\tan\beta}{1+\tan\alpha\tan\beta}=\frac{\frac{1}{y}-x}{1+\frac{x}{y}}=\frac{1-xy}{x+y}[/math], থেকে ক্রস-মাল্টিপ্লাই করে পাওয়া যায় [math]x+y+\sqrt{3}\,xy=\sqrt{3}[/math]।

গ ব্যবহার করুন [math]\cos A-\cos B=-2\sin\frac{A+B}{2}\sin\frac{A-B}{2}[/math], ফলে [math]\cos\theta-\cos9\theta=2\sin5\theta\sin4\theta[/math] এবং সমীকরণটি হচ্ছে [math] \sin5\theta(2\sin4\theta-1)=0[/math]. অতএব সাধারণ সমাধানগুলো: (i) [math]\sin5\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{n\pi}{5},\ n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]; (ii) [math]\sin4\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\frac{\pi}{24}+k\frac{\pi}{2}\ \text{অথবা}\ \theta=\frac{5\pi}{24}+k\frac{\pi}{2},\ k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #102 (ID: 442458)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]\cot \theta-\tan \theta=\frac{6}{5}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]2 \sin 2 \theta+2(\sin \theta+\cos \theta)+1=0[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1}(\cot 3 x)+\tan ^{-1}(-\cot 5 x)=2 x[/math] দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ বর্ণিত সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1}(\cot 3 x)+\tan ^{-1}(-\cot 5 x)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\cot 3 x-\cot 5 x}{1+\cot 3 x \cot 5 x} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{\tan 3 x}-\frac{1}{\tan 5 x}}{1+\frac{1}{\tan 3 x} \cdot \frac{1}{\tan 5 x}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{\tan 5 x-\tan 3 x}{\tan 3 x \cdot \tan 5 x}}{\frac{\tan 3 x \cdot \tan 5 x+1}{\tan 3 x \cdot \tan 5 x}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\tan 5 x-\tan 3 x}{1+\tan 3 x \cdot \tan 5 x} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}(5 x-3 x) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \tan 2 x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=2 x=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \tan ^{-1}(\cot 3 x)+\tan ^{-1}(-\cot 5 x)=2 x .[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot \theta-\tan \theta=\frac{6}{5}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}-\frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}=\frac{6}{5}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta}{\sin \theta \cos \theta}=\frac{6}{5}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta}{2 \sin \theta \cdot \cos \theta}=\frac{3}{5}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos 2 \theta}{\sin 2 \theta}=\frac{3}{5}\\\Rightarrow \cot 2 \theta=\frac{3}{5}\\\Rightarrow 2 \cdot \theta=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \theta=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}} \text {. }[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)
[Image]
গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ বর্ণিত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \sin 2 \theta+2(\sin \theta+\cos \theta)+1=0\\\Rightarrow 2.2 \sin \theta \cos \theta+2 \sin \theta+2 \cos \theta+1=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin \theta(2 \cos \theta+1)+1(2 \cos \theta+1)=0\\\Rightarrow (2 \cos \theta+1)(2 \sin \theta+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]2 \cos \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
আবার, [math]2 \sin \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin \theta=-1\\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=-\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)\\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{z}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]\theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{z} \text {. }[/math]


Explanation:
ক কারণ [math]\cot 3x=\tan\!\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3x\right)[/math] এবং [math]-\cot 5x=\tan\!\left(5x-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)[/math], সুতরাং [math]\tan^{-1}(\cot 3x)+\tan^{-1}(-\cot 5x)=\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3x\right)+\left(5x-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=2x[/math] (প্রধান মানের মধ্যে থাকলে)।

খ শুরুতে [math]\cot\theta-\tan\theta=\dfrac{\cos^2\theta-\sin^2\theta}{\sin\theta\cos\theta}=\dfrac{\cos2\theta}{\tfrac{1}{2}\sin2\theta}=2\cot2\theta[/math], অতএব [math]2\cot2\theta=\dfrac{6}{5}\Rightarrow\cot2\theta=\dfrac{3}{5}\Rightarrow\tan2\theta=\dfrac{5}{3}[/math], ফলে [math]\sin2\theta=\dfrac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\dfrac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]।

গ [math]2\sin2\theta+2(\sin\theta+\cos\theta)+1=0[/math] থেকে [math]4\sin\theta\cos\theta+2\sin\theta+2\cos\theta+1=(2\sin\theta+1)(2\cos\theta+1)=0[/math], সুতরাং [math]\sin\theta=-\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] বা [math]\cos\theta=-\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]; সাধারণ সমাধান: [math]\theta= \tfrac{7\pi}{6}+2k\pi,\ \tfrac{11\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+2k\pi,\ \tfrac{4\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math] (যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math])।

Question #103 (ID: 442457)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cot ^{-1} \cos \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{2}}[/math] এর মুখ্যমান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. উদ্দীপকে উল্লেখিত [math]f(x)[/math] এর জন্য [math]f^{-1}(x)+f^{-1}(y)=\pi[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, প্রাপ্ত সজ্ঞারপথটি একটি সরলরেখা নির্দেশ করে যার ঢাল -1 হবে ।
গ. [math]\{(x)\}^{2}+f^{\prime}(x)=3 f(x)[/math] হলে বিশেষ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর যখন [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=\cot ^{-1} \cos \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} \cos \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\cot ^{-1} \cot \frac{\pi}{3} e \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\pi\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\pi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\tan \pi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=0\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মূখ্য মান [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]

[Image]
খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 এবং [math]f^{-1}(y)=\tan ^{-1} y[/math]
এখানে, [math]f^{-1}(x)+f^{-1}(y)=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\pi\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\pi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\tan \pi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x+y=0[/math]
 যা একটি সরলরেখার সমীকরনসরলরেখাটির ঢাল [math]=-\frac{1}{1}=-1[/math]
প্রাপ্ত সঞ্চারপথটি একটি সরলরেখা নির্দেশ করে যার ঢাল [math]– 1[/math]
. (প্রমাণিত)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad f(x)=\sec ^{2} x[/math]
এখানে, [math]\{f(\mathbf{x})\}^{2}+f^{\prime}(\mathbf{x})=3 f(\mathbf{x})[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow (\tan x)^{2}+\sec ^{2} x=3 \tan x\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x+\sec ^{2} x=3 \tan x\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x+1+\tan ^{2} x=3 \tan x\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x+1=3 \tan x\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x-3 \tan x+1=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x-2 \tan x-\tan x+1=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x(\tan x-1)-1(\tan x-1)=0\\\Rightarrow (\tan x-1)(2 \tan x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\tan x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan x=1\\\Rightarrow \tan x=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \quad x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{Z}[/math]
 [math]x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে[math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
অথবা [math]2 \tan x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan x=1\\\Rightarrow \tan x=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=n \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{Z}[/math]
 [math]x=n \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে[math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]n=[/math]
 হলে [math]x=2 \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 সীমার মধ্যে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}, \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]x=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}, \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক ধরি [math]\theta=\csc^{-1}\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{2}}[/math], তাহলে [math]\sin\theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3/2}}=\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}[/math] এবং ধারাবাহিকভাবে ধনাত্মক কোণ হওয়ায় [math]\cos\theta=\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{3}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]; ফলে [math]\cot^{-1}(\cos\theta)=\cot^{-1}\!\big(\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\big)[/math] এবং এটি [math]\pi/3[/math]।

খ যদি [math]\tan^{-1}x+\tan^{-1}y=\pi[/math] হয়, তাহলে উভয় পাশে [math]\tan[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে [math]\tan(\tan^{-1}x+\tan^{-1}y)=\tan\pi=0[/math] এবং যোগসূত্র দিয়ে [math]\dfrac{x+y}{1-xy}=0\Rightarrow x+y=0[/math] (যেখানে অর্ন্তগত হার অসূন্য), তাই লুকসটি সরলরেখা [math]y=-x[/math] যার ঢাল [math]-1[/math]।

গ এখানে [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math] হলে [math]f'(x)=\sec^2x=1+\tan^2x[/math]; দিয়া দেওয়া সমীকরণে বসালে [math]\tan^2x+(1+\tan^2x)=3\tan x[/math] বা [math]2t^2-3t+1=0[/math] (যেখানে [math]t=\tan x[/math]) ফলে [math]t=1[/math] বা [math]t=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]; তাই 0≤x≤2π ক্ষেত্রে সমাধানগুলো হল [math]x=\pi/4,\,5\pi/4,\,\arctan(1/2),\,\pi+\arctan(1/2)[/math]।

Question #104 (ID: 442456)

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{11}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{16}{65}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2+\cos \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math] খ, দেখাও যে, দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর মান [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এর সমাধান কর যখন [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কধরি, [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan \theta=x[/math]
এখন [math]\sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} x}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 x^{c}}{1+x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \text {. }[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো )খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর রাশি [math]=\sec ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{12}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{16}{65}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{r}\sqrt{65^{2}-16^{2}}=63 \\\sec ^{-1} \frac{16}{65}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63}\end{array}[/math]
[পাশের চিত্র হতে][math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{12}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{5}{12}}{1-\frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{5}{12}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{48+15}{36}}{\frac{36-20}{36}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{63}{16}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{16}{63} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{63}{16}+\frac{16}{63}}{1-\frac{63}{16} \cdot \frac{16}{63}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{63}{16}+\frac{16}{63}}{1-1} . \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{63}{16}+\frac{16}{63}}{0}=\tan ^{-1} \infty \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{2}=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sec ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{12}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{16}{65}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)[বি. দ্র. প্রশ্নে [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{11}{5}[/math]
 এর স্থানে [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{12}{5}[/math]
 হবে ]গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2+\cos \theta\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin \theta-\cos \theta=2\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta-\frac{1}{2} \cos \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \sin \theta \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{6}-\cos \theta \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=1\\\Rightarrow \sin \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)^{}=1\end{array}[/math]
$ \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{6}=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} [math]যেখানে,[/math]
n \in Z$$ \Rightarrow \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}$[math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{8 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{3 \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}=-\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=-2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{7 \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}=-\frac{10 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
 সীমার মধ্যে, [math]\theta=-\frac{4 \pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]\theta=-\frac{4 \pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]

Question #105 (ID: 442455)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018, 2018, 2018, 2018 | Boards: Rajshahi Board, Chittagong Board, Barisal Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board, Cumilla Board, Chattogram Board, Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ :


[Image]


দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]1+\sin ^{2} x-2 \cos ^{2} x+3 \cos x=3-\cos ^{2} x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right)=\frac{2}{9}[/math] যেখানে [math]A B=2, y=\sqrt{5}[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প- ২ এ বর্ণিত সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কমনে করি, [math]\sin ^{-1} x=\theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \sin \theta=x[/math]
এখন [math]\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=x\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\\\Rightarrow \theta+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2} .[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)খদেওয়া আছে, [math]A B=2, y=\sqrt{5}[/math]
তাহলে [math]x^{2}=2^{2}+(\sqrt{5})^{2}=4+5 = 9[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=3[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\\Rightarrow 1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\left\{1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1-\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\right\}^{2}-1+\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2} \\\Rightarrow -\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow -\frac{1}{9}+\frac{1}{3}=\frac{-1+3}{9}=\frac{2}{9}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right)=\frac{2}{9}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)গপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ ,[math]\begin{array}{l}1+\sin ^{2} x-2 \cos ^{2} x+3 \cos x=3-\cos ^{2} x \\\Rightarrow 1+1-\cos ^{2} x-2 \cos ^{2} x+3 \cos x-3+\cos ^{2} x=0 \\\Rightarrow -2 \cos ^{2} x+3 \cos x-1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x-3 \cos x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x-2 \cos x-\cos x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x(\cos x-1)-1(\cos x-1)=0 \\\Rightarrow (\cos x-1)(2 \cos x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় , [math]\cos x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos x=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x=2 n \pi[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathrm{n} \in[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos x=1\\\Rightarrow \cos x=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos x=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\\\therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
যেখানে, [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সাধারণ সমাধান : [math]x=2 n \pi, 2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\sin^{-1}x[/math], তাহলে [math]\sin\theta=x[/math] ও [math]\cos\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\bigr)=\sin\theta=x[/math]; যেহেতু [math]\cos^{-1}x[/math] হল [math]x[/math]-এর কোসাইন ইনভার্স, তাই [math]\cos^{-1}x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math] এবং ফলে [math]\sin^{-1}x+\cos^{-1}x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math].

খ ব্যবহার করি [math]\sin(\cos^{-1}u)=\sqrt{1-u^2}[/math] ও [math]\cos(\sin^{-1}v)=\sqrt{1-v^2}[/math] (প্রাসঙ্গিক ডোমেইনের জন্য ধনাত্মক শিকড়)। তাই
[math]\sin^{2}\!\bigl(\cos^{-1}\tfrac{1}{x}\bigr)=1-\tfrac{1}{x^{2}},\quad
\cos^{2}\!\bigl(\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\bigr)=1-\tfrac{1}{x},[/math]
অতএব ভিন্নতা হলো [math]\tfrac{1}{x}-\tfrac{1}{x^{2}}=\tfrac{x-1}{x^{2}}[/math]. সমীকরণ [math]\tfrac{x-1}{x^{2}}=\tfrac{2}{9}[/math] দিলে [math]2x^{2}-9x+9=0[/math] থেকে [math]x=3[/math] বা [math]x=\tfrac{3}{2}[/math]; ডোমেইন শর্ত ( [math]x\ge1[/math] ) পূরণ হচ্ছি, তাই উভয়টি গ্রহণযোগ্য।

গ সমীকরণ সরল করলে [math]1+\sin^{2}x-2\cos^{2}x+3\cos x=3-\cos^{2}x[/math] থেকে [math]2\cos^{2}x-3\cos x+1=0[/math] হয়; [math]\cos x=1[/math] বা [math]\cos x=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]. অতএব সাধারণ সমাধানগুলি হল [math]x=2k\pi[/math] বা [math]x=2k\pi\pm\tfrac{\pi}{3}[/math] যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #106 (ID: 442454)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018, 2018, 2018, 2018 | Boards: Dinajpur Board, Dhaka Board, Sylhet Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board, Dhaka Board, Jessore Board, Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প -১ : [math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{4}{5}\right)+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\right)-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right)[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প -২ : [math]4\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5,-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1}\left(2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)[/math]
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ বর্ণিত সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কমনে করি, [math]\sin ^{-1} x=A[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \sin A=x[/math]
এবং [math]\cos A=\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} A}=\sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
এখন [math]\sin 2 A=2 \sin A \cos A=2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 A=\sin ^{-1}\left(2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad 2 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1}\left(2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right) .[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)খপ্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{4}{5}\right)+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\right)-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{4}{3}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}\right)-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{8+3}{6}}{1-\frac{2}{3}}\right)-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{6}}{\frac{1}{3}}-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}-\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{11}\right)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{11}{2}\right)-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{11}{2}\right)=0[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]0[/math]

[Image]

[Image]
গপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]4\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos \theta\right)=5[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} \theta+4 \cos \theta-5=0 \\\Rightarrow 4\left(1-\cos ^{2} \theta\right)+4 \cos \theta-5=0 \\\Rightarrow 4-4 \cos ^{2} \theta+4 \cos \theta-5=0 \\\Rightarrow -4 \cos ^{2} \theta+4 \cos \theta-1=0 \\\Rightarrow 4 \cos ^{2} \theta-4 \cos \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \cos \theta-1)^{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta-1=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 যখন [math]n[/math]
-এর মান শূন্য বা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা ৷যখন [math]n = 0[/math]
, তখন, [math]\theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
যখন [math]n = 1,[/math]
 তখন, [math]\theta= \pm \frac{5 \pi}{3}, \frac{7 \pi}{3}[/math]
যখন [math]n = − 1[/math]
, তখন, [math]\theta=-\frac{5 \pi}{3},-\frac{7 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধিতে ৪-এর মানসমূহ : [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \pm \frac{5 \pi}{3} .[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \pm \frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক ধরা যাক [math]a=\sin^{-1}x[/math], তখন [math]x=\sin a[/math] এবং [math]\sin(2a)=2\sin a\cos a=2x\sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]. অতএব [math]2a=\sin^{-1}\big(2x\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\big)[/math], কিন্তু এটি তখনই সঠিক যখন [math]2a\in\big[-\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{\pi}{2}\big][/math], δηে [math]a\in\big[-\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{\pi}{4}\big][/math] অর্থাৎ [math]x\in\big[-\tfrac{\sqrt2}{2},\tfrac{\sqrt2}{2}\big][/math].

খ নিন [math]A=\sin^{-1}\tfrac{4}{5},\;B=\cos^{-1}\tfrac{2}{\sqrt5}[/math]; তখন [math]\sin(A+B)=\tfrac{11}{5\sqrt5},\;\cos(A+B)=\tfrac{2}{5\sqrt5}[/math] এবং তাই [math]\tan(A+B)=\tfrac{11}{2}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]A+B=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{11}{2}[/math]. এছাড়া [math]\cot^{-1}\tfrac{2}{11}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{11}{2}[/math], ফলে সমগ্র যোগফলটি [math]0[/math]।

গ সমীকরণ থেকে [math]4(1-\cos^{2}\theta+\cos\theta)=5[/math] হয়ে [math](2\cos\theta-1)^{2}=0[/math] পাওয়া যায়, সুতরাং [math]\cos\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]. কর্মক্ষম পরিসরে [math]-2\pi<\theta<2\pi[/math] এর সমাধানগুলি হল [math]\theta=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{3},\;\pm\tfrac{5\pi}{3}[/math]।

Question #107 (ID: 442453)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ :


[Image]


দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]g(x)=\cot x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} 4[/math] ও [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math] এর সমষ্টি নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর আলোকে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\frac{1}{2} \varnothing+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\cot ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} \frac{29}{28}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \cdot g\left(\frac{3 \pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=1,0 \leq \theta \leq \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan ^{-1} 4[/math]
 ও [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]
 এর সমষ্টি : [math]=\tan ^{-1} 4+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{4+\frac{5}{3}}{1-4 \cdot \frac{5}{3}} .[x y>1] \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{12+5}{3-20}\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{17}{-17}\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi-\tan ^{-1}(1)=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4} .\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4} .[/math]
 খবামপক্ষ [math]=\frac{1}{2} \varnothing+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\left(\frac{2}{3}\right)^{2}}{1+\left(\frac{2}{3}\right)^{2}}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} .2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{4}}{1-\frac{2}{3} \cdot \frac{3}{4}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{8+9}{12}}{1-\frac{1}{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17}{12}}{\frac{1}{2}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{17}{6}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17}{6}-\frac{1}{2}}{1+\frac{17}{6} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17-3}{6}}{1+\frac{17}{12}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{14}{6} \times \frac{12}{29}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}, \\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} \frac{29}{28}+\cot ^{-1} 2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\cot ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} \frac{29}{28}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \frac{1}{2} \varnothing+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\cot ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} \frac{29}{28} .[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cot x[/math]
এবং [math]g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \cdot g\left(\frac{3 \pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \cdot \cot \left(\frac{3 \pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=1\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta \cdot \tan 2 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta \cdot \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}=1\\\Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan ^{2} \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta\\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{3}\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right)\\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{7 \pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]0 \leq \theta \leq \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক arctan যোগের সূত্র ব্যবহার করলে [math]\tan^{-1}a+\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] (প্রয়োজনে [math]+\pi[/math] যোগ করে)। এখানে [math]a=4,b=\tfrac{5}{3}[/math], ফলে অনুপাতটি [math]\frac{4+5/3}{1-20/3}=-1[/math] এবংprodukt>1 হওয়ায় [math]\tan^{-1}(-1)+\pi=-\tfrac{\pi}{4}+\pi=\tfrac{3\pi}{4}[/math] হয়।

খ প্রথমে [math]\sin^{-1}\tfrac{3}{5}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{3}{4}[/math] লিখে নাও; আর ডানদিকের কটগুলিকে [math]\cot^{-1}x=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{x}[/math] (ধনাত্মক ক্ষেত্রে) হিসেবে লিখলে RHS = [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{2}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{28}{29}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{17}{6}[/math]; তখন LHS থেকে (অন্তর্বর্তী গণনা করে) পাওয়া যায় [math]\tfrac{1}{2}\varnothing=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{2}{3}[/math] এবং উভয় পক্ষ একই মান দেয়, সুতরাং সমীকরণটি সত্য।

গ [math]g(x)=\cot x[/math] অনুযায়ী [math]g\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\big)=\tan\theta[/math] এবং [math]g\big(\tfrac{3\pi}{2}-2\theta\big)=\cot\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-2\theta\big)=\tan2\theta[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\tan\theta\cdot\tan2\theta=1[/math]. [math]t=\tan\theta[/math] নিলে [math]\tfrac{2t^2}{1-t^2}=1\Rightarrow t^2=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math], অতএব [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{6},\tfrac{5\pi}{6}[/math] (যাহা [math]0\le\theta\le\pi[/math] মধ্যে আছে)।

Question #108 (ID: 442452)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]f(x)=\sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]A=2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে সমাধান কর : [math]f(\sin \theta)=0[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\dot{A}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \left\{1+\tan ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}+\left\{1+\cot ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)+1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right) \\\Rightarrow 2+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2} \\\Rightarrow 2+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}=2+\frac{1}{9}+\frac{1}{4}=\frac{72+4+9}{36}=\frac{85}{36}\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\frac{85}{36^{\circ}}[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sqrt{2} x^{2}-3 x+\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(\sin \theta)=\sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \sin \theta+\sqrt{2}[/math]
এখানে, [math]f(\sin \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta-\sin \theta+\sqrt{2}=0\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin \theta(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})-1(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})=0\\\Rightarrow (\sin \theta-\sqrt{2})(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1)=0\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1=0 \quad[\because(\sin \theta-\sqrt{2}) 
eq 0]\\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]n \in Z .[/math]
 গ
[Image]
দেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{aligned}A & =2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}} \\& \Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}}{1-\frac{1}{8}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \times \frac{8}{7}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}{1-\frac{4 \sqrt{2}}{7} \times \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{8+7}{7 \sqrt{2}}}{\frac{7 \sqrt{2}-4 \sqrt{2}}{7 \sqrt{2}}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{15}{3 \sqrt{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore A=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\cot^{-1}3=\theta \Rightarrow \tan\theta=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] এবং [math]\tan^{-1}2=\phi \Rightarrow \cot\phi=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], তাই [math]\sec^{2}\theta=1+\tan^{2}\theta=1+\tfrac{1}{9}=\tfrac{10}{9}[/math] এবং [math]\csc^{2}\phi=1+\cot^{2}\phi=1+\tfrac{1}{4}=\tfrac{5}{4}[/math]; যেগুলোর যোগফল [math]\tfrac{10}{9}+\tfrac{5}{4}=\tfrac{85}{36}[/math].

খ সমীকরণটি হল [math]\sqrt{2}\sin^{2}\theta-3\sin\theta+\sqrt{2}=0[/math]; ডিসক্রিমিনেন্ট [math]1[/math] হলে মূলগুলো [math]\sin\theta=\dfrac{3\pm1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] (অগ্রহণযোগ্য) অথবা [math]\sin\theta=\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]; অতএব সাধারণ সমাধান [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]\theta=\tfrac{3\pi}{4}+2k\pi[/math]।

গ নাং [math]\alpha= \sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{3}\Rightarrow \tan\alpha=\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math] এবং [math]\beta=\cos^{-1}\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}\Rightarrow \tan\beta=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]; এরপর [math]\tan(2\alpha)=\dfrac{2\tan\alpha}{1-\tan^{2}\alpha}=\dfrac{8}{7\sqrt{2}}[/math] এবং [math]\tan(2\alpha+\beta)=\dfrac{\tan(2\alpha)+\tan\beta}{1-\tan(2\alpha)\tan\beta}=\dfrac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math], তাই [math]A=\tan^{-1}\dfrac{5}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]।

Question #109 (ID: 442451)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে [math]\tan ^{-1}\{(2+\sqrt{3}) f(x)\}+\tan ^{-1}\{(2-\sqrt{3})[/math] [math]f(x)\}=\tan ^{-1}\{2 f(2 x)\}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cos x+\sin x[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\left[\because \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}\right.[/math]
 যখন [math]x y=1][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \quad \tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{3}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)
[Image]
খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(2 x)=\tan 2 x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1}\{(2+\sqrt{3}) f(x)\}+\tan ^{-1}\{(2-\sqrt{3}) f(x)\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\{(2+\sqrt{3}) \tan x\}+\tan ^{-1}\{(2-\sqrt{3}) \tan x\} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{(2+\sqrt{3}+2-\sqrt{3}) \tan x}{1-(2+\sqrt{3})(2-\sqrt{3}) \tan ^{2} x} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{4 \tan x}{1-(4-3) \tan ^{2} x} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left\{2 \times \frac{2 \tan x}{1-\tan ^{2} x}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}(2 \tan 2 x) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\{2 f(2 x)\}\end{array}[/math]
=ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1}\{(2+\sqrt{3}) f(x)\}+\tan ^{-1}\{(2-\sqrt{3}) f(x)\}=\tan ^{-1}\{2 f(2 x)\} .[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\tan x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cot 2 x[/math]
এখানে [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cos x+\sin x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cot 2 x=\cos x+\sin x\\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos 2 x}{\sin 2 x}=\cos x+\sin x\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\sin 2 x(\cos x+\sin x)\\\Rightarrow \left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)-2 \sin x \cos x(\cos x+\sin x)=0\\\Rightarrow (\cos x+\sin x)(\cos x-\sin x-2 \sin x \cos x)=0\\\text{ হয় } \cos x+\sin x=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1+\tan x=0[\cos x[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan x=-1\\\Rightarrow \tan x=\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা [math]\cos x-\sin x-2 \sin x \cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x-\sin x=2 \sin x \cos x\\\Rightarrow \cos x-\sin x=\sin 2 x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (\cos x-\sin x)^{2}=\sin ^{2} 2 x[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} x+\sin ^{2} x-2 \sin x \cos x=\sin ^{2} 2 x\\\Rightarrow 1-\sin 2 x=\sin ^{2} 2 x\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} 2 x+\sin 2 x-1=0 \text{.}\\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1+4}}{2}=\frac{1}{2}(-1 \pm \sqrt{5})\end{array}[/math]
অর্থাৎ, [math]\sin 2 x=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{5})[/math]
 কারণ সাইন অনুপাতের মান [math]-1[/math]
অপেক্ষা ক্ষুদ্রতর হতে পারে না ।ধরি , [math]\sin \alpha=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{5}) \Rightarrow \alpha=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{5})\right\}[/math]
তাহলে [math]\sin 2 x=\sin \alpha[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad 2 x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \alpha[/math]
 যেখানে [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\frac{1}{2} n \pi+(-1)^{n}, \frac{1}{2} \alpha \text {. }[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]x=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{1}{2} n \pi+(-1)^{n}, \frac{1}{2} \alpha[/math]
যেখানে, [math]\alpha=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{5})\right\}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}\frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math], তাহলে [math]\cos\alpha=\frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math] এবং [math]\sin\alpha=\frac{3}{\sqrt{34}}[/math]; তাই [math]\tan\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha\right)=\cot\alpha=\frac{\cos\alpha}{\sin\alpha}=\frac{5}{3}[/math], ফলে [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{5}{3}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos^{-1}\frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}[/math].

খ ব্যবহার করি যোগের সূত্র: যদি [math]A=\tan^{-1}a,\;B=\tan^{-1}b[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]A+B=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] (প্রধান মান ধরে)। এখানে [math]a=(2+\sqrt{3})\tan x,\;b=(2-\sqrt{3})\tan x[/math], তাই [math]a+b=4\tan x,\;ab=(4-3)\tan^2 x=\tan^2 x[/math] এবং ফলে যোগফল [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{4\tan x}{1-\tan^2 x}=\tan^{-1}\{2\tan(2x)\}=\tan^{-1}\{2f(2x)\}[/math]।

গ সমীকরণটি লিখি [math]\tan\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2x\right)=\cos x+\sin x\Rightarrow\cot2x=\cos x+\sin x[/math]; দুই পাশে [math]\sin2x[/math] দিয়ে গুণ করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]\cos2x=2\sin x\cos x(\cos x+\sin x)[/math], যা সরল করলে [math](\cos x+\sin x)(\cos x-\sin x-2\sin x\cos x)=0[/math] হয়। তাই বা তো [math]\cos x+\sin x=0\Rightarrow x=-\frac{\pi}{4}+n\pi[/math], অথবা [math]\cos x-\sin x-2\sin x\cos x=0\Rightarrow \tan x=\frac{1}{3}\Rightarrow x=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}+n\pi[/math] (যেখানে [math]n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math])।

Question #110 (ID: 442450)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]f(a)=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{a}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{b}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]g(\alpha)=\sin (\pi \cos \alpha)-\cos (\pi \sin \alpha)[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cot \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে যদি [math]g(\alpha)=0[/math] হয়, তবে দেখাও যে, [math]\alpha=\pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে [math]f(a)=\alpha[/math] হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin \alpha=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=\cot \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cot \left(\cot ^{-1} 2\right) \\\Rightarrow 2\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান : [math]2[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\cot ^{-2} \frac{2}{1} .[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(\alpha)=\sin (\pi \cos \alpha)-\cos (\pi \sin \alpha)[/math]
 [math]g(\alpha)=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin (\pi \cos \alpha)-\cos (\pi \sin \alpha)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos \alpha)=\cos (\pi \sin \alpha)\\\Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos \alpha)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \alpha\right)\\\Rightarrow \pi \cos \alpha=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \alpha\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \alpha=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin \alpha\\\Rightarrow \quad \cos \alpha \pm \sin \alpha=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (\cos \alpha \pm \sin \alpha)^{2}=\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} \theta+\sin ^{2} \theta \pm 2 \cos \alpha \cdot \sin \alpha=\frac{1}{4}\\\Rightarrow 1 \pm \sin 2 \alpha=\frac{1}{4}\\\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 \alpha=\frac{1}{4}-1\\\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 \alpha=\frac{-3}{4}\\\Rightarrow \sin 2 \alpha= \pm \frac{3}{4} \text{,}\\\Rightarrow 2 \alpha= \pm \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \alpha= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(a)=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{a}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{b}[/math]
 [math]f(a)=\alpha[/math]
 হলে [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{a}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{b}=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} a+\cos ^{-1} b=\alpha\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left(a b-\sqrt{\left(1-a^{2}\right)\left(1-b^{2}\right)}\right)=\alpha\\\Rightarrow a b-\sqrt{\left(1-a^{2}\right)\left(1-b^{2}\right)}=\cos \alpha\\\Rightarrow a b-\cos \alpha=\sqrt{\left(1-a^{2}\right)\left(1-b^{2}\right)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (a b-\cos \alpha)^{2}=\left(1-a^{2}\right)\left(1-b^{2}\right)[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow a^{2} b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha+\cos ^{2} \alpha=1-b^{2}-a^{2}+a^{2} b^{2}\\\Rightarrow a^{2}+b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha=1-\cos ^{2} \alpha\\\Rightarrow a^{2}+b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha=\sin ^{2} \alpha\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \alpha=a^{2}+b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \sin \alpha=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}-2 a b \cos \alpha}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)

Explanation:
ক একটি সমকোণী ত্রিভুজে বিপরীত অংশ [math]1[/math], কপাল (hypotenuse) [math]\sqrt{5}[/math] হলে পার্শ্বটী দৈর্ঘ্য [math]\sqrt{5-1}=2[/math]; তাই [math]\cot\!\left(\sin^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right)=\frac{\text{adj}}{\text{opp}}=\frac{2}{1}=2.[/math]

খ [math]g(\alpha)=0[/math] থেকে [math]\sin(\pi\cos\alpha)=\cos(\pi\sin\alpha)=\sin\!\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi\sin\alpha\right)[/math], তাই বাৎলো [math]\pi\cos\alpha=\frac{\pi}{2}\mp\pi\sin\alpha+2\pi n[/math]; n=0 ছাড়া মানসমূহ [math]\sin\alpha\pm\cos\alpha=\tfrac12+2n[/math] সম্ভব নয়, ফলে নেগেট ও পজেটিভ কেসগুলো বর্গ করলে [math]\sin2\alpha=\mp\tfrac{3}{4}[/math] হয় এবং তাই [math]\alpha=\pm\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\!\frac{3}{4}.[/math]

গ ধরি [math]\theta=\sec^{-1}\frac{1}{a},\ \phi=\sec^{-1}\frac{1}{b}[/math] ফলে [math]\cos\theta=a,\ \cos\phi=b[/math] এবং [math]\alpha=\theta+\phi[/math]; তখন [math]\sin\alpha=\sin\theta\cos\phi+\cos\theta\sin\phi[/math] এবং [math]\cos\alpha=\cos\theta\cos\phi-\sin\theta\sin\phi[/math] ব্যবহার করে বর্গ এবং [math]\sin^2+\cos^2=1[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে সহজে পাওয়া যায় [math]\sin\alpha=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}-2ab\cos\alpha}[/math] (ধনাত্মক শিকড় নেওয়া হয়েছে)।

Question #111 (ID: 442448)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
