Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]\sec A=\sqrt{5}, \operatorname{cosec} B=\frac{5}{3}[/math] এবং [math]cot C = 3.[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]f(x) = sinx[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{17}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{26}}{5}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর। 5
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ থেকে, [math]A+C-\frac{1}{2} B[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. সমাধান কর : দৃশ্যকল্প-২ থেকে [math]\sqrt{3} f(x)-f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}+x\right)=2[/math] যখন [math]-2 \pi


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{17}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{26}}{5}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{5}}{1-\frac{1}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{5}}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5+4}{20-1}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{9}{19}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{9}{19}\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{9}{19} \text { }[/math]

[Image]
খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ এ[math]\sec A=\sqrt{5}[/math]
 এবং [math]\operatorname{cosec} B=\frac{5}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \mathrm{A}=\sec ^{-1} \cdot \sqrt{5} \quad \Rightarrow \mathrm{B}=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]
এবং [math]\cot C=3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow C=\cot ^{-1} 3[/math]
প্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=A+C-\frac{1}{2} B[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}+\cot ^{-1} 3-\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{5}{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{6}{9+1}\right)-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\tan ^{-1} 2\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]

[Image]
গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
এখানে, [math]\sqrt{3} f(x)-f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}+x\right)=2[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin x-\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}+x\right)=2\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin x-\cos x=2\\\Rightarrow \cos x-\sqrt{3} \sin x=-2\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos x-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin x=-1\end{array}[/math]
[ উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+(-\sqrt{3})^{2}}[/math]
 বা, [math]2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{3}-\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=-1\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x+\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=-1\\\Rightarrow x+\frac{\pi}{3}=(2 n+1) \pi ; n \in Z\\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\pi-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}\\\therefore \quad x=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=2 \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}=\frac{8 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-2 \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}=\frac{-4 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]-2 \pi [math]x=\frac{-4 \pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:
ক cosec⁻¹√17 হলে sinθ = 1/√17 ⇒ θ = [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{4}[/math], এবং sec⁻¹(√26/5) হলে cosφ = 5/√26 ⇒ φ = [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{5}[/math]; অতএব [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{4}+\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{5}=\tan^{-1}\frac{9}{19}.[math]

খ [math]\sec A=\sqrt5⇒\tan A=2,[\ \cosec B=5/3⇒\sin B=3/5⇒B=\arcsin(3/5),\ \tan C=\tfrac{1}{3}.][/math] দেখা যায় [math]\tan(A+C)=\tan^{-1}2+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{3}=\tan^{-1}7[/math] এবং [math]\tan2C=\tfrac{3}{4}[/math] থেকে [math]B=2C[/math]; ফলে [math]A+C-\tfrac{1}{2}B=A.[/math]

গ [math]\sin\!\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}+x\big)=\cos x[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\sqrt3\sin x-\cos x=2\Leftrightarrow 2\sin\big(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\big)=2[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin\big(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\big)=1[/math] এবং [math]x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math]; যদি [math]x\in[-2\pi,2\pi][/math] ধরা হয় তবে সমাধানগুলো [math]x=-\tfrac{4\pi}{3},\ \tfrac{2\pi}{3}.[math]

Question #112 (ID: 442449)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math] এর মান কত?
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]\sqrt{3} g(x)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1[/math]; যখন [math]-2 \pi


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3+2}{6}}{1-\frac{1}{6}}=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{5}{6}}{\frac{5}{6}}\right)[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান : [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 এবং [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
এখন, [math]f\left\{\sqrt{2} \mathrm{~g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right\}+f\{\sqrt{\mathrm{g}(2 \theta)}\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right\}+\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{\cos (2 \theta)}\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta\}+\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{2 \sin ^{2} \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \cdot \sqrt{2} \sin \theta+\cos 2 \theta\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos 2 \theta\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{1-\cos 2 \theta+\cos 2 \theta\}=\sin ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
নির্ণেয় মান [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
এখন, [math]\sqrt{3} g(x)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \cos x+\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \cos x+\sin x=1 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cdot \cos x+\frac{1}{2} \sin x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in Z \\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2},(12 n-1) \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\mathbf{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{-\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{5 \pi}{2}, \frac{11 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\mathbf{n}=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{-3 \pi}{2}, \frac{-13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]-2 \pi [math]x=\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{-\pi}{6}, \frac{11 \pi}{6}, \frac{-3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক যেহেতু [math]\tan^{-1}a+\tan^{-1}b=\tan^{-1}\frac{a+b}{1-ab}[/math] (এবং এখানে [math]abগ [math]\cos\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin x[/math] ব্যবহার করে সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\sqrt{3}\cos x+\sin x=1[/math]; একে রূপান্তর করে [math]2\cos\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\bigr)=1[/math], এবং তাই [math]\cos\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\bigr)=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], ফলে সাধারণ সমাধান [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math]; যদি ধরা হয় [math]-2\pi\le x\le2\pi[/math], তবে উপরের সমাধানগুলো থেকে পাওয়া মানগুলো হল [math]x=-\tfrac{3\pi}{2},\;-\tfrac{\pi}{6},\;\tfrac{\pi}{2},\;\tfrac{11\pi}{6}.[/math]

Question #113 (ID: 442446)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Mymensingh Board | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\cos x, A=\sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{x}, B=\sec ^{-1} \frac{3}{y}[/math]

Options:
ক. সমাধান কর : [math]\tan 2 x-\tan x=0[/math]
খ. সমাধান কর [math]\sqrt{2} f(x)-\sqrt{2} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1[/math] যখন [math]-\pi
গ. দেখাও যে, [math]A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] সমীকরণটি একটি উপবৃত্ত নির্দেশ করে ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{array}{l}\tan 2 x-\tan x=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 2 x}{\cos 2 x}-\frac{\sin x}{\cos x}=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 2 x \cos x-\sin x \cos 2 x}{\cos x \cos 2 x}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin (2 x-x)=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin x=0 \\\therefore x=n \pi(\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}) \text { [Ans.] }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\quad \sqrt{2} f(x)-\sqrt{2} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \cos x-\sqrt{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \cos x-\sqrt{2} \sin x=1 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{2}}{2} \cos x-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2} \sin x=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{2})^{2}+(-\sqrt{2})^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}-\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow x+\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}-\frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}-\frac{\pi}{4}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}-\frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{12}, 2 n \pi-\frac{7 \pi}{12}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]-\piগদেওয়া আছে, [math]A=\sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{x}, B=\sec ^{-1} \frac{3}{y}[/math]
এবং [math]A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{x}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{3}{y}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [মান বসিয়ে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{2}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{y}{3}=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{2} \cdot \frac{y}{3}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{9}\right)}\right\}=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{6}-\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{9}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}}=\cos \frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{6}-\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{9}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}}=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{6}=\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{9}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}} \\\Rightarrow \left(\frac{x y}{6}\right)^{2}=1-\frac{y^{2}}{9}-\frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}+\frac{y^{2}}{9}+\frac{x^{2}}{4}-\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}=1 \\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{y^{2}}{9}=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{2^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{3^{2}}=1[/math]
 যা একটি উপবৃত্তের সমীকরণের অনুরূপ ।সমীকরণটি একটি উপবৃত্ত নির্দেশ করে।[দেখানো হলো]

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক সমাধান: [math]\tan2x=\tan x[/math] হলে [math]2x=x+n\pi[/math] তাই [math]x=n\pi[/math] (যেখানে [math]n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]); [math]\tan x[/math] অপরিসীম হলে সমীকরণ প্রযোজ্য নয়।

খ সমাধান: [math]f(x)=\cos x,\; f\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-x\bigr)=\sin x[/math], তাই [math]\sqrt2(\cos x-\sin x)=1\Rightarrow 2\cos\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)=1[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\cos\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)=\tfrac12[/math], ফলে [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{12}+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{12}+2k\pi[/math]।

গ বিবরণ: [math]\sec A=\tfrac{2}{x}\Rightarrow \cos A=\tfrac{x}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\sec B=\tfrac{3}{y}\Rightarrow \cos B=\tfrac{y}{3}[/math]; যদি [math]A+B=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\cos B=\sin A[/math], ফলে [math]\bigl(\tfrac{x}{2}\bigr)^2+\bigl(\tfrac{y}{3}\bigr)^2=1[/math], যা উপবৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Question #114 (ID: 442447)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}, g(y)=\frac{1-y^{2}}{1+y^{2}}[/math] এবং [math]h(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{~m}+\cos ^{-1} \mathrm{n}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে , প্রমাণ কর যে [math]m^{2}+n^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1}{f(a)}-\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{g(b)}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math] হলে দেখাও যে [math]x=\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
গ. [math]0 \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]2 h(\theta) . h(3 \theta)=1[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]\sin ^{-1} m+\sin ^{-1} n=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \mathrm{~m}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{n} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{m}=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{n}\right) \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{m}=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{n}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow m^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} n\right)[/math]
 [বর্গ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow m^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} n\right)\\\Rightarrow m^{2}=1-\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} n\right)\right\}^{2}\\\Rightarrow m^{2}=1-n^{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad m^{2}+n^{2}=1 .[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত)[বি: দ্র: প্রশ্নে [math]\cos ^{-1} n[/math]
 এর পরিবর্তে [math]\sin ^{-1} n[/math]
 হবে ]খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 এবং [math]g(y)=\frac{1-y^{2}}{1+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(\mathrm{a})=\frac{2 \mathrm{a}}{1+\mathrm{a}^{2}} \quad \therefore \mathrm{g}(\mathrm{b})=\frac{1-\mathrm{b}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{b}^{2}}[/math]
এখানে, [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1}{f(a)}-\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{g(b)}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \quad \sin ^{-1} f(\mathrm{a})-\cos ^{-1} g(b)=2 \tan ^{-1} x\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}=2 \tan ^{-1} x\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} a-2 \tan ^{-1} b=2 \tan ^{-1} x\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{~b}=\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=\tan ^{-1} x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x=\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
(দেখানো হলো)গদেওয়া আছে, [math]h(x)=\sin x[/math]
 হলে [math]2 \mathrm{~h}(\theta) \cdot h(3 \theta)=1 ; 0 \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin \theta \cdot \sin 3 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \cos (3 \theta-\theta)-\cos (3 \theta+\theta)=1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta-\cos 4 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=1+\cos 4 \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=2 \cos ^{2} 2 \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} 2 \theta-\cos 2 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta(2 \cos 2 \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\cos 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা [math]2 \cos 2 \theta-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}, \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}n=1 \prime, \theta=\frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{7 \pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6} \
=2 \prime 2, \theta=\frac{5 \pi}{4}, \frac{13 \pi}{6}, \frac{11 \pi}{6} \
=3 \prime, \theta=\frac{7 \pi}{4}, \frac{19 \pi}{6}, \frac{17 \pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]0 \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে নির্ণেয় সমাধান :[math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{7 \pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \frac{11 \pi}{6}, \frac{7 \pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা থাক্‌ক [math]\sin^{-1}m=\theta[/math], তাহলে [math]m=\sin\theta[/math] এবং [math]\cos^{-1}n=\pi/2-\theta[/math] থেকে [math]n=\cos(\pi/2-\theta)=\sin\theta=m[/math]. ফলে [math]m=n[/math] এবং [math]m^{2}+n^{2}=2m^{2}[/math]; তাই [math]m^{2}+n^{2}=1[/math] হতে হলে [math]m=\pm 1/\sqrt{2}[/math] হতে হবে।

খ নিন [math]A=\tan^{-1}a,\;B=\tan^{-1}b[/math]. তখন [math]f(a)=\dfrac{2a}{1+a^{2}}=\sin2A[/math] ফলে [math]\csc^{-1}\frac{1}{f(a)}=2A[/math], এবং [math]g(b)=\dfrac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}=\cos2B[/math] ফলে [math]\sec^{-1}\frac{1}{g(b)}=2B[/math]. দেওয়া সমীকরণ থেকে [math]2A-2B=2\tan^{-1}x\Rightarrow \tan^{-1}x=A-B\Rightarrow x=\tan(A-B)=\frac{a-b}{1+ab}[/math]।

গ সমীকরণটি লিখুন [math]2\sin\theta\sin3\theta=1[/math], এবং [math]\sin3\theta=3\sin\theta-4\sin^{3}\theta[/math] ব্যবহার করে [math]6\sin^{2}\theta-8\sin^{4}\theta=1[/math], যেখানে [math]s=\sin^{2}\theta[/math] দিলে [math]8s^{2}-6s+1=0[/math] হয়, সমাধান হলো [math]s=1/2[/math] বা [math]s=1/4[/math]. তাই [math]\sin\theta=\pm1/\sqrt{2} থেকে [math]\theta=\pi/4,3\pi/4,5\pi/4,7\pi/4[/math] এবং [math]\sin\theta=\pm1/2 থেকে [math]\theta=\pi/6,5\pi/6,7\pi/6,11\pi/6[/math] (সকল [math]0\le\theta\le2\pi[/math])।

Question #115 (ID: 442445)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Mymensingh Board | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\boxed{\mathbf{~ক~}}[/math]


[Image]


বামপক্ষ [math]=\operatorname{cosec} \sin ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \frac{x}{y}[/math] [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec} \sin ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y^{0}} \\\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y} \\\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{y}{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{y}{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]

= ডানপক্ষ

[প্রমাণিত]

[math]\\[/math] [math]\boxed{\mathbf{~খ~}}[/math]

দেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\operatorname{cosec} x-\cot x[/math]

এবং [math]f(\theta)=\frac{3}{4}[/math] [math]\therefore \operatorname{cosec} \theta-\cot \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math] [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}-\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1-\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow 4-4 \cos \theta=3 \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow 3 \sin \theta-4=-4 \cos \theta \\\Rightarrow (3 \sin \theta-4)^{2}=(-4 \cos \theta)^{2} \\\Rightarrow 9 \sin ^{2} \theta-24 \sin \theta+16=16 \cos ^{2} \theta \\\Rightarrow 9 \sin ^{2} \theta-24 \sin \theta+16=16\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow 9 \sin ^{2} \theta-24 \sin \theta+16=16-16 \sin ^{2} \theta \\\Rightarrow 25 \sin ^{2} \theta-24 \sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow 25 \sin ^{2} \theta=24 \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{24}{25}\end{array}[/math] [math]\therefore \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}[/math]

[প্রমাণিত]

[math]\\[/math] [math]\boxed{\mathbf{~গ~}}[/math]

দেওয়া আছে,

[math]\begin{array}{l}g(5 \theta)-\sqrt{3} g(\theta)=g(3 \theta)\\\Rightarrow \sin 5 \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=\sin 3 \theta\\\Rightarrow \sin 5 \theta-\sin 3 \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \left(\frac{5 \theta+3 \theta}{2}\right) \sin \left(\frac{5 \theta-3 \theta}{2}\right)=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 \theta \sin \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 \theta \sin \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \sin \theta(2 \cos 4 \theta-\sqrt{3})=0\end{array}[/math]

হয়, [math], \sin \theta=0[/math] [math]\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 4 \theta-\sqrt{3}=0[/math] [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 \theta=\sqrt{3}\\\Rightarrow \cos 4 \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos 4 \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow 4 \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{4}\left(2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math] [math]\therefore[/math] নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi, \frac{1}{4}\left(2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]

Options:


Correct Answer: 

Solution:


Explanation:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\sec^{-1}\frac{x}{y}[/math], তখন [math]\cos\theta=\frac{y}{x}[/math] এবং [math]\tan\theta=\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y}[/math]; তাই [math]\operatorname{cosec}\big(\sin^{-1}(\tan\theta)\big)=\operatorname{cosec}\big(\sin^{-1}\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y}\big)=\frac{y}{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}[/math] (এখানে [math]y\neq0[/math])। 

খ লেখাকে সাধারণ ভগ্নাংশে লিখলে [math]\operatorname{cosec}\theta-\cot\theta=\frac{1-\cos\theta}{\sin\theta}=\frac{3}{4}[/math] হয়; সরলীকরণ ও বর্গ করলে [math]25\sin^{2}\theta-24\sin\theta=0[/math] পাওয়া যায়। [math]\sin\theta=0[/math] হলে cosec অজ্ঞাত হওয়ায় বাদ দিয়ে বাকি সমাধান [math]\sin\theta=\frac{24}{25}[/math], ফলে [math]\theta=\sin^{-1}\frac{24}{25}[/math]।

গ পার্থক্য পরিচয় ব্যবহার করে [math]\sin5\theta-\sin3\theta=2\cos4\theta\sin\theta[/math] পাওয়া যায়, ফলে [math]\sin\theta(2\cos4\theta-\sqrt{3})=0[/math]; তাই [math]\theta=n\pi[/math] বা [math]\cos4\theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math] হলে [math]4\theta=2n\pi\pm\frac{\pi}{6}[/math] থেকে [math]\theta=\frac{1}{4}\big(2n\pi\pm\frac{\pi}{6}\big)[/math], যেখানে [math]n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #116 (ID: 442444)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১: [math]g(x)=\sin x^{}[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২: [math]f(x)=\cos ^{-1} x .[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cot ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} y^{}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x y=1 .[/math]
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ থেকে সমাধান কর : [math]2 g(\pi-x) g(3 x)=1,[/math] যখন [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi .[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ [math]f(x)+f(y)+f(z)=\pi[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2} +z^{2}+2 x y z=1[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cot ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow x=\cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\cot ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x=\tan \cot ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow x=\tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{y} \\\Rightarrow x=\frac{1}{y}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x y=1[/math]
[দেখানো হলো][math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]2 \mathrm{~g}(\pi-x) \mathrm{g}(3 \mathrm{x})=1[/math]
 যখন, [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin (\pi-x) \sin 3 x=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x \sin 3 x=1\\\Rightarrow \cos (3 x-x)-\cos (3 x+x)=1\end{array}[/math]
][math][\because 2 \sin A \sin B=\cos (A-B)-\cos (A+B)][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x-\cos 4 x=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=1+\cos 4 x \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} 2 x \quad\left[\because 2 \cos ^{2} x=1+\cos 2 x\right] \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} 2 x-\cos 2 x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x(2 \cos 2 x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \cos 2 x=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} \text {. }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে x এর মান নির্ণয়
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় মান [math]=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \frac{7 \pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \frac{7 \pi}{6}, \frac{11 \pi}{6} \text { [Ans.] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]f(x)+f(y)+f(z)=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y+\cos ^{-1} z=\pi\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} .\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\}=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=\cos \left(\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\right)\\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-\cos \cos ^{-1} z\\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-z\\\Rightarrow x y+z=\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)^{}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (x y+z)^{2}=\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে বর্গ করে ][math]\Rightarrow x^{2} y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1-x^{2}-y^{2}+x^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1[/math]
[দেখানো হলো][math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\cot^{-1}x,\ \beta=\cot^{-1}y[/math] তাই [math]\alpha+\beta=\pi/2[/math]. কিন্তু [math]\tan\alpha=1/x,\ \tan\beta=1/y[/math] এবং [math]\tan(\alpha+\beta)=\dfrac{\tan\alpha+\tan\beta}{1-\tan\alpha\tan\beta}[/math]; যেহেতু [math]\tan(\pi/2) অপসীম, মানে নিরিখ হিসাবে ডেনোমিনেটর শূন্য, তাই [math]1-\tan\alpha\tan\beta=0[/math] অর্থাৎ [math](1/x)(1/y)=1[/math] ফলে [math]xy=1[/math].

খ [math]g(\pi-x)=\sin(\pi-x)=\sin x[/math] হলে সমীকরণ হয় [math]2\sin x\sin3x=1[/math]; [math]\sin3x=3\sin x-4\sin^3 x[/math] ব্যবহার করলে [math]8\sin^4x-6\sin^2x+1=0[/math] হয় যাতে [math]\sin^2x=1/2[/math] অথবা [math]\sin^2x=1/4[/math]. তাই সমাধানগুলোর মধ্যে [math]\sin x=±1/\sqrt2[/math] থেকে [math]x=\pi/4,3\pi/4,5\pi/4,7\pi/4[/math] এবং [math]\sin x=±1/2[/math] থেকে [math]x=\pi/6,5\pi/6,7\pi/6,11\pi/6[/math] (সবই [math]0\le x\le2\pi[/math])।

গ ধরা যাক [math]A=\cos^{-1}x,\ B=\cos^{-1}y,\ C=\cos^{-1}z[/math] ফলে [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math] এবং পরিচিত সূত্র থেকে [math]\cos^2A+\cos^2B+\cos^2C+2\cos A\cos B\cos C=1[/math]; এই ক্ষেত্রে [math]\cos A=x,\ \cos B=y,\ \cos C=z[/math] হওয়ায় সরাসরি [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2xyz=1[/math] প্রমাণিত হয়।

Question #117 (ID: 442443)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]N=\tan ^{-1}\left(\left(\operatorname{cosec} \tan ^{-1} x-\tan \cot ^{-1} x\right)\right.[/math] এবং [math]f(\theta)=\cos \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. যদি [math]x=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math] হয়, তবে [math]\tan x[/math] এর মান কত হবে?
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]N=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]f(\theta)+f(2 \theta)+f(3 \theta)=0[/math] যখন [math]-2 \pi \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi .[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]x=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
এখন, [math]\sin ^{2} 2 x+\cos ^{2} 2 x=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin 2 x=\sqrt{1-\cos ^{2 } x}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{1-\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{2}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{1-\frac{9}{16}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{\frac{7}{16}}=\frac{\sqrt{7}}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan x=\frac{\sin x}{\cos x}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{2 \sin x \cos x}{2 \cos ^{2} x}[/math]
 [লব ও হরকে [math]2 \cos x[/math]
 দ্বারা গুণ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 2 x}{1_{1}+\cos 2 x} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\frac{\sqrt{7}}{4}}{1+\frac{3}{4}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{7}}{\frac{4}{4}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{7}}{4} \times \frac{4}{7} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{7}} \text { [Ans.] }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\tan ^{-1} \cdot x[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে
[Image]
[math]\begin{aligned}\therefore \tan ^{-1} x & =\operatorname{cosec}-\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{x} \\& {\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\right] }\end{aligned}[/math]
এখানে, [math]N=\tan ^{-1}\left(\operatorname{cosec} \tan ^{-1} x-\tan \cot ^{-1} x\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\operatorname{cosec} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{x}-\tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}{x}-\frac{1}{x}\right) \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1}{x}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2\left(\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-{1}}{x}\right)}{1-\left(\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}-1}}{x}\right)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2\left(\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1}{x}\right)}{1-\frac{1+x^{2}-2 \sqrt{1+x^{2}+1}}{x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2\left(\frac{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1}{x}\right)}{\frac{x^{2}-1-x^{2}+2 \sqrt{1+x^{2}-1}}{x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2\left(\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1\right)}{x}}{\frac{2 \sqrt{1+x^{2}}-2}{x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{2\left(\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1\right)}{x} \times \frac{x^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{1+x^{2}}-1\right)}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} x\quad \quad \therefore N=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
[দেখানো হলো][math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(\theta)=\cos \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]f(\theta)+f(2 \theta)+f(3 \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \theta+\cos 2 \theta+\cos 3 \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow (\cos 3 \theta+\cos \theta)+\cos 2 \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \left(\frac{3 \theta+\theta}{2}\right) \cos \left(\frac{3 \theta-\theta}{2}\right)+\cos 2 \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 \theta \cos \theta+\cos 2 \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos \hat{2 \theta}(2 \cos \theta+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{4},+\frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, 2 \pi-\frac{2 \pi}{3},-2 \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\text { |Ans.| }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক এখানে [math]x=\tfrac{1}{2}\cos^{-1}\tfrac{3}{4}[/math] হলে [math]\cos 2x=\tfrac{3}{4}[/math], তাই [math]\sin^2 x=\tfrac{1-\cos2x}{2}=\tfrac{1}{8}[/math] এবং [math]\cos^2 x=\tfrac{7}{8}[/math]; কারণ [math]x\in[0,\tfrac{\pi}{2}][/math] তাই ধনাত্মক মান নেবে এবং [math]\tan x=\dfrac{\sin x}{\cos x}=\dfrac{1/(2\sqrt2)}{\sqrt7/(2\sqrt2)}=\dfrac{1}{\sqrt7}[/math]।

খ ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}x[/math], তাহলে [math]\csc\alpha=\dfrac{\sqrt{1+x^2}}{x}[/math] এবং [math]\tan(\cot^{-1}x)=\dfrac{1}{x}[/math]; ফলে অভ্যন্তরীণটি [math]\dfrac{\sqrt{1+x^2}-1}{x}[/math] হয়, এবং পরিচিত পরিচ্ছিন্ন সূত্র দিয়ে [math]\tan\frac{\alpha}{2}=\dfrac{\sqrt{1+\tan^2\alpha}-1}{\tan\alpha}[/math] হওয়ায় [math]N=\tan^{-1}\!\big(\dfrac{\sqrt{1+x^2}-1}{x}\big)=\tfrac{1}{2}\tan^{-1}x[/math]। 

গ [math]\cos\theta+\cos2\theta+\cos3\theta=2\cos2\theta\cos\theta+\cos2\theta=\cos2\theta(2\cos\theta+1)=0[/math]। তাই (i) [math]\cos2\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+k\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math], এবং (ii) [math]\cos\theta=-\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\pm\tfrac{2\pi}{3},\ \pm\tfrac{4\pi}{3}[/math]; উপরের মানগুলো থেকে [-2π,2π] মধ্যে পাওয়া সমাধানগুলো হল [math]\theta=-\tfrac{7\pi}{4},-\tfrac{5\pi}{4},-\tfrac{3\pi}{4},-\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{\pi}{4},\tfrac{3\pi}{4},\tfrac{5\pi}{4},\tfrac{7\pi}{4},\pm\tfrac{2\pi}{3},\pm\tfrac{4\pi}{3}[/math]।

Question #118 (ID: 442442)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] ও [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. সমাধান কর : [math]2\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=\sqrt{3}[/math]
খ. সমাধান কর : [math]f(x)+g(x)=f(2 x)+g(2 x)[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]4 g(x) g(2 x) g(3 x)=1[/math] যখন, [math]0


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}2\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=\sqrt{3} \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{12} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=h \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{12} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 ও [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}f(x)+g(x)=f(2 x)+g(2 x) \\\Rightarrow \sin x+\cos x=\sin 2 x+\cos 2 x \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x-\sin x=\cos x-\cos 2 x \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{2 x+x}{2} \sin \frac{2 x-x}{2}=2 \sin \frac{x+2 x}{2} \sin \frac{2 x-x}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2}=\sin \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{\dot{x}}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{x}{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2}\right)=0 \\\therefore \sin \frac{x}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{x}{2}=n \pi\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা,[math]\cos \frac{3 x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \frac{3 x}{2}}{\cos \frac{3 x}{2}}=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan \frac{3 x}{2}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\Rightarrow \frac{3 x}{2}=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=2 n \pi, \frac{2}{3}\left(n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
'প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, .[math]4 g(\dot{x}) g(2 x) g(3 x)=1[/math]
 যখন, [math]0[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 \cos x \cos 2 x^{\prime} \cos 3 x=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x \cdot(2 \cos 3 x \cdot \cos x)=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x\{\cos (3 x+x)+\cos (3 x-x)\}=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math][\because 2 \cos A \cos B=\cos (A+B)+\cos (A-B)][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cdot \cos 2 x:(\cos 4 x+\cos 2 x)=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 x \cdot \cos 2 x+2 \cos ^{2} 2 x-1=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 x \cdot \cos 2 x+\cos 4 x=0\left[ \therefore \cos 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} x-1\right]\\\Rightarrow \cos 4 x(2 \cos 2 x+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 4 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=-\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow 2 \mathrm{x}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]0<\mathrm{x}<\pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে x এর মান নির্ণয়ঃ
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; \frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{3 \pi}{8}, \frac{5 \pi}{8}, \frac{7 \pi }{8} \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:

Question #119 (ID: 442441)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]P=\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\cot ^{-1} 3 \text {. }[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{m}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{n}{b}=x \text {. }[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]p=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এর সাহায্যে দেখাও যে, [math]\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 m n}{a b} \cos x+\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} x[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow & 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}\right)+\sec ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-1 \\\Rightarrow & \left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\sec \left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}^{2} \\\Rightarrow & 4^{2}+3^{2} \\\Rightarrow & 16+9 \\\Rightarrow & 25 \text { |Ans.|. }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ
[Image]
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে পাই,$\begin{aligned}P & =\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\cot ^{-1} 3 \\&\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2}\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\end{aligned}$[math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{10}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore P=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} \frac{m}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{n}{b}=x\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{m}{a} \cdot \frac{n}{b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=x\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{m n}{a b}-\sqrt{1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}+\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}\right\}=x\\\Rightarrow \frac{m n}{a b}-\sqrt{1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}+\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}=\cos x\\\Rightarrow \frac{m n}{a b}-\cos x=\sqrt{1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}+\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \left(\frac{m n}{a b}-\cos x\right)^{2}=1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}+\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 (বর্গ করে )[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-2 \cdot \frac{m n}{a b} \cos x+\cos ^{2} x=1-\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}+\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}\\\Rightarrow \frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-\frac{2 m n}{a b} \cos x+\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{m^{2} n^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}=1-\cos ^{2} x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 m n}{a b} \cos x+\frac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} x[/math]
[দেখানো হলো]

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\cot^{-1}\frac{1}{4}[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\theta=4[/math] এবং [math]\sec^{2}\theta=1+\tan^{2}\theta=1+16=17[/math]; আর [math]\phi=\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\tan^{2}\phi=\frac{1-\cos^{2}\phi}{\cos^{2}\phi}=\frac{8}{1}=8[/math], সুতরাং যোগফল = [math]17+8=25[/math]।

খ ধরো [math]\alpha=\sec^{-1}\sqrt{5}[/math] হলে [math]\cos\alpha=1/\sqrt{5}[/math] ফলে [math]\tan\alpha=2[/math] এবং [math]\alpha=\tan^{-1}2[/math]; যদি [math]\gamma=\cot^{-1}3[/math] হয় তবে [math]\tan\gamma=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] থেকে [math]\sin(2\gamma)=\dfrac{2\tan\gamma}{1+\tan^{2}\gamma}=\dfrac{3}{5}[/math], তাই [math]2\gamma=\sin^{-1}\tfrac{3}{5}[/math] বা [math]\gamma=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\tfrac{3}{5}[/math], ফলে অনুষঙ্গে থাকা দুটি পদ পরস্পর বিপরীত হবে এবং [math]P=\alpha=\tan^{-1}2[/math]।

গ ধরা যাক [math]\phi=\cos^{-1}\frac{m}{a},\ \psi=\cos^{-1}\frac{n}{b}[/math] তাই [math]\phi+\psi=x[/math], [math]\cos\phi=\frac{m}{a},\ \cos\psi=\frac{n}{b}[/math]; এখন [math]\cos x=\cos(\phi+\psi)=\cos\phi\cos\psi-\sin\phi\sin\psi[/math] প্রতিস্থাপন করলে সরলীকরণে পাওয়া যায় [math]\dfrac{m^{2}}{a^{2}}-\dfrac{2mn}{ab}\cos x+\dfrac{n^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin^{2}x[/math]।

Question #120 (ID: 442440)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১ : [math]A=\cot ^{-1} 7, B=\cot ^{-1} 3, g(A)=\cos 2 A, h(B)=\sin 4 B .[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২ : [math]f(\alpha)=\cos \alpha, g(\alpha)=\sin 2 \alpha, h(\alpha)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{-1} \tan \cot ^{-1} \sqrt{2}[/math] এর মুখ্যমান নির্ণয় কর ।
খ. উদ্দীপক-১ এ প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]A=g^{-1}\{h(B)\}[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপক-২ এর আলোকে সমাধান কর : [math]f(\alpha)+g\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)=h(\alpha)[/math] যখন, [math]-2 \pi \leq \alpha \leq 2 \pi .[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \cos ^{-1} \tan \cot ^{-1} \cdot \sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow & \cos ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} . \\\Rightarrow & \cos ^{-1} \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow & \cos ^{-1} \cos \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \frac{\pi}{4},-\frac{\pi}{4} .\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \tan \cot ^{-1} \sqrt{2}[/math]
 এর মূখ্যমান [math]=\frac{\pi}{4} \text { |Ans.| }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক-১ হতে পাই,[math]A=\cot ^{-1} 7, B=\cot ^{-1} 3, g(A)=\cos 2 A, h(B)=\sin 4 B[/math]
 [math]\therefore h(B)=\sin \left(4 \cot ^{-1} 3\right)[/math]
 [ মান বসিয়ে ]ধরি, [math]y=g(A)=\cos 2 A[/math]
এখন, [math]y=g(A)[/math]
 [math]\therefore A=\mathrm{g}^{-1}\left(y_{}\right)[/math]
আবার, [math]y=\cos 2 A[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \mathrm{~A}=\cos ^{-1} \mathrm{y} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{A}=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \mathrm{y} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{g}^{-1}(\mathrm{y})=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \mathrm{y} \\\therefore g^{-1}(\mathrm{~A})=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \mathrm{~A}\end{array}[/math]
এখন, [math]\mathrm{g}^{-1}\{\mathrm{~h}(\mathrm{~B})\}=\mathrm{g}^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(4 \cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(4 \cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(2 \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{9}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{9-1}{9}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left[\sin \left\{2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \cdot \frac{9}{8}\right)\right\}\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\right)\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{3}{4}}{1-\frac{9}{16}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{16-9}{16}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left[\sin \left\{\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{2} \cdot \frac{16}{7}\right)\right\}\right] .\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{24}{7}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}\right)\right\}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \mathrm{g}^{-1}\{\mathrm{~h}(\mathrm{~B})\}=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\therefore \cos \theta & =\frac{24}{25} \\\therefore \sin \theta & =\sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} \theta} \\& \Rightarrow \sqrt{1-\left(\frac{24}{25}\right)^{2}} \\& \Rightarrow \sqrt{\frac{625-576}{625}}=\sqrt{\frac{49}{625}}=\frac{7}{25}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\therefore \tan \frac{\theta}{2} & =\frac{\sin \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos \frac{\theta}{2}} \\& \Rightarrow \frac{2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2} \cos \frac{\theta}{2}}{2 \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}=\frac{\sin \theta}{1+\cos \theta}=\frac{\frac{7}{25}}{1+\frac{24}{25}}=\frac{7}{25} \times \frac{25}{49}=\frac{1}{7}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{1}{7}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\left[\because \theta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}\right]\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}=\cot ^{-1} 7\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathbf{B}^{-1}\{\mathrm{~h}(\mathbf{B})\}=\cot ^{-1} 7[/math]
(প্রমানিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গউদ্দীপক-২ হতে পাই : [math]f(\alpha)=\cos \alpha ; g(\alpha)=\sin 2 \alpha, h(\alpha)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(\alpha)+g\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)=h(\alpha)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \alpha+\sin 2 \cdot \frac{\alpha}{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cos \alpha+\sin \alpha=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \sin \alpha+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \cos \alpha=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2} \cdot \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় দিকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \alpha \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos \alpha \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos \alpha \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \alpha \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\alpha-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow \alpha-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3},(n \in \mathbb{Z})\\\Rightarrow \alpha=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4},(n \in \mathbb{Z}) \text{.}\\\Rightarrow \alpha=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4},(n \in \mathbb{Z})\\\therefore \alpha=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{12},(n \in \mathbb{Z})\end{array}[/math]
 [math]-2 \pi \leq \alpha \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\boldsymbol{\alpha}[/math]
 এর মান নির্ণয় :
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\alpha=-\frac{\pi}{12}, \frac{7 \pi}{12}, \frac{23 \pi}{12},-\frac{17 \pi}{12} \text { |Ans.| }[/math]


Explanation:
ক প্রথমে ধরুন [math]x=\cot^{-1}\sqrt{2}[/math], তখন [math]\tan x=1/\sqrt{2}[/math] এবং ফলে [math]\cos^{-1}\{\tan(\cot^{-1}\sqrt{2})\}=\cos^{-1}(1/\sqrt{2})=[\pi/4][/math].

খ দিচ্ছে [math]A=\cot^{-1}7,\;B=\cot^{-1}3[/math]; তাই [math]\tan A=1/7,\;\tan B=1/3[/math]. আবার [math]\cos2A=\frac{1-\tan^2A}{1+\tan^2A}=\frac{48/49}{50/49}=24/25[/math] এবং [math]\sin4B=2\sin2B\cos2B=2\cdot\frac{3}{5}\cdot\frac{4}{5}=24/25[/math], ফলে [math]g(A)=h(B)[/math]; A কেবলমাত্র [math][0,\pi/2][/math] এ আছে, তাই উপযুক্ত শাখা নিলে [math]A=g^{-1}\{h(B)\}[/math] সত্যি।

গ [math]\cos\alpha+\sin\alpha=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] লিখলে বামদিক হচ্ছে [math]\sqrt{2}\sin(\alpha+\pi/4)=1/\sqrt{2}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin(\alpha+\pi/4)=1/2[/math]. তাই [math]\alpha+\pi/4=\pi/6+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]5\pi/6+2k\pi[/math], ফলে [math]\alpha=-\pi/12+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\alpha=7\pi/12+2k\pi[/math]. [math]-2\pi\le\alpha\le2\pi[/math] বিবেচনায় সমাধানগুলো হল [math]\alpha\in\{-17\pi/12,\,-\pi/12,\,7\pi/12,\,23\pi/12\}[/math]।

Question #121 (ID: 442438)

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]a \sin x+b \cos x=1[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. সমাধান কর : [math]\tan ^{2} \theta-3 \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta+1=0[/math]
খ. [math]a=\sqrt{3}[/math] এবং [math]b = 1[/math] হলে দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর, যেখানে [math]-2 \pi
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এর আলোকে [math]f(x)+f(3 x)+f(5 x)+f(7 x)=0[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর, যেখানে [math]0


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]\tan ^{2} \theta-3 \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2} \theta-3 \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \sec ^{2} \theta-3 \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta=0 \quad\left[\because \sec ^{2} \theta-\tan ^{2} \theta=1\right]\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos ^{2} \theta}-3 \cdot \frac{1}{\sin ^{2} \theta}=0\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin ^{2} \dot{\theta}-3 \cos ^{2} \theta}{\cos ^{2} \theta \sin ^{2} \theta}=0\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta-3 \cos ^{2} \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta-3\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)=0\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta-3+3 \sin ^{2} \theta=0\\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} \theta=3\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta=\frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \sin \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \mid \text { Ans. } \mid[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে পাই,[math]a \sin x+b \cos x=1[/math]
 [math]a=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]b = 1[/math]
 হলে সমীকরণটি হতে পাই,[math]\sqrt{3} \sin x+\cos x=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin x+\frac{1}{2} \cos x=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}+1^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\cos x \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin \left(x+\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow x+\frac{\pi}{6}=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore n=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6}=0[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য[math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{4 \pi}{6}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য[math]\mathbf{n}=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{-8 \pi}{6}=-\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য[math]n=2[/math]
 হলে [math]x=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 \pi[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়[math]n=-2[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{\pi}{6}=-2 \pi[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=0, \frac{2 \pi}{3},-\frac{4 \pi}{3} \text { |Ans.| }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে পাই, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(x)+f(3 x)+f(5 x)+f(7 x)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x+\cos 3 x+\cos 5 x+\cos 7 x=0\\\Rightarrow (\cos 5 x+\cos x)+(\cos 7 x+\cos 3 x)=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{5 x+x}{2} \cos \frac{5 x-x}{2}+2 \cos \frac{7 x+3 x}{2} \cos \frac{7 x-3 x}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 3 x \cos 2 x+2 \cos 5 x \cos 2 x=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x(\cos 5 x+\cos 3 x)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]2 \cos 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 \mathrm{x}=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \mathrm{x}=(2 \mathrm{n}+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos 5 x+\cos 3 x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{5 x+3 x}{2} \cos \frac{5 x-3 x}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 x \cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos 4 x \cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos 4 x=0 \\\Rightarrow 4 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4},(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2},(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]n[/math]
 এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।[math]\mathbf{0}<\mathrm{x}<\pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে x এর মান নির্ণয়ঃ
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=\frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{3 \pi}{8}, \frac{5 \pi}{8}, \frac{7 \pi}{8}, \frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
 ও [math]\frac{\pi}{2} \cdot \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:
ক সমাধান: সমীকরণকে [math]\sin^{2}\theta=s[/math] ধরে নিরূপণ করলে [math]\dfrac{s}{1-s}-\dfrac{3}{s}+1=0[/math] থেকে [math]s=\dfrac{3}{4}খ সমাধান: [math]\sqrt{3}\sin x+\cos x=1গ সমাধান: যোগফলকে জোড়া করে সহজ করলে [math]\cos x+\cos3x+\cos5x+\cos7x=4\cos4x\cos2x\cos x</math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই কোনো একটি কারণ শূন্য হলে সমাধান; অর্থাৎ [math]\cos x=0,\,\cos2x=0</math] বা [math]\cos4x=0</math]. (উদাহরণস্বরূপ, যদি [math]0<x<2\pi</math] হয়, তাহলে সমাধানগুলোর সমষ্টি হচ্ছে [math]x=\pi/2,\,3\pi/2;</math] [math]x=\pi/4,\,3\pi/4,\,5\pi/4,\,7\pi/4;</math] এবং [math]x=\pi/8+k\pi/4\;(k=0,\dots,7)</math] সমাধানসমূহের মিল)।

Question #122 (ID: 442439)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১ : [math]\sec \alpha=\frac{p}{x}, \sec \beta=\frac{q}{y}[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২ : [math]f(x)=\sec x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 1\right)+\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. উদ্দীপক-১ এ [math]\alpha+\beta=\gamma[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{p q} \cos \gamma=\sin ^{2} \gamma .[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপক-২ এর আলোকে [math]f(x) \cdot f(3 x)+2=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 1\right)+\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)+1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right) \\\Rightarrow 1+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)\right\}^{2}+1-\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2} \\\Rightarrow 1+1^{2}+1-\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2} \\\Rightarrow 3-\frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{12-1}{4}=\frac{11}{4} \text { [Ans.] } \\\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক-১ হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{c}\sec \alpha=\frac{\mathrm{p}}{\mathrm{x}}, \\\therefore \alpha=\sec ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{p}}{\mathrm{x}}\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]\sec \beta=\frac{q}{y}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \beta=\sec ^{-1} \frac{q}{y}[/math]
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\alpha+\beta=\gamma[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow _{} \sec ^{-1} \frac{p}{x}+\sec ^{-1} \frac{q}{y}=\gamma[/math]
 [মান বসিয়ে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{p}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{{y}}{q}=\gamma \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x}{p} \cdot \frac{{y}}{q}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\gamma\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x y}{p q}-\sqrt{\left.1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}\right\}}=\gamma\right. \\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{p q}-\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}}=\cos \gamma \\\Rightarrow \frac{x y}{p q}-\cos \gamma=\sqrt{1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \left(\frac{x y}{p q}-\cos \gamma\right)^{2}=1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}[/math]
 [বর্গ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}-2 \cdot \frac{x y}{p q} \cos y+\cos ^{2} \gamma=1-\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{p^{2} q^{2}}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{p q} \cos \gamma=1-\cos ^{2} \gamma\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{p q} \cos \gamma=\sin ^{2} \gamma\left[\because \sin ^{2} \gamma+\cos ^{2} \gamma=1\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{p q} \cos \gamma=\sin ^{2} \gamma[/math]
[প্রমাণিত][math]\\[/math]
 গ“উদ্দীপক-২ হতে পাই, [math]f(x)=\sec x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}f(x) \cdot f(3 x)+2=0\\\Rightarrow \sec x \sec 3 x+2=0\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos x} \cdot \frac{1}{\cos 3 x}+2=0\\\Rightarrow \frac{1+2 \cos x \cos 3 x}{\cos x \cos 3 x}=0\\\Rightarrow 1+2 \cos x \cos 3 x=0\\\Rightarrow 1+\cos (x+3 x)+\cos (x-3 x)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos 4 x+\cos 2 x=0 \quad[\because \cos (-x)=\cos x] \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} 2 x+\cos 2 x=0 \quad\left[\because 2 \cos ^{2} x=1+\cos 2 x\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos 2 x(2 \cos 2 x+1)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x=-1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=-\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}, n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \cdot \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\cot^{-1}1=\pi/4[/math] তাই [math]\sec^{2}(\pi/4)=(\sqrt{2})^{2}=2[/math]. এছাড়া [math]\cos^{-1}(1/2)=\pi/3[/math] তাই [math]\sin^{2}(\pi/3)=(\sqrt{3}/2)^{2}=3/4[/math]. ফলে যোগফল [math]2+\tfrac{3}{4}=\tfrac{11}{4}[/math].

খ উদ্দীপক-১ থেকে [math]\cos\alpha=\tfrac{x}{p},\ \cos\beta=\tfrac{y}{q}[/math]. এবার [math]\sin^{2}(\alpha+\beta)=(\sin\alpha\cos\beta+\cos\alpha\sin\beta)^{2}[/math] প্রসার করে এবং [math]\sin^{2}\theta=1-\cos^{2}\theta[/math] ব্যবহারে সরলীকরণ করলে দেখা যায় যে বামপাশের প্রকাশই [math]\frac{x^{2}}{p^{2}}+\frac{y^{2}}{q^{2}}-\frac{2xy}{pq}\cos\gamma[/math] এবং এটি [math]\sin^{2}\gamma[/math]-র সমান হয়।

গ সমীকরণ থেকে [math]\sec x\sec 3x=-2\Rightarrow \cos x\cos 3x=-\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]. ব্যবহার করলে [math]\cos4x+\cos2x= -1[/math] যা সরলীকরণে [math]\cos2x(2\cos2x+1)=0[/math] দেয়. অতএব [math]\cos2x=0\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+k\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] বা [math]\cos2x=-\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{3}+k\pi\ \text{অথবা}\ x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+k\pi[/math], যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math].

Question:
উদ্দীপক : [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math] [math]A=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}, B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}, C=\cot ^{-1} 2, D=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29} .[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-3 \sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{3}\right)=1[/math]
খ. [math]f(\pi g(x))=g(\pi f(x))[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math] (সংশোধিত)
গ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 A+B=2(C+D)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
] কবামপক্ষ, [math]=\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-3 \sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 2\right)-3 \sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\\Rightarrow 1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 2\right)-3\left\{1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+\left\{\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}^{2}-3\left[1+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2}\right] \\\Rightarrow 1+(2)^{2}-3\left\{1+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+4-3\left(1+\frac{1}{3}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=1+4-3-1=5-4=1=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[প্রমাণিত][math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক হতে পাই, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
এবং [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ[math]\begin{array}{c}f(\pi g(x))=g(\pi f(x)) \\\Rightarrow f(\pi \cos x)=g(\pi \sin x) \\\Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos x)=\cos (\pi \sin x)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos x)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x\right)[/math]
 [সূত্র [math]\left.\cos x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm x\right)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \pi \cos x=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x\\\Rightarrow \cos x \pm \sin x=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (\cos x \pm \sin x)^{2}=\frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} x+\sin ^{2} x \pm 2 \sin x \cos x=\frac{1}{4}\\\Rightarrow 1 \pm \sin 2 x=\frac{1}{4} \quad[\sin 2 x=2 \sin x \cos x]\\\Rightarrow 1-\frac{1}{4}= \pm \sin 2 x\\\Rightarrow \frac{4-1}{4}= \pm \sin 2 x\\\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 x=\frac{3}{4}\\\Rightarrow \sin ( \pm 2 x)=\frac{3}{4}\\\Rightarrow \pm 2 x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
[দেখানো হলো][math]\\[/math]
 গ
[Image]
উদ্দীপক হতে পাই,,[math]A=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}, B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}, C=\cot ^{-1} 2, D=\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ [math]=2 \mathrm{~A}+\mathrm{B}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13} \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{3}{4}}{1-\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{2}}{1-\frac{9}{16}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{16-9}{16}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{16}{7}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{5}{13} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{24}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{12}{5}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{24}{7}+\frac{12}{5}}{1-\frac{24}{7} \cdot \frac{12}{5}}\left[\because \frac{24}{7} \times \frac{12}{5}>1\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{120+84}{35}}{\frac{35-288}{35}} \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{204}{35} \times \frac{35}{-253}\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(-\frac{204}{253}\right)\end{array}[/math]
ডানপক্ষ [math]=2(C+D)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2\left(\cot ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}\right) \\\Rightarrow 2\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{28}{29}\right) \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{28}{29}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{28}{29}} \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{29+56}{58}}{58} \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{85}{58} \times \frac{58}{30}\right) \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{85}{30}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{85}{30}}{1-\left(\frac{85}{30}\right)^{2}}\left[\because \frac{85}{30}>1\right] \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{170}{30}}{1-\frac{7225}{900}} \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{17}{3}}{\frac{900-7225}{900}} \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{17}{3} \times \frac{900}{-6325}\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(-\frac{17 \times 300}{6325}\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(-\frac{204}{253}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ = ডানপক্ষ[প্ৰমাণিত]

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক যদি [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{2}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\sin\theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] তাই [math]\csc^2\theta=5[/math]; আর যদি [math]\phi=\cot^{-1}\sqrt{3}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\tan\phi=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] এবং [math]\sec^2\phi=\frac{4}{3}[/math]; তাই [math]5-3\cdot\frac{4}{3}=1[/math].

খ শর্ত থেকে [math]\sin(\pi\cos x)=\cos(\pi\sin x)=\sin\bigl(\pi(\tfrac{1}{2}-\sin x)\bigr)[/math], তাই বা তো [math]\cos x+\sin x=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] বা [math]\cos x-\sin x=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math]; তারা স্কোয়ার করে পাওয়া যায় [math]\sin2x=\mp\frac{3}{4}[/math], সুতরাং [math]x=\pm\frac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{4}[/math]।

গ [math]A=\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{5}\Rightarrow\tan A=\frac{3}{4}[/math] এবং [math]B=\cos^{-1}\frac{5}{13}\Rightarrow\tan B=\frac{12}{5}[/math] থেকে [math]\tan(2A+B)=-\frac{204}{253}[/math]; অন্যদিকে [math]C=\cot^{-1}2\Rightarrow\tan C=\frac{1}{2}[/math], [math]D=\tan^{-1}\frac{28}{29}[/math] হলে [math]\tan(C+D)=\frac{17}{6}[/math] এবং ফলে [math]\tan2(C+D)=-\frac{204}{253}[/math]; তাই কোণদ্বয় সমান এবং [math]2A+B=2(C+D)[/math]।

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]q=\cos ^{-1} p[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math] এর মান বের কর ।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে [math]q=\cos ^{-1} p[/math] এর [math]-1 \leq p \leq 1[/math] ব্যবধিতে লেখচিত্র অংকন কর।
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে [math]2\{f(x)\}^{2}+5 f(x)-3=0[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right) \\& \Rightarrow \left(\frac{\frac{3+2}{6}}{\frac{6}{6-1}}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{6}{6}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1} \cdot\left(\frac{5}{6} \times \frac{6}{5}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1} 1 \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{4} .\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \tan \left(\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)^{}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} \text { [Ans.] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে পাই, [math]\mathrm{q}=\cos ^{-1} \mathrm{p}[/math]
, যেখানে, [math]-1 \leq p \leq 1[/math]
আবার, [math]q=\cos ^{-1} p[/math]
 এর অর্থ [math]p=\cos q[/math]
 (এখানে কেবল মূখ্য মান ধরা হবে). সুতরাং যখন, [math]-1 \leq p \leq 1[/math]
 তখন [math]0 \leq q \leq \pi[/math]
 [math]-1 \leq p \leq 1[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে, [math]p[/math]
 এর মান নিয়ে তার প্রতিসঙ্গী [math]q[/math]
 এর মানগুলো নিচের ছকে দেওয়া হলো।[math]\begin{array}{|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|c|}\hline \mathrm{q} & 180^{\circ} & 150^{\circ} & 120^{\circ} & 90^{\circ} & 60^{\circ} & 30^{\circ} & 0 \\\hline \mathrm{p} . & -1 & -0.87 & -0.5 & 0 & 0.5 & 0.87 & 1 \\\hline\end{array}[/math]
ছক কাগজের x অক্ষ বরাবর ক্ষুদ্রতম বর্গের [math]10[/math]
 ঘর [math]= 1[/math]
 একক এবং [math]y[/math]
 অক্ষ বরাবর [math]1[/math]
 ঘর [math]=5^{\circ}[/math]
 ধরে প্রাপ্ত [math]p[/math]
 ও [math]q[/math]
 এর মানগুলো বসিয়ে [math]q=\cos ^{-1} p[/math]
 এর লেখচিত্র পাওয়া যায়।
[Image]
[math]\\[/math]
 গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে পাই, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]2\{f(x)\}^{2}+5 f(x)-3=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} x+5 \sin x-3=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} x+6 \sin x-\sin x-3=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x(\sin x+3)-1(\sin x+3)=0 \\\Rightarrow (\sin x+3)(2 \sin x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]2 \sin x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin x=1 \\\Rightarrow \sin x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin x=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin x+3=0[/math]
কিন্তু , [math]\sin x 
eq-3[/math]
[কারণ [math]\sin x[/math]
 এর সর্বনিম্ন মান [math]- 1[/math]
 ][math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক টানা সূত্র ব্যবহার করে [math]\tan(A+B)=\frac{\tan A+\tan B}{1-\tan A\tan B}[/math], তাই [math]\tan\Big(\tan^{-1}\tfrac12+\tan^{-1}\tfrac13\Big)=\frac{\tfrac12+\tfrac13}{1-\tfrac12\cdot\tfrac13}=\frac{5/6}{5/6}=1[/math]; দুইারকেই মূল পরিসরে আছে তাই যোগফল [math]\pi/4[/math]।  
খ [math]q=\cos^{-1}p[/math] হলে [math]p\in[-1,1][/math] এ [math]q[/math] monotonically decreasing; [math]q(1)=0,\;q(0)=\pi/2,\;q(-1)=\pi[/math] — আরককোসাইন ক্রমশ নিচে নামে।  
গ [math]y=\sin x[/math] ধরি, সমীকরণ হলো [math]2y^2+5y-3=0[/math] যার মূল [math]y=\tfrac12,\;y=-3[/math]; [math]y=-3[/math] গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়, তাই [math]\sin x=\tfrac12[/math] থেকে [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]x=\tfrac{5\pi}{6}+2k\pi\;(k\in\mathbb{Z})[/math]।

Question #125 (ID: 442435)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২: [math]A=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে , [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{15}\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{13}\right)=30[/math]
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর আলোকে [math]2 f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \cdot f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 \theta\right)+1=0[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান কর।
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ থেকে দেখাও যে, [math]A=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{27}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{15}\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \left\{\sec \left(\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{15}\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\operatorname{cosec}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{13}\right)\right\}^{2} \\\Rightarrow \left\{\sec \left(\sec ^{-1} 4\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\operatorname{cosec}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{13}\right)\right\}^{2}\end{array}[/math]
[চিত্র (i) হতে পাই]
[Image]
[math]=(4)^{2}+\left\{\operatorname{cosec}\left(\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{14}\right)\right\}^{2}[/math]
[চিত্র (ii) হতে পাই ]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 16+(\sqrt{14})^{2} \\\Rightarrow 16+14 \\\Rightarrow 30\end{array}[/math]
[প্রমাণিত][math]\\[/math]
 খদৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে পাই, [math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]2 f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right): f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 \theta\right)+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \cdot \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 \theta\right)+1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta \cos 3 \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos (\theta+3 \theta)+\cos (\theta-3 \theta)=-1 \\\quad[\because 2 \cos A \cdot \cos B=\cos (A+B)+\cos (A-B)]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 4 \theta+\cos 2 \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow 1+\cos 4 \theta+\cos 2 \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} 2 \theta+\cos 2 \theta=0\left[\because 1 \cdot+\cos 2 \theta=2 \cos ^{2} \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta(2 \cos 2 \theta+1)=0 \text {. }\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}, n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে পাই,[math]\text { - } A=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sin \theta=\frac{3}{5} \\\therefore \cos \theta=\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} \theta}=\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{3}{5}\right)^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sqrt{1-\frac{9}{25}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{\frac{25-9}{25}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{\frac{16}{25}}=\frac{4}{5}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{\sin \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2} \cos \frac{\theta}{2}}{2 \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [ডানপক্ষের লব ও হরকে [math]2 \cos \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা গুণ করে ][math]\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{\sin \theta}{1+\cos \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{\frac{3}{5}}{1+\frac{4}{5}}\\\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{3}{5} \times \frac{5}{9}\\\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{1}{3}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\\\therefore \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
এখানে, [math]A=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{i}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3-2}{6}}{\frac{6+1}{6}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l} \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{6} \times \frac{6}{7}\right)+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{7}+\frac{1}{4}}{1-\frac{1}{7} \cdot \frac{1}{4}}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{4+7}{28}}{\frac{28-1}{28}}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{11}{28} \times \frac{28}{27}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{27}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore A=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{27}[/math]
[প্রমাণিত]

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক কর্তন: [math]\sec^{2}x=1+\tan^{2}x[/math] এবং [math]\cosec^{2}x=1+\cot^{2}x[/math] ব্যবহার করে, [math]x=\tan^{-1}\sqrt{15}\Rightarrow \sec^{2}x=1+15=16[/math] এবং [math]y=\cot^{-1}\sqrt{13}\Rightarrow \cosec^{2}y=1+13=14[/math]; তাই যোগফল [math]16+14=30[/math].

খ কর্তন: [math]f(\theta)=\sin\theta[/math] হলে [math]f\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\bigr)=\cos\theta[/math] ও [math]f\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-3\theta\bigr)=\cos3\theta[/math], ফলে সমীকরণটি হয় [math]2\cos\theta\cos3\theta+1=0[/math]. এখানে [math]2\cos\theta\cos3\theta=\cos4\theta+\cos2\theta[/math], তাই [math]\cos4\theta+\cos2\theta+1=0\Rightarrow \cos2\theta(2\cos2\theta+1)=0[/math], অতএব [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+n\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] বা [math]\cos2\theta=-\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{3}+n\pi[/math] বা [math]\theta=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}+n\pi,\;n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

গ কর্তন: [math]\cosec^{-1}\sqrt{5}=\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] হলে ঐ কোণটির ট্যান [math]=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], এবং [math]\sin^{-1}\tfrac{3}{5}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{3}{4}[/math] হওয়ায় তার অর্ধেকের ট্যান [math]=\tfrac{1}{3}[/math]. তাই [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\tan^{-1}\tfrac{3}{4}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{7}[/math] এবং এটি [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{4}[/math] যোগ করলে [math]\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{7}+\tan^{-1}\tfrac{1}{4}=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{11}{27}[/math], ফলে [math]A=\tan^{-1}\tfrac{11}{27}[/math]।

Question #126 (ID: 442434)

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ :


[Image]


দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]4 \cos x \cos 2 x \cdot \cos 3 x=1[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে; [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এ [math]\angle A C B=2 x[/math] হলে [math]\cot ^{-1} 3-x[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এর সমীকরণটি [math]0


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\\\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right)\\\Rightarrow x=\cos \sin ^{-1} y\\\Rightarrow x=\cos \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\\\Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \text{.}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2}[/math]
 [বর্গ করে][math]\therefore \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1[/math]
[ দেখানো হলো ]
[Image]
[math]\\[/math]
 খ
[Image]
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে পাই,এখানে, [math]\angle A B C=[/math]
 এক সমকোণএবং [math]\angle A C B=2 x[/math]
 [ সূক্ষ্মকোণ ][math]\therefore \triangle \mathrm{ABC}[/math]
 এ [math]\mathrm{AC}^{2}=\mathrm{AB}^{2}+\mathrm{BC}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \mathrm{BC}^{2}=\mathrm{AC}^{2}-\mathrm{AB}^{2} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{BC}^{2}=5^{2}-3^{2} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{BC}^{2}=25-9 \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{BC}^{2} \doteq 16 \\\therefore \mathrm{BC}=4 \\\therefore \tan \angle \mathrm{ACB}=\frac{\mathrm{AB}}{\mathrm{BC}} \\\Rightarrow \tan 2 \mathrm{x}=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow 2 \mathrm{x}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
প্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]\cot ^{-1} 3-x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{9}}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{{-1}} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{9-1}{9}}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \cdot\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{8}\right)-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \text { 0 [Ans.] }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}4 \cos x \cdot \cos 2 x \cdot \cos 3 x=1 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x \cdot(2 \cos 3 x \cdot \cos x)=1 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x\{\cos (3 x+x)+\cos (3 x-x)\}=1 \\{[\because 2 \cos A \cos B=\cos (A+B)+\cos (A-B)]}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos 2 x(\cos 4 x+\cos 2 x)=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 x \cos 2 x+2 \cos ^{2} 2 x-1=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 4 x \cos 2 x+\cos 4 x=0 \quad\left[\because 2 \cos ^{2} x-1=\cos 2 x\right]\\\Rightarrow \cos 4 x(2 \cos 2 x+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 4 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} . \\\therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]0<\mathbf{x}<\pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে x এর মান নির্ণয়:
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{3 \pi}{8}, \frac{5 \pi}{8} ; \frac{7 \pi}{8} \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\sin^{-1}x,\ \beta=\sin^{-1}y[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math], ফলে [math]\beta=\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha[/math] এবং [math]y=\sin\beta=\cos\alpha[/math]. তাই [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=\sin^{2}\alpha+\cos^{2}\alpha=1[/math] (এখানে ধরে নেওয়া হয়েছে [math]x,y\in[-1,1][/math])
