Question #127 (ID: 442432)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin \tan ^{-1} \cos \sec ^{-1} y[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]f\left(\pi f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi f(\theta)\right)[/math] হলে, দেখাও যে, [math]\theta= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4^{}}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]1+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-6 x\right)=0[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \sin \tan ^{-1} \cos \sec ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow & \sin \tan ^{-1} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{y} \\\Rightarrow & \sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{y} \\\Rightarrow & \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+y^{2}}} \\\Rightarrow & \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+y^{2}}} \text { [Ans.] }\end{aligned}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}f\left(\pi f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi f(\theta)\right) \\\Rightarrow f\left(\pi \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow f(\pi \cos \theta)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow \pi \cos \theta=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta \pm \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (\cos \theta \pm \sin \theta)^{2}=\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} \theta+\sin ^{2} \theta \pm 2 \sin \theta \cos \theta=\frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow 1 \pm \sin 2 \theta=\frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow 1-\frac{1}{4}= \pm \sin 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 \theta=1-\frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow \pm \sin 2 \theta=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \sin ( \pm 2 \theta)=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \pm 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta= \pm \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
[দেখানো হলো][math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ[math]\begin{array}{l}1+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-6 x\right)=0\\\Rightarrow 1+\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)+\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-4 x\right)+\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-6 x\right)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos 2 x+\cos 4 x+\cos 6 x=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x+2 \cos \frac{4 x+6 x}{2} \cos \frac{4 x-6 x}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cdot \cos ^{2} x+2 \cos 5 x \cos (-x)=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x+2 \cos 5 x \cos x=0 \quad[\because \cos (-\theta)=\cos \theta]\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x(\cos x+\cos 5 x)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]2 \cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 এখানে, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos x+\cos 5 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{x+5 x}{2} \cos \frac{x-5 x}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos 3 x \cos (-2 x)=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 3 x \cos 2 x=0 \quad[\because \cos (-\theta)=\cos \theta]\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 3 x=0\Rightarrow 3 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 এখানে, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 এখানে, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 অথবা, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
এখানে, [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2},(2 n+1)^{\frac{\pi}{4}},(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 যখন, [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z} \mid \text { Ans. } \mid[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\sec^{-1}y=\theta\Rightarrow \cos\theta=1/y[/math] হলে [math]\sin\big(\tan^{-1}(\cos(\sec^{-1}y))\big)=\sin\big(\tan^{-1}(1/y)\big)=[\mathfrac{1/y}{\sqrt{1+(1/y)^2}}]=\frac{\operatorname{sgn}(y)}{\sqrt{y^2+1}}[/math], বিশেষ করে যদি [math]y>0[/math] হয় তখন [math]1/\sqrt{y^2+1}[/math] হয়।

খ সূত্রটি বসালে [math]\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\cos(\pi\sin\theta)[/math] হয়, অর্থাৎ [math]\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\pi\sin\theta\big)[/math] থেকে দুটি ক্ষেত্রে মিলতে পারে: [math]\cos\theta=\tfrac12\mp\sin\theta[/math] । উভয় ক্ষেত্র বর্গ করে ফলায় [math]\sin2\theta=\pm\tfrac{3}{4}[/math], ফলে [math]\theta=\pm\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\tfrac{3}{4}[/math]।

গ [math]f(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-a)=\cos a[/math] ব্যবহার করে [math]1+\cos2x+\cos4x+\cos6x=0[/math] পাওয়া যায়; এটিকে [math]c=\cos2x[/math] ধরে সরল করলে [math]2c(2c^2+c-1)=0[/math] হয়, ফলে [math]\cos2x=0,\;{1\over2},\;-1[/math]. অতএব সাধারণ সমাধান হল [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+k\frac{\pi}{2},\;x=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{6}+k\pi,\;x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+k\pi\ (k\in\mathbb Z)[/math]।

Question #128 (ID: 442433)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos \left(2 \cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(\theta))+\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর : [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)+f(\theta)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)+f(2 \theta)[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}& \cos \left(2 \cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}\right)^{} \\\Rightarrow & \cos \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \cos \left\{\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\left(\frac{2}{3}\right)^{2}}{1+\left(\frac{2}{3}\right)^{2}}\right\}\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\right] \\\Rightarrow & \frac{1-\frac{4}{9}}{1+\frac{4}{9}} \\\Rightarrow & \frac{9-4}{9+4} \\\Rightarrow & \frac{5}{9} \times \frac{9}{13}=\frac{5}{13} \text { |Ans.l }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ [math]=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(\theta))+\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\sin \left(\frac{\pi^{\prime}}{2}-2 \theta\right)}\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
[ সূত্র [math]\left.\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y^{}=\sin ^{-1}\left(x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}+y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{2 \sin ^{2} \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\right\} \\\left[2 \sin ^{2} \theta=1-\cos 2 \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \cdot \sqrt{2} \sin \theta+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos 2 \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(2 \sin ^{2} \theta+1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \sin \frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ [প্রমাণিত][math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ[math]\begin{array}{l}f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)+f(\theta)=f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)+f(2 \theta) \\\Rightarrow \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)+\sin \theta=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)+\sin 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta+\sin \theta=\cos 2 \theta+\sin 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta-\sin 2 \theta=\cos 2 \theta-\cos \theta \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{\theta+2 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta-2 \theta}{2}=2 \sin \frac{2 \theta+\theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta-2 \theta}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \sin C-\sin D=2 \cos \frac{C+D}{2} \sin \frac{C-D}{2}\right][/math]
[ এবং [math]\left.\cos C-\cos D=2 \sin \frac{C+D}{2} \sin \frac{D-C}{2}\right][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \left(-\frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \left(-\frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow -\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=-\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}[\because \sin (-\theta)=\sin \theta][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে – 1 দ্বারা গুণ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2} \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\theta}{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}-\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}\right)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}-\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}=\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \frac{3 \theta}{2}}{\cos \frac{3 \theta}{2}}=1[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin \frac{\theta}{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan \frac{3 \theta}{2}=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \frac{3 \theta}{2}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{3 \theta}{2}=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathbf{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \cdot \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=2 n \pi, \frac{2}{3}\left(n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \mid \text { Ans.| }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\alpha=\cot^{-1}\frac{3}{2}\Rightarrow \tan\alpha=\frac{2}{3}[/math], এবং [math]\cos2\alpha=\frac{1-\tan^2\alpha}{1+\tan^2\alpha}=\frac{1-\tfrac{4}{9}}{1+\tfrac{4}{9}}=\frac{5}{13}[/math], তাই মান [math]\tfrac{5}{13}[/math].

খ [math]\sqrt{2}f(\theta)=\sqrt{2}\sin\theta[/math] এবং [math]\sqrt{f(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-2\theta)}=\sqrt{\cos2\theta}[/math]; কারণ [math](\sqrt{2}\sin\theta)^2+(\sqrt{\cos2\theta})^2=2\sin^2\theta+\cos2\theta=1[/math] এবং উভয় ঘনমূল অঋণাত্মক হলে [math]\arcsin a+\arcsin b=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] প্রয়োগ করলে সমীকরণটি সত্য।

গ [math]\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)+\sin\theta=\cos\theta+\sin\theta[/math] ও [math]\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-2\theta)+\sin2\theta=\cos2\theta+\sin2\theta[/math]; তাই সমীকরণটি হলে [math]\sqrt{2}\sin(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4})=\sqrt{2}\sin(2\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4})[/math], মানে [math]\sin A=\sin B[/math] থেকে (i) [math]\theta=2n\pi[/math] বা (ii) [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{6}+\tfrac{2n\pi}{3}[/math], যেখানে [math]n\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #129 (ID: 442428)

Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]2 \sin ^{2} \theta-2=\cos 2 \theta[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]f(y)=\tan ^{-1} {y} .[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মুখ্যমান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর সমীকরণ সমাধান কর যেখানে [math]-2 \pi \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-2 হতে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan \{2 f(x)\}=2 \tan \left\{f(x)+f\left(x^{3}\right)\right\}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}\cos ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\right) & =\cos ^{-1}\left\{\cos \left( \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}\right)\right\} \\& \Rightarrow \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\right)[/math]
 এর মূখ্যমান [math]=\frac{2 \pi}{3} \text { |Ans.| }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদৃশ্যকল্প- ১ হতে[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \sin ^{2} \theta-2=\cos 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta-2=1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta \quad\left[\because 1-\cos 2 \theta=2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta+2 \sin ^{2} \theta=1+2 \\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} \theta=3\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \sin \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
এখন, [math]-2 \pi \leq \theta \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মান নির্ণয় :
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{3},-\frac{2 \pi}{3},-\frac{4 \pi}{3},-\frac{5 \pi}{3}, \frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \frac{4 \pi}{3}, \frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\text { |Ans.| }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে,[math]f(y)=\tan ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 এবং [math]f\left(x^{3}\right)=\tan ^{-1} x^{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\text { L.H.S. } & =\tan \{2 \mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\} \\& \Rightarrow \tan \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}\right) \\& \Rightarrow \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}\right)=\frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}} \\\text { R.H.S. } & =2 \tan \left\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})+\mathrm{f}\left(\mathrm{x}^{3}\right)\right\} \\& \Rightarrow 2 \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}^{3}\right)\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow2 \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+x^{3}}{1-x^{4}} \quad\left[\because \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=2 \frac{x\left(1+x^{2}\right)}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)\left(1-x^{2}\right)} \\\Rightarrow \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\\\therefore \text{ L.H.S. = R.H.S. [Proved]}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ক [math]\cos^{-1}\!(-\tfrac{1}{2})[/math] এর মুখ্যমান হলো [math]\frac{2\pi}{3}[/math], কারণ মুখ্যশ্রেনী [math][0,\pi][/math] এবং [math]\cos\frac{2\pi}{3}=-\tfrac{1}{2}[/math].

খ সমীকরণটি সরল করলে [math]-2\cos^{2}\theta=\cos2\theta[/math] হয়, যা দিয়ে পাওয়া যায় [math]\cos^{2}\theta=\tfrac{1}{4} অর্থাৎ [math]\cos\theta=\pm\tfrac{1}{2}; তাই সমাধানসমূহ হল [math]\theta=\pm\frac{\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\pm\frac{2\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math], [math]-2\pi\le\theta\le2\piগ ধরে নিন [math]a=f(x)=\tan^{-1}x[/math] এবং [math]b=f(x^{3})=\tan^{-1}x^{3}; তখন [math]\tan\{2a\}=\dfrac{2\tan a}{1-\tan^{2}a}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^{2}}</math] এবং [math]2\tan\{a+b\}=2\cdot\dfrac{\tan a+\tan b}{1-\tan a\tan b}=2\cdot\dfrac{x+x^{3}}{1-x^{4}}=\dfrac{2x(1+x^{2})}{1-x^{4}}</math], এবং প্রথমটি গুণে বিভাজক [math](1+x^{2})[/math] যোগ করলে এগুলো সমান হয়, তাই সূত্রটি সঠিক।

Question #130 (ID: 442429)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Mymensingh Board | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\cos x[/math] একটি ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশন ।

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x=\frac{1}{y}[/math] [যেখানে, [math]x>0, y>0,0
খ. যদি [math]f^{-1}(2 x)+f^{-1}(2 y)=\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=\frac{1}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]f(x)+\sqrt{3} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে,[math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [যেখানে, [math]x>0, y>0,0[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x \dot{y}}=\tan \frac{\pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\infty\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y}{1-x y}=\frac{1}{0}\\\Rightarrow 1-x y=0\\\Rightarrow x y=1\\\therefore x=\frac{1}{y} \text{ [Showed]}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খধরি, [math]y=f(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{f}^{-1}(\mathrm{y})[/math]
এখন, [math]y=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\cos ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow f^{-1}(y)=\cos ^{-1} y \\\therefore f^{-1}(x)=\cos ^{-1} x \\\therefore f^{-1}(2 x)=\cos ^{-1} 2 x\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]f^{-1}(2 y)=\cos ^{-1} 2 y[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]f^{-1}(2 x)+f^{-1}(2 y)=\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} 2 x+\cos ^{-1}, 2 y=\frac{3 \pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{4 x y-\sqrt{\left(1-4 x^{2}\right)\left(1-4 y^{2}\right)}\right\}=\frac{3 \pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\left.\left[\because \cos ^{-1} p+\cos ^{-1} q=\cos ^{-1}\left\{p q-\sqrt{\left(1-p^{2}\right)\left(1-q^{2}\right.}\right)\right\}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 x y-\sqrt{1-4 y^{2}-4 x^{2}+16 x^{2} y^{2}}=\cos \frac{3 \pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow 4 x y-\sqrt{1-4 x^{2}-4 y^{2}+16 x^{2} y^{2}}=0\\\Rightarrow 4 x y=\sqrt{1-4 x^{2}-4 y^{2}+16 x^{2} y^{2}}\\\Rightarrow 16 x^{2} y^{2}=1-4 x^{2}-4 y^{2}+16 x^{2} y^{2}\\\Rightarrow 4 \mathrm{x}^{2}+4 \mathrm{y}^{2}=1\\\Rightarrow 4\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=\frac{1}{4}\\\text{[Showed]}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin x[/math]
এখন, [math]f(x)+\sqrt{3} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos x+\sqrt{3} \sin x=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \frac{1}{2}+\sin x \cdot \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{4}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin x \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{3} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{3}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=2 n \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{12} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[\text { Ans. }][/math]


Explanation:
ক [math]\tan^{-1}x+\tan^{-1}y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে দুপক্ষের টানজেন্ট নিলে [math]\tan(\tan^{-1}x+\tan^{-1}y)=\tan\frac{\pi}{2}[/math], বাম পাশে [math]\frac{x+y}{1-xy}[/math] এবং ডান দিকে অসীম—অতএব হরফের নামাকার শূন্য হওয়া উচিত, ফলে [math]1-xy=0\Rightarrow xy=1[/math] এবং ধনাত্মক হলে [math]x=\frac{1}{y}[/math]। 

খ [math]f^{-1}(2x)+f^{-1}(2y)=\frac{3\pi}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\arccos(2x)+\arccos(2y)=\frac{3\pi}{2}[/math] ধরে [math]A=\arccos(2x),\;B=\arccos(2y)[/math] হলে [math]\cos(A+B)=\cos\frac{3\pi}{2}=0[/math], তাই [math](2x)(2y)=\sin A\sin B=\sqrt{1-4x^2}\sqrt{1-4y^2}[/math] (arccos-এর রেঞ্জে সাইন >=0), বর্গ করে সরল করলে পাওয়া যায় [math]x^2+y^2=\frac{1}{4}[/math]। 

গ [math]\cos x+\sqrt{3}\cos\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sqrt{2}[/math] এ [math]\cos\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin x[/math] বসালে [math]\cos x+\sqrt{3}\sin x=\sqrt{2}[/math]; এটিকে রূপান্তর করলে [math]2\cos\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{3}\bigr)=\sqrt{2}[/math], তাই [math]\cos\bigl(x-\tfrac{\pi}{3}\bigr)=\tfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math] এবং সমাধান হল [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{12}+2k\pi\ \text{বা}\ x=\tfrac{7\pi}{12}+2k\pi,\ k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #131 (ID: 442430)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\cos x ; h(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর : [math]\cot ^{-1}(\tan 2 \varphi)+\cot ^{-1}(-\tan 3 \varphi)=\varphi[/math]
খ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে সমাধান কর : [math](2+\sqrt{3}) f(2 \theta)=1-f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)[/math]
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 h\left(\frac{\sqrt{a-b}}{\sqrt{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a f(\theta)}{a+b f(\theta)}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\cot ^{-1}(\tan 2 \varphi)+\cot ^{-1}(-\tan 3 \varphi)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\tan 2 \varphi}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{-\tan 3 \varphi}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}(\cot 2 \varphi)+\tan ^{-1}(-\cot 3 \varphi)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\cot 2 \varphi-\cot 3 \varphi}{1+\cot 2 \varphi \cot 3 \varphi}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{\cos 2 \varphi}{\sin 2 \varphi}-\frac{\cos 3 \varphi}{\sin 3 \varphi}}{1+\frac{\cos 2 \varphi}{\sin 2 \varphi} \cdot \frac{\cos 3 \varphi}{\sin 3 \varphi}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{\sin 3 \varphi \cos 2 \varphi-\cos 3 \varphi \sin 2 \varphi}{\sin 2 \varphi \sin 3 \varphi}}{\frac{\sin 3 \varphi \sin 2 \varphi+\cos 3 \varphi \cos 2 \varphi}{\sin 2 \varphi \sin 3 \varphi}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sin 3 \varphi \cos 2 \varphi-\cos 3 \varphi \sin 2 \varphi}{\cos 3 \varphi \cos 2 \varphi+\sin 3 \varphi \sin 2 \varphi}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{\sin (3 \varphi-2 \varphi)}{\cos (3 \varphi-2 \varphi)}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sin \varphi}{\cos \varphi}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}(\tan \varphi)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \varphi=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ [প্রমাণিত][math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \mathrm{f}(2 \theta)=\cos 2 \theta \\\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)\end{array}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{c}(2+\sqrt{3}) f(2 \theta)=1-f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3}) \cos 2 \theta=1-\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3}) \cos 2 \theta=1-\sin 2 \theta\\\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3})\left(\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)=\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta-2 \sin \theta \cos \theta\\\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3})\left(\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)=(\cos \theta-\sin \theta)^{2}\\\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3})(\cos \theta+\sin \theta)(\cos \theta-\sin \theta)-(\cos \theta-\sin \theta)^{2}=0\\\Rightarrow (\cos \theta-\sin \theta)\{(2+\sqrt{3})(\cos \theta+\sin \theta)-(\cos \theta-\sin \theta)\}=0\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sin \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\cos \theta\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা , [math](2+\sqrt{3})(\cos \theta+\sin \theta)-(\cos \theta-\sin \theta)=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow (2+\sqrt{3})(\cos \theta+\sin \theta)=(\cos \theta \sim \sin \theta) \\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta+\sin \theta}{\cos \theta-\sin \theta}=\frac{\cdot}{2+\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta+\sin \theta+\cos \theta-\sin \theta}{\cos \theta+\sin \theta-\cos \theta+\sin \theta}=\frac{1+2+\sqrt{3}}{1-2-\sqrt{3}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষকে যোজন-বিয়োজন করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{2 \cos \theta}{2 \cdot \sin \theta}=\frac{3+\sqrt{3}}{-1-\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \cot \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}(1+\sqrt{3})}{-(1+\sqrt{3})} \\\Rightarrow \cot \theta=-\sqrt{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=-\tan \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
এবং [math]h(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=2 h\left(\frac{\sqrt{a-b}}{\sqrt{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{a-b}}{\sqrt{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{a-b}{a+b} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{1+\frac{a-b}{a+b} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \quad\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\right][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{(a-b)}{(a+b)} \frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}{1+\frac{(a-b)}{(a+b)}\frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{(a+b) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-(a-b) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{(a+b) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+(a-b) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{a \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-a \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{a \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+b \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+a \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-b \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{b\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+a\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)}{a\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+b\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a \cos \theta}{a+b \cos \theta} \quad\left[\because \cos 2 \theta=\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a f(\theta)}{a+b f(\theta)} \quad[\because f(x)=\cos x]\end{array}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore 2 h\left(\frac{\sqrt{a-b}}{\sqrt{a+b}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\cos ^{-1} \frac{b+a f(\theta)}{a+b f(\theta)}[/math]
[প্রমাণিত]

Explanation:
ক সমাধান: [math]a=\tan2\varphi,\; b=-\tan3\varphi[/math] ধরলে সূত্র [math]\cot^{-1}a+\cot^{-1}b=\cot^{-1}\frac{ab-1}{a+b}[/math] প্রযোজ্য; এখানে [(ab-1)/(a+b)] সরলীকরণ করলে [math]\cot\varphi[/math] পাওয়া যায় (কারণ ত্রিকোণমিতিক পরিচিতি ব্যবহার করে নূন্যাঙ্ক গুণাবলী রূপান্তর করা যায়), ফলে সমষ্টি [math]\cot^{-1}(\cot\varphi)=\varphi[/math]। 

খ সমাধান: [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math] বসালে [math](2+\sqrt3)\cos2\theta=1-\sin2\theta[/math] হয়; [math]t=2\theta[/math] ধরে [math](2+\sqrt3)\cos t+\sin t=1[/math] কে [math]R\cos(t-\alpha)[/math] আকারে লিখলে [math]\tan\alpha=2-\sqrt3=\tan\frac{\pi}{12}[/math] ও [math]\cos(t-\alpha)=1/R=\cos\frac{5\pi}{12}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, ফলে [math]t=\frac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]t=-\frac{\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}+k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=-\frac{\pi}{6}+k\pi[/math]। 

গ প্রমাণ: [math]t=\tan\frac{\theta}{2}[/math] নিধারণ করে বামে থাকা হালফাংশনে [math]k=\sqrt{\frac{a-b}{a+b}}[/math] হলে [math]\cos\big(2\arctan(kt)\big)=\frac{1-k^2t^2}{1+k^2t^2}[/math] হয়; অন্যদিকে ডানদিকের ভগ্নাংশে [math]\cos\theta=\frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}[/math] ব্যবহার করে সরলীকরণ করলে একই অনুপাত [math]\frac{b+a\cos\theta}{a+b\cos\theta}=\frac{1-k^2t^2}{1+k^2t^2}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, অতএব সমীকরণটি সত্য।

Question #132 (ID: 442431)

Question:
[math]f(a)=\tan ^{-1} a, g(a)=\sin a[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]f\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)+f\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর ।
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]2 f\left(\sqrt{\frac{x-y}{x+y}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\sec ^{-1} \frac{x+y g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}{y+x g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর [math]g\left(\frac{\pi^{}}{2}-x\right)+g(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(a)=\tan ^{-1} a[/math]
প্রদত্ত রাশি, [math]f\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)+f\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{5}}{1-\frac{1}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{5}} \cdot\left[\because \frac{1}{3} \times \frac{1}{5}খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{a})=\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}[/math]
এবং [math]g(a)=\sin a[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\therefore g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) & =\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \\& \Rightarrow \cos \theta\end{aligned}[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=2 f\left(\sqrt{\frac{x-y}{x+y}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{x-y}}{\sqrt{x+y}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{x-y}{x+y} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{1+\frac{x-y}{x+y} \tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \quad\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\right][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\frac{(x-y)}{(x+y)} \cdot \frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}{1+\frac{(x-y)}{(x+y)} \cdot \frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{(x+y) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-(x-y) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{(x+y) \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{z}+(x-y) \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{x \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+y \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-x \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+y \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{x \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+y \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+x \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-y \sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{y\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+x\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)}{x\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}+\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)+y\left(\cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}-\sin ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}\right)} \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{y+x \cos \theta}{x+y \cos \theta}\left[\because \cos 2 \theta=\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \sec ^{-1} \frac{x+y \cos \theta}{y+x \cos \theta} \\\Rightarrow \sec ^{-} \frac{x+y g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}{y+x g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}\left[\because g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\cos \theta\right] .\end{array}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore 2 f\left(\sqrt{\frac{x-y}{x+y}} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}\right)=\sec ^{-1} \frac{x+y g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}{y+x g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত)[math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(a)=\sin a[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)+g(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)+\sin x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cos x+\sin x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \sin x+\cos x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \sin x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \cos x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2} \cdot \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3},(n \in \mathbb{Z}) \\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4},(n \in \mathbb{Z})\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{4} ;(n \in \mathbb{Z}) \\\therefore x=2 n \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{12} ;(n \in \mathbb{Z})\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=2 n \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{12},(n \in \mathbb{Z}) \quad \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক f(1/3)+f(1/5)=arctan(1/3)+arctan(1/5) এবং arctan যোগসূত্র ব্যবহার করলে [math]\arctan u+\arctan v=\arctan\frac{u+v}{1-uv}[/math], ফলে [math]\arctan\frac{1/3+1/5}{1-(1/3)(1/5)}=\arctan\frac{4}{7}[/math].

খ এখানে [math]g(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\cos\theta[/math]; যদি [math]u=\sqrt{\frac{x-y}{x+y}}\tan\frac{\theta}{2}[/math] ধরে নি, তাহলে [math]2\arctan u=\phi[/math] হলে [math]\sec\phi=\frac{1+u^2}{1-u^2}[/math]. আর [math]u^2=\frac{x-y}{x+y}\tan^2\frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{x-y}{x+y}\frac{1-\cos\theta}{1+\cos\theta}[/math] বসিয়ে সরলায়ন করলে [math]\frac{1+u^2}{1-u^2}=\frac{x+y\cos\theta}{y+x\cos\theta}[/math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই সমীকরণটি সন্তুষ্ট হয়।

গ [math]g(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-x)+g(x)=\cos x+\sin x=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt2}[/math] এবং [math]\sin x+\cos x=\sqrt2\sin\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)[/math], তাই [math]\sin\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] এবং সমাধানগুলো হল [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{12}+2\pi k[/math] বা [math]x=\tfrac{7\pi}{12}+2\pi k[/math], যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #133 (ID: 442426)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board

Question:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\varphi(x)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math] এবং

[math]\mathrm{h}(\theta)=\cos \theta-\sin \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\frac{2}{9}[/math]
খ. [math]\varphi(\mathbf{x})+\varphi(y)+\varphi(\mathbf{z})=\pi[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1[/math]
গ. [math](-\pi, \pi)[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]h(\theta)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}\text { L.H.S. } & =\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\& \Rightarrow 1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-1+\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\& \Rightarrow -\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2} \\& \Rightarrow -\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2} \\&\Rightarrow -\frac{1}{9}+\frac{1}{3}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{-1+3}{9}=\frac{2}{9}=\text { R.H.S. [Proved] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\varphi(x)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
এবং [math]\varphi(x)+{\varphi}(\mathrm{y})+\varphi(\mathrm{z})=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y+\cos ^{-1} z=\pi \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\}=\pi-\cos ^{-1} z \\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=\cos \left(\pi-\cos ^{-1} z\right) \\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-\cos \cos ^{-1} z \\\Rightarrow x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}=-z \\\Rightarrow x y+z=\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (x y+z)^{2}=1-x^{2}-y^{2}+x^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [ বর্গ একক ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x^{2} y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1-x^{2}-y^{2}+x^{2} y^{2} \\\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2 x y z=1 \text { [Showed] }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]h(\theta)=\cos \theta-\sin \theta[/math]
এবং [math]h(\theta)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \cos \theta-\sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \cos \theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cdot \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+(-1)^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}-\sin \theta \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math][\because \cos A \cos B-\sin A \sin B=\cos (A+B)][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \theta+\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\begin{aligned}\therefore \theta & =2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}-\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\& \Rightarrow 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{12}, 2 n \pi-\frac{7 \pi}{12} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{aligned}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]-\pi<\theta<\pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মান নির্ণয় :
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=-\frac{7 \pi}{12}, \frac{\pi}{12} \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক যদি [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}\tfrac{1}{3}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\sin\alpha=\sqrt{1-(1/3)^2}=\tfrac{2\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math] এবং [math]\beta=\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\cos\beta=\sqrt{1-(1/3)}=\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}[/math]; তাই [math]\sin^2\alpha-\cos^2\beta=\tfrac{8}{9}-\tfrac{6}{9}=\tfrac{2}{9}[/math].

খ ধরে নিন [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}x,\ \beta=\cos^{-1}y,\ \gamma=\cos^{-1}z[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta+\gamma=\pi[/math]; তাহলে cos-এর যোগ সূত্র প্রয়োগ করলে বিস্তৃত বিযুক্তির সরলকরণ থেকে সহজেই পাওয়া যায় যে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}+2xyz=1[/math]।

গ লিখুন [math]\cos\theta-\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}\cos\!\left(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\right)[/math], সুতরাং [math]\sqrt{2}\cos\!\left(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\right)=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] হলে [math]\cos\!\left(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\right)=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] অর্থাৎ [math]\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{3}+2k\pi[/math]; (-π,π) ব্যবধিতে সমাধানগুলো হল [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{12}[/math] এবং [math]\theta=-\tfrac{7\pi}{12}[/math]।

Question #134 (ID: 442427)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math] এর মান বের কর ।
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(\theta))+\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)+\sqrt{3} f(\theta)=\sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\begin{aligned}\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) & =1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \cdot\left[\because \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta=1\right] \\& \Rightarrow 1-\left\{\sin \cdot\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2} \\& \Rightarrow 1-\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2} \\& \Rightarrow 1-\frac{1}{3} \\& \Rightarrow \frac{3-1}{ 3} \\& \Rightarrow \frac{2}{3} \text { [Ans.] }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{f}(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{f}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right) \doteq \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)=\cos 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বামপক্ষ [math]=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} f(\theta))+\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 \theta\right)}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
[সূত্র [math]\left.\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\sin ^{-1}\left(x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}+y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{r}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{2 \sin ^{2} \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\right\} \\{\left[\because 2 \sin ^{2} \theta=1-\cos 2 \theta\right]}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \cdot \sqrt{2} \sin \theta+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos 2 \theta\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(2 \sin ^{2} \theta+1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right)\left[\because 1-\cos 2 \theta=2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} 1 \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \sin \frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2} \quad \therefore\end{array}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ [math]\text { [Proved] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\cos \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)+\sqrt{3} f(\theta)=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cdot \frac{1}{2}+\sin \theta \cdot \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math]
 উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{4}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{4}\\\Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=2 n \pi+\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{12} ; n \in Z \mid A \text { Ass. } \mid[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক যেহেতু [math]\sin \alpha=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math], তাই [math]\cos^2\alpha=1-\sin^2\alpha=1-\tfrac{1}{3}=\tfrac{2}{3}[/math] এবং [math]\cos^{2}\!\left(\sin^{-1}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=\tfrac{2}{3}[/math]।

খ প্রথমটি হল [math]\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin\theta)[/math] এবং দ্বিতীয়টি হল [math]\sin^{-1}\!\big(\sqrt{\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-2\theta)}\big)=\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2\theta})[/math]; উভয় আর্গুমেন্ট অ-বেষ্ণু তাই আউটপুট [math][0,\tfrac{\pi}{2}][/math]-এ। যদি [math]A=\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{2}\sin\theta)[/math] তাহলে [math]\cos A=\sqrt{1-2\sin^2\theta}=\sqrt{\cos 2\theta}[/math], যা সমান [math]\sin\big(\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2\theta})\big)[/math]। অতএব দুইটি কোণ সাম্যের এবং [math]A+\sin^{-1}(\sqrt{\cos 2\theta})=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয়।

গ [math]\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\cos\theta[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\cos\theta+\sqrt{3}\sin\theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]. বামপাশের অ্যাম্প্লিটিউড [math]R=\sqrt{1+3}=2[/math] দিয়ে [math]2\sin(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{6})=\sqrt{2}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{6})=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]. তাই সাধারন সমাধান হলো [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{12}+2k\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\tfrac{7\pi}{12}+2k\pi[/math], যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #135 (ID: 442425)

Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১ : [math]A=3 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২ : [math]f(x)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{-1} \sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] এর মুখ্য মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ হতে দেখাও যে, [math]A=\tan ^{-1} 3[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ হতে [math]2\{f(x)\}^{2}-11 f(x)+5=0[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর। যেখানে, [math]0^{\circ} \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 এর জন্য
[Image]
[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{11}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin ^{1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} \sin ^{} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow\cos ^{-1} \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{4} \cdot \mid \text { Ans. }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 এর জন্য
[Image]
[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\tan ^{-1} 1[/math]
এবং [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 এর জন্য
[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
দেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{aligned}A & =3 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\& \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}-4\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{3}\right\}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\right.\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 3 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1}\left(3 x-4 x^{3}\right)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}-\frac{4}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 1+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1+\frac{1}{2}}{1-1 \cdot \frac{1}{2}}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{2+1}{2}}{\frac{2-1}{2}}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \cdot\left(\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{2}{1}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore A=\tan ^{-1} 3 \quad \text { [Showed] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore 2\{f(x)\}^{2}-11 f(x)+5=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} x-11 \sin x+5=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} x-10 \sin x-\sin x+5=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x(\sin x-5)-1(\sin x-5)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (\sin x-5)(2 \sin x-1)=0[/math]
হয় [math]\sin x-5=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin x=5[/math]
ইহা গ্রহণযোগ্য নয় ৷কারণ, [math]\sin x[/math]
 এর সর্বোচ্চ মান [math]1[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \sin x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin x=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore x=n \pi+,(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
এখন, [math]0 \leq x \leq 2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]n[/math]
 এর বিভিন্ন মানের জন্য x এর মানসমূহ,[math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = 1[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n = -1[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=-\frac{7 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\left[-\frac{7 \pi}{6}\right.[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়][math]n = 2[/math]
 হলে, [math]x=\frac{13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\left[\frac{13 \pi}{6}\right.[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য নয় ][math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6} \text { |Ans.| }[/math]

Question #136 (ID: 442424)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board

Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] এবং [math]g(y)=\cos y[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math]f(x)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)+f(3 x)=1+g(x)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর।
গ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{f\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)}{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{f(\alpha) \cdot g(\beta)}{g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\beta\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha\right)}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}& \sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{8+3}{6}}{\frac{6-4}{6}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{11}{6} \times \frac{6}{2}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2} \text { [Ans:] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(3 x)=\sin 3 x[/math]
এবং [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cos 2 x[/math]
আবার, [math]g(y)=\cos y[/math]
 [math]\therefore g(x)=\cos x[/math]
এবং [math]g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\sin 2 x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(x)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)+f(3 x)=1+g(x)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x+\sin 2 x+\sin 3 x=1+\cos x+\cos 2 x \\\Rightarrow \sin 3 x+\sin x+\sin 2 x=1+\cos 2 x+\cos x \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{3 x+x}{2} \cos \frac{3 x-x}{2}+\sin 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} x+\cos x\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \sin C+\sin D=2 \sin \frac{C+D}{2} \cos \frac{C-D}{2} ; 2 \cos ^{2} x=1+\cos 2 x\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin 2 x \cos x+\sin 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} x+\cos x \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x(2 \cdot \cos x+1)=\cos x(2 \cos x+1) \\\Rightarrow (2 \cos x+1) \sin 2 x-(2 \cos x+1) \cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \cos x+1)(\sin 2 x-\cos x)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore 2 \cos x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x=-1 \\\Rightarrow \cos x=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos {x}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin 2 x-\cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin x \cos x-\cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos x(2 \sin x-1)=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x-1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in Z[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান,[math]x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3},(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in Z|A n s .|[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 এবং [math]g(y)=\cos y[/math]
 [math]\text { L.H.S. }=2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{f\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)}{f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2}}{\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2}}{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2}} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{2 \cdot \frac{\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}}{1-\frac{\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}} \mid\right.[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left\{\frac{2 \sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}{\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)}\right\}[/math]
এখন, [math]2 \sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \left(2 \sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \frac{\alpha}{2}\right) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left\{2 \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \sin 2\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right) \cdot|\because \sin 20=2 \sin \theta \cos \theta|\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\beta\right) \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \cos \beta\end{array}[/math]
$$ এবং [math]\cos ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin ^{2} \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin ^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\Rightarrow & \left\{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)+\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\} \\& \left\{\cos \frac{\alpha}{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)-\sin \frac{\alpha}{2} \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\right\} \cdot\left[\because a^{2}-b^{2}=(a+b)(a-b)\right] \\\Rightarrow & \cos \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \cdot \cos \left(\frac{\alpha}{2}-\frac{\beta}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math][\because \cos A \cos B+\sin A \sin B=\cos (A+B)]^{}[/math]
[ এবং [math]\cos A \cdot \cos B-\sin A \sin B=\cos (A+B)][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \left\{\frac{\alpha}{2}+\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\} \cdot \cos \left\{\frac{\alpha}{2}-\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2}\left\{\cos 2 \cdot \frac{\alpha}{2}+\cos 2 \cdot\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math][\because 2 \cos A \cos B=\cos (A+B)+\cos (A-B)][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2}\left\{\cos \alpha+\cos \left(\beta-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{.2}(\cos \alpha+\sin \beta)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { L.H.S. }=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2} \sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\frac{1}{2}(\cos \alpha+\sin \beta)}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\sin \beta+\cos \alpha} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sin \alpha \cos \beta}{\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\beta\right)+\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha\right)} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{f(\alpha) \cdot \operatorname{s}(\beta)}{g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\beta\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha\right)}=\text { R.H.S |Proved }\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #137 (ID: 442423)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১ : [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২ : [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)+\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{1+y^{2}}{1-y^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+z^{2}}{2 z}\right)=\pi[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. [math](-2 \pi, 2 \pi)[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]f(x)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] মীকরণটি সমাধান কর।
গ. উদ্দীপক-২ হতে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]x+y+z=x y z[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে
[Image]
[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে
[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}& \cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow & \cot \cos ^{-1} \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow & \cot \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow & \cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow & \frac{3}{4} \text { |Ans }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\therefore f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right) & =\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right) \\& \Rightarrow \sin x\end{aligned}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]f(x)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \sin x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \cos x+\sin x=1\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos x+\frac{1}{2} \cdot \sin x=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}+1^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে পাই][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\sin x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [যেখানে [math]n=0, \pm 1, \pm 2 \ldots][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\frac{2 \pi+\pi}{6}, 2 n \pi-\frac{2 \pi-\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math](-2 \pi, 2 \pi)[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে x এর মান
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x \doteq-\frac{3 \pi}{2},-\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{11 \pi}{6} \text {. [Ans.] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গউদ্দীপক-২ হতে, [math]\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)+\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{1+y^{2}}{1-y^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+z^{2}}{2 z}\right)=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x+\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-y^{2}}{1+y^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 z}{1+z^{2}}=\pi\\\left[\because \tan ^{-1} x=\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{x} ; \sin ^{-1} x=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1}{x} ; \cos ^{-1} x=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right]\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} y+\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} z=\pi\\\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y+\tan ^{-1} z=\pi\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{x+y+z-x y z}{1-x y-y z-z x}\right)={\pi}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y+z-x y z}{1-x y-y z-z x}=\tan \pi\\\Rightarrow \frac{x+y+z-x y z}{1-x y-y z-z x}=0\\\Rightarrow x+y+z-x y z=0\\\therefore x+y+z=x y z \text{ [Proved]}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\frac{3}{4}[/math], তাই [math]\sin\theta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]. ফলে [math]\cot\big(\cos^{-1}(\sin\theta)\big)=\cot\big(\cos^{-1}\tfrac{3}{5}\big)=\frac{3/5}{4/5}=\frac{3}{4}[/math].

খ সমীকরণে [math]\cos(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-x)=\sin x[/math], ফলে [math]\cos x+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\sin x=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]. এটি [math]\tfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\cos\big(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\big)=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] বা [math]\cos\big(x-\tfrac{\pi}{6}\big)=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], তাই [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math] বা [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2k\pi[/math]; (-2π,2π) মধ্যে মানগুলো: [math]\{-\tfrac{3\pi}{2},\tfrac{\pi}{2},-\tfrac{\pi}{6},\tfrac{11\pi}{6}\}[/math]।

গ প্রথম টার্ম থেকে [math]\cot^{-1}\tfrac{1}{x}=\tan^{-1}x[/math]; যেভাবে [math]\sec^{-1}\tfrac{1+y^2}{1-y^2}=2\tan^{-1}y[/math] এবং [math]\csc^{-1}\tfrac{1+z^2}{2z}=2\tan^{-1}z[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি দেয় [math]\tan^{-1}x+\tan^{-1}y+\tan^{-1}z=\pi[/math]. ত্রিকোণী যোগের সূত্র অনুযায়ী [math]\tan(\text{sum})=\dfrac{x+y+z-xyz}{1-(xy+yz+zx)}=0[/math], অতএব সংখ্যার উপরিপদ শূন্য হওয়ায় [math]x+y+z=xyz[/math]।

Question #138 (ID: 442422)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{array}{l}A=\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}, B=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{p}{q}, C=\sin ^{-1} r \\f(x)=\sin \alpha x, g(x)=\sin \beta x .\end{array}[/math]

Options:
ক. দেখাও যে, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
খ. [math]p=3, q=5, r=\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math] হলে প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]A-B+C=\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]\alpha=1, \beta=3[/math] হলে [math]-\pi[/math] হতে [math]\pi[/math] ব্যবধির মধ্যে [math]2 f(x) \cdot g(x)=1[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান নির্ণয় কর ।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কধরি, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\tan \theta[/math]
আমরা জানি,[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan 2 \theta=\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \\\Rightarrow \tan 2 \theta=\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}} \\\therefore 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}\\\text{[Showed]}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}[/math]
 এর জন্য,
[Image]
[math]\therefore \sec ^{-1} \cdot \sqrt{5}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\right)[/math]
 এর জন্য
[Image]
[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
দেওয়া আছে,[math]p=3, q=5[/math]
 এবং [math]r=\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
উদ্দীপক হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{A}=\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5} \\\mathrm{~B}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{Q}}{\mathrm{q}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]C=\sin ^{-1} r=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}& \Rightarrow \sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2}\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right) \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2 \\& \Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\text { R.H.S. }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \quad \text { [Proved] }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin \alpha x[/math]
 এবং [math]g(x)=\sin \beta x[/math]
এখন, [math]2 f(x) g(x)=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin \alpha x \cdot \sin \beta x=1\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x \sin 3 x=1,[\because \alpha=1, \beta=3]\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin 3 x \sin x=1\\\Rightarrow \cos (3 x-x)-\cos (3 x+x)=1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x-\cos 4 x=1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=1+\cos 4 x\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} 2 x\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} 2 x-\cos 2 x=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x(2 \cos 2 x-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয়, [math]\cos 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} !\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]2 \cos 2 x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \mathrm{x}=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]-\pi
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=-\frac{5 \pi}{6},-\frac{3 \pi}{4},-\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{3 \pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\text { [Ans.| }[/math]


Explanation:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}x[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\theta=x[/math] এবং [math]\tan(2\theta)=\dfrac{2\tan\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}=\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math], ফলে [math]2\tan^{-1}x=\tan^{-1}\dfrac{2x}{1-x^2}[/math] (প্রধান শাখায় মানমান ধরে)। 

খ [math]\sec A=\sqrt5[/math] থেকে [math]\cos A=1/\sqrt5,\ \sin A=2/\sqrt5[/math] এবং [math]\sin(2B)=3/5[/math] হলে অর্ধ-কোণ সূত্রে [math]\sin B=\sqrt{\dfrac{1-\cos(2B)}{2}}=\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math], তাই [math]B=C[/math]. ফলে [math]A-B+C=A[/math] এবং [math]\cot A=\dfrac{\cos A}{\sin A}=\dfrac{1/\sqrt5}{2/\sqrt5}=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], তাই [math]A=\cot^{-1}\tfrac{1}{2}[/math].

গ [math]2\sin x\sin3x=1[/math] হলে [math]2\sin x\sin3x=6\sin^2x-8\sin^4x=1[/math] নিয়ে [math]8s^4-6s^2+1=0[/math] (যেখানে [math]s=\sin x[/math]) হয়, ফলে [math]s^2=1/2[/math] অথবা [math]s^2=1/4[/math]. সুতরাং [math]\sin x=\pm 1/\sqrt2[/math] অথবা [math]\pm 1/2[/math], এবং [math]-\pi থেকে [math]\pi মধ্যে সমাধানগুলো হলো [math]x=\pm\pi/4,\ \pm3\pi/4,\ \pm\pi/6,\ \pm5\pi/6[/math].

Question #139 (ID: 442421)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১ : [math]M=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২ : [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] ও [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math] (সংশোধিত)
খ. উদ্দীপক-১ হতে দেখাও যে, [math]M[/math] এর মান [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]f(x)+g(x)=g(2 x)+f(2 x)[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} y=\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}[/math]
দেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} y\\\Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} y\right)\\\Rightarrow x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y\\\Rightarrow x=\sin \sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\\\Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \text{.}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}=1-\mathrm{y}^{2}[/math]
 [ উভয় পক্ষকে বর্গ করে ][math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1 \quad \text { [Showed] }[/math]
বি: দ্র: মূল প্রশ্নটি হলো, [math]\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=1[/math]
 হলে, দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
যা প্রমাণ করা সম্ভব নয়। তাই সংশোধন করে প্রশ্নটির সমাধান করা হয়েছে।[math]\\[/math]
 খ [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে
[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
দেওয়া আছে,[math]\begin{aligned}M & =\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\& \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3^{}}{5}+\frac{1}{2}\left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right) \\ &\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}} \\& {\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right] }\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{10}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2 \\\therefore M=\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \text { [Showed] }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
এবং [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\begin{array}{l}f(x)+g(x)=g(2 x)+f(2 x) \\\Rightarrow \sin x+\cos x=\cos 2 x+\sin 2 x \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x+\cos 2 x=\sin x+\cos x \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x-\sin x=\cos x-\cos 2 x \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{2 x+x}{2} \sin \frac{2 x-x}{2}=2 \sin \frac{x+2 x}{2} \sin \frac{2 x-x}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \sin C-\sin D=2 \cos \frac{C+D}{2} \sin \frac{C-D}{2}\right][/math]
[ এবং [math]\left.\cos C-\cos D=2 \sin \frac{C+D}{2} \sin \frac{D-C}{2}\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2}=\sin \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2} \sin \frac{x}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{x}{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2}\right)=0 \\\therefore \sin \frac{x}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{x}{2}=n \pi \\\therefore x=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা,[math]\begin{array}{l}\begin{array}{l}\cos \frac{3 x}{2}-\sin \frac{3 x}{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{3 x}{2}=\cos \frac{3 x}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \frac{3 x}{2}}{\cos \frac{3 x}{2}}=1\end{array}\\\Rightarrow \tan \frac{3 x}{2}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\\\Rightarrow \frac{3 x}{2}=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{2}{3}\left(n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান : [math]x=2 n \pi, \frac{2}{3}\left(n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \mid \text { Ans. } \mid[/math]


Explanation:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\alpha=\cos^{-1}x,\ \beta=\cos^{-1}y[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta=\pi/2[/math]; তখন [math]y=\cos\beta=\cos(\pi/2-\alpha)=\sin\alpha[/math] এবং [math]x=\cos\alpha[/math], ফলে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=\cos^{2}\alpha+\sin^{2}\alpha=1[/math]।

খ যদি [math]A=\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt5}[/math] তাহলে [math]\cos A=\frac{1}{\sqrt5},\ \sin A=\frac{2}{\sqrt5}[/math] এবং [math]A=\tan^{-1}2[/math]। যদি [math]B=\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{5}[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]\tan\frac{B}{2}=\frac{\sin B}{1+\cos B}=\frac{3/5}{1+4/5}=\frac{1}{3}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\frac{B}{2}=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}[/math], সুতরাং মাঝের দুটি পদ পরস্পরকে নস্যাৎ করে এবং [math]M=A=\tan^{-1}2=\cot^{-1}\frac{1}{2}[/math] হয়।

ঘ লিখতে পারি [math]\sin x+\cos x=\sqrt2\sin\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)[/math] এবং [math]\cos2x+\sin2x=\sqrt2\sin\bigl(2x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)[/math], তাই [math]\sin(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4})=\sin(2x+\tfrac{\pi}{4})[/math]. ফলত বা্‌জায়ঃ (i) [math]x+{\pi\over4}=2x+{\pi\over4}+2k\pi\Rightarrow x=2k\pi[/math] এবং (ii) [math]x+{\pi\over4}=\pi-(2x+{\pi\over4})+2k\pi\Rightarrow x={\pi\over6}+{2k\pi\over3}[/math], যেখানে [math]k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]।

Question #140 (ID: 442418)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin A=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}, \cos B=\frac{4}{5}, \cot C=3[/math]

এবং [math]\mathrm{g}(\theta)=\cos \theta-\cos 7 \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin \cdot \tan ^{-1} \cdot \cot \cdot \cos ^{-1} y=y[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]A-\frac{1}{2} B+C=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
গ. যদি [math]g(\theta)=\sin 4 \theta[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\sin \tan ^{-1} \cot \cos ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \tan ^{-1} \cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{y}{\sqrt{1-y^{2}}} \\=\sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{\sqrt{1-y^{2}}} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{y}{1} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} y\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{y}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \sin \tan ^{-1} \cot \cos ^{-1} y=y[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক হতে পাই, [math]\sin A=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \therefore A=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cos B=\frac{4}{5} \therefore B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5} \\\cot C=3 \therefore C=\cot ^{-1} 3\end{array}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\frac{1}{2} B=\theta[/math]
 .........(i)[math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos 2 \theta=\frac{4}{5}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{4}{5}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]4+4 \tan ^{2} \theta=5-5 \tan ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]9 \tan ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{9}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan \theta=\frac{1}{3} . \therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{1}{2} \mathrm{~B}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [ (i) হতে ]বামপক্ষ [math]=A-\frac{1}{2} B+C[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\cot ^{-1} 3 \\=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{1}=\tan ^{-1} 2=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \mathrm{A}-\frac{1}{2} \mathrm{~B}+\mathrm{C}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 গউদ্দীপক হতে পাই, [math]g(\theta)=\cos \theta-\cos 7 \theta[/math]
আবার [math]g(\theta)=\sin 4 \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos \theta-\cos 7 \theta=\sin 4 \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 4 \theta \sin 3 \theta-\sin 4 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 4 \theta(2 \sin 3 \theta-1)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\sin 4 \theta=0[/math]
 অথবা, [math]2 \sin 3 \theta-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]4 \theta=n \pi[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 3 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]3 \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{n \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]\theta=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{4}, \frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18} ; \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
যখন n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা। [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরি [math]\theta=\cos^{-1}y\in[0,\pi][/math]. entonces [math]\cot(\cos^{-1}y)=\cot\theta[/math], [math]\tan^{-1}(\cot\theta)=\tan^{-1}(\tan(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta))=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math] (কারণ [math]\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\in(-\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{\pi}{2})[/math]) এবং তাই [math]\sin(\tan^{-1}(\cot(\cos^{-1}y)))=\sin(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\cos\theta=y[/math].

খ [math]\sin A=2/\sqrt{5}\Rightarrow \tan A=2\Rightarrow A=\tan^{-1}2[/math], [math]\cos B=4/5\Rightarrow \tan B=3/4\Rightarrow B=\tan^{-1}(3/4)[/math], এবং [math]\cot C=3\Rightarrow \tan C=1/3\Rightarrow C=\tan^{-1}(1/3)[/math]. কারণ [math]2\tan^{-1}x=\tan^{-1}\!\left(\frac{2x}{1-x^2}\right)[/math], [math]2\tan^{-1}(1/3)=\tan^{-1}(3/4)[/math], ফলে [math]\tfrac12B=C[/math] এবং [math]A-\tfrac12B+C=A=\tan^{-1}2[/math].

গ [math]g(\theta)=\cos\theta-\cos7\theta=-2\sin\big(4\theta\big)\sin\big(-3\theta\big)=2\sin4\theta\sin3\theta[/math]. যদি [math]g(\theta)=\sin4\theta[/math], তবে [math]2\sin4\theta\sin3\theta=\sin4\theta[/math], ফলে বা তো [math]\sin4\theta=0\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{n\pi}{4}[/math] অথবা [math]2\sin3\theta=1\Rightarrow \sin3\theta=\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \theta=\tfrac{\pi}{18}+\tfrac{2k\pi}{3}\ অথবা \theta=\tfrac{5\pi}{18}+\tfrac{2k\pi}{3}[/math] (যেখানে [math]n,k\in\mathbb{Z}[/math]).

Question #141 (ID: 442419)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x=a \cos P, y=b \cos Q[/math] এবং [math]f(z)=\tan z \cdot \tan 3 z[/math]

Options:
ক. যদি [math]\sin ^{-1} m+\sin ^{-1} n=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] তবে দেখাও যে, [math]m^{2}+n^{2}=1[/math]
খ. যদি [math]P+Q=\psi[/math] হয় তবে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2 x y}{a b} \cos \psi+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \psi[/math]
গ. যদি f (z) = 1 হয় তবে z এর মান নির্ণয় কর, যখন [math]-\frac{\pi}{2} \leq \mathrm{z} \leq \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কদেওয়া আছে, [math]\sin ^{-1} m+\sin ^{-1} n=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin ^{-1} m=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} n[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]m=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} n\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]m=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} n\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{m}=\cos \left\{\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-\mathrm{n}^{2}}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{m}=\sqrt{1-\mathrm{n}^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]m^{2}=1-n^{2}[/math]
সুতরাং [math]\mathrm{m}^{2}+\mathrm{n}^{2}=1[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো) [math]\\[/math]
 খএই সমাধানের দ্রষ্টব্যঃউদ্দীপক-১ হতে পাই, [math]\cos x=\frac{p}{a}[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos y=\frac{q}{b}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{p}}{\mathrm{a}}[/math]
 এবং [math]y=\cos ^{-1} \frac{q}{b}[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে, [math]x+y=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{p}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{q}{b}=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{p q}{a b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{p^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{q^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}\right)}=\cos \alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}}-\cos \alpha=\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{p^{2} q^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-\frac{2 p q}{a b} \cos \alpha+\cos ^{2} \alpha=1-\frac{p^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{q^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{p^{2} q^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}+\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}-\frac{2 \mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}} \cos \alpha=1-\cos ^{2} \alpha[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}-\frac{2 \mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}} \cos \alpha+\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \alpha[/math]
 [math]b^{2} p^{2}- \text{2abpq} \cos \alpha+a^{2} q^{2}=a^{2} b^{2} \sin ^{2} \alpha[/math]
. (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(z)=\tan z \cdot \tan 3 z[/math]
 এবং [math]f(z)=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan 3 z \cdot \tan z=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\sin 3 z}{\cos 3 \ {z}} \frac{\sin z}{\cos z}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos 3 z \cos z-\sin 3 z \sin z=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos (3 z+z)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos 4 z=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]4 z=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{z}=(2 \mathrm{n}+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
; যেখানে n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।n = - 3 হলে [math]z=-\frac{5 \pi}{8} ; n=-2[/math]
 হলে [math]z=-\frac{3 \pi}{8}[/math]
n = − 1 হলে [math]z=-\frac{\pi}{8} ; n=0[/math]
 হলে [math]z=\frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
n = 1 হলে [math]\mathrm{z}=\frac{3 \pi}{8} ; \mathrm{n}=2[/math]
 হলে [math]z=\frac{5 \pi}{8}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত [math]-\frac{\pi}{2} \leq \mathrm{z} \leq \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 সীমার মধ্যে নির্ণেয় সমাধান :[math]z=-\frac{3 \pi}{8}, \frac{-\pi}{8}, \frac{\pi}{8}, \frac{3 \pi}{8} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\sin^{-1}m=\alpha,[/math] [math]\sin^{-1}n=\beta[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]; তখন [math]n=\sin\beta=\sin\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\alpha\right)=\cos\alpha[/math] (কারণ [math]\alpha\in[-\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{\pi}{2}][/math] হলে [math]\cos\alpha\ge0[/math]), তাই [math]n^{2}=\cos^{2}\alpha=1-\sin^{2}\alpha=1-m^{2}[/math] এবং ফলত [math]m^{2}+n^{2}=1[/math]।

খ [math]\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}=\cos^{2}P,[/math] [math]\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\cos^{2}Q[/math] এবং [math]\cos\psi=\cos(P+Q)=\cos P\cos Q-\sin P\sin Q[/math] ব্যবহার করে বামপক্ষকে সরল করলে তা [math]\sin^{2}(P+Q)[/math] এ পরিণত হয়; ফলে [math]\frac{x^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{2xy}{ab}\cos\psi+\frac{y^{2}}{b^{2}}=\sin^{2}\psi[/math] সত্য হয়।

গ [math]\tan z\tan 3z=1[/math] থেকে [math]\tan 3z=\cot z=\tan\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-z\right)[/math], তাই [math]3z=\frac{\pi}{2}-z+k\pi[/math] এবং [math]z=\frac{\pi}{8}+k\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]; [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}\le z\le\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] এর অধীনে সমাধানগুলো হল [math]z=\pm\frac{\pi}{8},\ \pm\frac{3\pi}{8}[/math]।
