Question #142 (ID: 442420)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} x, g(x)=\cos x[/math]

Options:
ক. সমাধান কর: [math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math].
খ. দেখাও যে, [math]f\left(\sqrt{2} \mathrm{~g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)+f(\sqrt{\mathrm{g}(2 \theta)})=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math].
গ. সমাধান কর: [math]\mathrm{g}(\mathrm{x})+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{~g}^{\prime}(\mathrm{x})=\sqrt{2}[/math], যখন [math]-\pi<\mathrm{x}<\pi[/math].


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক [math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]\theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
যেখানে n-এর মান শূন্য বা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা। (Ans.) [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
 এবং [math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\mathrm{f}\left(\sqrt{2} \mathrm{~g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)+\mathrm{f}(\sqrt{\mathrm{g}(2 \theta)})[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=f\left(\sqrt{2} \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)+f(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}) \\=f(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+f(\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}) \\=\sin ^{-1}(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)+\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \sqrt{1-\cos 2 \theta}+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta}\right\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\{\sqrt{2} \sin \theta \times \sqrt{2} \sin \theta+\sqrt{\cos 2 \theta} \cdot \sqrt{\cos 2 \theta}\} \\=\sin ^{-1}\left\{2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos 2 \theta\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1}\left(2 \sin ^{2} \theta+1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right)=\sin ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{2}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \mathrm{f}\left(\sqrt{2} \mathrm{~g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\right)+\mathrm{f}(\sqrt{\mathrm{g}(2 \theta)})=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো) [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{g}^{\prime}(\mathrm{x})=-\sin \mathrm{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ দাঁড়ায়, [math]\cos x-\sqrt{3} \sin x=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos x-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
[ উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+(\sqrt{3})^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos x \cos \frac{\pi}{3}-\sin x \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(x+\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow x+\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{3}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]
সুতরাং [math]x=2 n \pi-\frac{7 \pi}{12}, 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
 যখন n এর মান শূন্য বা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা।অতঃপর, n = − 1 হলে, [math]x=\frac{-31 \pi}{12}, \frac{-25 \pi}{12}[/math]
n = 0 হলে, [math]x=-\frac{7 \pi}{12},-\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
n = 1 হলে, [math]x=\frac{17 \pi}{12}, \frac{23 \pi}{12}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত [math]-\pi[math]x=-\frac{7 \pi}{12},-\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\tan2\theta=\dfrac{2\tan\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}[/math] ব্যবহার করে [math]\dfrac{2\tan^2\theta}{1-\tan^2\theta}=1\Rightarrow 3\tan^2\theta=1[/math] ফলে [math]\tan\theta=\pm\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math], তাই [math]\theta=n\pi\pm\dfrac{\pi}{6}[/math] (n পূর্ণসংখ্যা)।

খ [math]g\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\bigr)=\cos(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\sin\theta[/math], সুতরাং দুই সংখ্যাটি হল [math]a=\sqrt{2}\sin\theta,\; b=\sqrt{\cos2\theta}[/math] এবং [math]a^2+b^2=2\sin^2\theta+\cos2\theta=1[/math]। দুইটি অপ্রতীক (nonnegative) হলে [math]\arcsin a+\arcsin b=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি সঠিক (ডোমেইন নিশ্চিত করার শর্ত: [math]\cos2\theta\ge0[/math] এবং [math]\sqrt{2}\sin\theta\in[0,1][/math])।

গ [math]\cos x-\sqrt{3}\sin x=R\cos(x+\phi)[/math] দিয়ে গণনা করলে [math]R=2,\;\phi=\tfrac{\pi}{3}[/math], ফলে [math]2\cos\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{3}\bigr)=\sqrt{2}\Rightarrow \cos\bigl(x+\tfrac{\pi}{3}\bigr)=\tfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}[/math]. তাই [math]x+\tfrac{\pi}{3}=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{4}+2k\pi[/math] থেকে (-π,π) মধ্যে [math]x=-\tfrac{\pi}{12},\;- \tfrac{7\pi}{12}[/math]।

Question #143 (ID: 442417)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]A=\sin ^{-1} \cdot \frac{2}{3}, B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}, C=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] এবং [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\cos ^{-1} x=2 \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math]
খ. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]A-\frac{1}{2} B+C=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{35}-1}{\sqrt{7}+\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে [math]f(x)+f(2 x)+f(3 x)=0[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর, যখন [math]0 \leq \mathrm{x} \leq \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কধরি, [math]y=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]x=\cos y[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]1+x=1+\cos y[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]1+x=2 \cos ^{2} \frac{y}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1+x}{2}=\cos ^{2} \frac{y}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \frac{y}{2}=\sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{y}{2}=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]y=2 \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} x=2 \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{A}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}, \mathrm{~B}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
এবং [math]C=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
বামপক্ষ, [math]=\mathrm{A}-\frac{1}{2} \mathrm{~B}+\mathrm{C}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
ধরি, [math]\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos 2 \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]4-4 \tan ^{2} \theta=3+3 \tan ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]7 \tan ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{7}} ;[\because \theta[/math]
 সূক্ষকোণ ][math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{7}} \therefore \frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{7}}[/math]
তাহলে, [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]

[Image]
[math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{7}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{7}}}{1+\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \frac{1}{\sqrt{7}}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2 \sqrt{7}-\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{35}}}{1+\frac{2}{\sqrt{35}}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \sqrt{7}-\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{35}+2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{2 \sqrt{7}-\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{35}+2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}}{1-\frac{2 \sqrt{7}-\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{35}+2} \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 \sqrt{35}-5+\sqrt{35}+2}{2 \sqrt{5}+5 \sqrt{7}-2 \sqrt{7}+\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3 \sqrt{35}-3}{3 \sqrt{5}+3 \sqrt{7}}\right)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{35}-1}{\sqrt{7}+\sqrt{5}}\right)=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore A-\frac{1}{2} B+C=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{35}-1}{\sqrt{7}+\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 এবং [math]f(x)+f(2 x)+f(3 x)=0[/math]
তাহলে, [math]\sin x+\sin 2 x+\sin 3 x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 3 x+\sin x+\sin 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 2 x \cos x+\sin 2 x=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin 2 x(2 \cos x+1)=0[/math]
হয় [math]\sin 2 x=0[/math]
 অথবা [math]2 \cos x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 x=n \pi[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x=-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
n এর মান শূন্য অথবা যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]x=0, \frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \pi \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ক নোট করুন [math]\theta=\cos^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math] হলে [math]\cos2\theta=2\cos^2\theta-1=2\cdot\frac{1+x}{2}-1=x[/math], তাই [math]2\theta=\cos^{-1}x[/math] (প্রিন্সিপাল মান ধরে) এবং ফলস্বরূপ [math]\cos^{-1}x=2\cos^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{1+x}{2}}[/math].

খ প্রথমে [math]\sin A=2/3,\cos A=\sqrt{5}/3\Rightarrow \tan A=2/\sqrt{5}[/math], [math]\tan C=1/\sqrt{5}[/math] থেকে [math]\tan(A+C)=\sqrt{5}[/math]; আর [math]\cos B=3/4\Rightarrow \tan\frac{B}{2}=\sqrt{\frac{1-\cos B}{1+\cos B}}=1/\sqrt{7}[/math]. তাই
[math]\tan\big(A-\tfrac{B}{2}+C\big)=\frac{\sqrt{5}-1/\sqrt{7}}{1+\sqrt{5}\cdot(1/\sqrt{7})}=\frac{\sqrt{35}-1}{\sqrt{7}+\sqrt{5}}[/math], ফলে অরকট্যান দেওয়া রূপে মেলে।

গ [math]\sin x+\sin2x+\sin3x= (\sin x+\sin3x)+\sin2x=2\sin2x\cos x+\sin2x=\sin2x(2\cos x+1)=0[/math].従って [math]\sin2x=0\Rightarrow x=0,\tfrac{\pi}{2},\pi[/math] অথবা [math]2\cos x+1=0\Rightarrow \cos x=-\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3}[/math] (প্রাহিত সীমায়), সুতরাং সমাধান: [math]x=0,\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{2\pi}{3},\pi[/math].

Question #144 (ID: 442416)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


Options:
ক. [math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} x[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. উদ্দীপকে [math]\angle \mathrm{BAC}=\alpha[/math] হলে, [math]\alpha+\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] থেকে দেখাও যে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপক অনুসারে [math]x+y=\sqrt{2}[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর যখন [math]-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
প্রদত্ত রাশি,[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \mathrm{x} \\=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{x^{2}-1} \\=\sin \cot ^{-1} \sqrt{x^{2}-1} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{x} \\=\frac{1}{x} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]\angle \mathrm{BAC}=\alpha[/math]
 এবং [math]\alpha+\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{ABC}[/math]
 একটি সমকোণী ত্রিভুজ এবং [math]\angle \mathrm{B}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \theta=\frac{A B}{A C}=\frac{y}{1}=y[/math]
এবং [math]\cos \theta=\frac{\mathrm{BC}}{\mathrm{AC}}=\frac{\mathrm{x}}{1}=\mathrm{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো) [math]\\[/math]
 গখ’ হতে পাই, [math]{\mathrm{x}}=\cos \theta[/math]
 এবং [math]y=\sin \theta[/math]
দেওয়া আছে, [math]x+y=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\sin \theta \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=1[/math]
 [ উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \quad \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=(8 \mathrm{n}+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [n যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা ]এখন, [math]\mathrm{n}=0, \pm 1, \pm 2[/math]
 ....... বসিয়ে,n = 0 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4} ; n=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
n = - 1 হলে, [math]\theta=-\frac{7 \pi}{4} ; \mathrm{n}=2[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=\frac{17 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=-2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=-\frac{15 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore-2 \pi<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta[/math]
 এর মান : [math]\frac{\pi}{4},-\frac{7 \pi}{4} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\sec^{-1}x[/math], ফলে [math]\cos\theta=1/x[/math] এবং [math]\tan(\sec^{-1}x)=\tan\theta[/math]. যদি [math]\phi=\cot^{-1}(\tan\theta)[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]\cot\phi=\tan\theta\Rightarrow \tan\phi=\cot\theta=\tan\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\bigr)[/math], তাই [math]\phi=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math] এবং [math]\sin(\cot^{-1}\tan\sec^{-1}x)=\sin\phi=\cos\theta=1/x[/math] (এখানে [math]|x|\ge1[/math])। 

খ যদি [math]\alpha+\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math], তাহলে [math]\alpha=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math]; তাই [math]\cos\alpha=\sin\theta[/math] এবং [math]\sin\alpha=\cos\theta[/math], সুতরাং [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=\sin^{2}\theta+\cos^{2}\theta=1[/math]। 

গ ধরে নিন [math]x=\sin\theta,[/math] [math]y=\cos\theta[/math] (উপরে পাওয়া সম্পর্ক অনুযায়ী), তখন [math]\sin\theta+\cos\theta=\sqrt{2}\Rightarrow \sqrt{2}\sin\bigl(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)=\sqrt{2}[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin\bigl(\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\bigr)=1[/math]. তাই [math]\theta+\tfrac{\pi}{4}=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math], ফলে [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+2k\pi[/math]; সীমা [math]-2\pi<\theta<2\pi[/math] এ মানগুলো হল [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}[/math] এবং [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{4}-2\pi[/math]।

Question #145 (ID: 442412)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Mymensingh Board | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]q=\tan ^{-1} p,-\infty

দৃশ্যকল্প-২: [math]f(x)=\cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এর সমীকরণটির লেখচিত্র অংকন কর।
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এর সাহায্যে [math]\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}+4\{(\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}-5=0[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর৷


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
প্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]=\sin \cos ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
[চিত্র ১ হতে]
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\end{array}[/math]
[চিত্র ২ হতে] [math]\\[/math]
 খপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\mathrm{q}=\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{p},-\inftyগদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\tan x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}+4\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}-5=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{2} x+4 \tan x-5=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{2} x+5 \tan x-\tan x-5=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan x(\tan x+5)-1(\tan x+5)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\therefore(\tan x+5)(\tan x-1)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\tan x=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [ যেখানে n শূন্য বা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা ]অথবা, [math]\tan x=-5[/math]
 [math]\tan \mathrm{x}=\tan \theta[/math]
 [ ধরি [math]-5=\tan \theta \left.\therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1}(-5)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore x=n \pi+\theta \\=n \pi+\tan ^{-1}(-5)\end{array}[/math]
 [যেখানে n এর মান শূন্য বা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা][math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}, \mathrm{n} \pi+\tan ^{-1}(-5) ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\sec^{-1}\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math], ফলে [math]\sec\theta=\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] এবং [math]\cos\theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math], তাই [math]\tan\theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]। অতএব [math]\sin\big(\cos^{-1}(\tan\theta)\big)=\sin\big(\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\big)=\sqrt{1-\frac{1}{3}}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}=\frac{\sqrt{6}}{3}[/math]।

খ [math]q=\tan^{-1}p[/math] হল আরকট্যান জের গ্রাফ: ডোমেইন সব বাস্তব সংখ্যা, রেঞ্জ [math](-\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{\pi}{2})[/math], অনুভূমিক অসীমবশত: [math]q=\pm\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math] এবং এটি উৎপত্তি দিয়ে (0,0) দিয়ে যায় ও ক্রমবর্ধমান। 

গ প্রথমে [math]f(x)=\cot\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-x\big)=\tan x[/math] পাওয়া যায়, তাই সমীকরণ হচ্ছে [math]\tan^2x+4\tan x-5=0[/math]। [math]t=\tan x[/math] নিলে [math]t^2+4t-5=0\Rightarrow t=1\text{ বা }t=-5[/math], ফলে সমাধানগুলো হল [math]\tan x=1\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{4}+n\pi[/math] এবং [math]\tan x=-5\Rightarrow x=-\arctan 5 + n\pi\,(n\in\mathbb{Z})[/math]।

Question #146 (ID: 442413)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\begin{aligned}f(x)= & \sin ^{-1} p+\sin ^{-1} q+\sin ^{-1} r \\& A=\cos x-\cos 2 x, R=1-\cos x\end{aligned}[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]f(x)=\pi[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]\mathrm{p} \sqrt{1-\mathrm{p}^{2}}+\mathrm{q} \sqrt{1-\mathrm{q}^{2}}+\mathrm{r} \sqrt{1-\mathrm{r}^{2}}=2 \mathrm{pqr}[/math]
গ. সমাধান কর [math]\frac{\mathrm{A}}{\mathrm{R}}=1[/math] যখন [math]0<\mathrm{x}<\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কবামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \times 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}\right)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{9+1}{9}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ [math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক হতে পাই, [math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} p+\sin ^{-1} q+\sin ^{-1} r[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})=\pi[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin ^{-1} p+\sin ^{-1} q+\sin ^{-1} r=\pi[/math]
মনে করি [math]\sin ^{-1} p=A \therefore p=\sin A[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{1-\mathrm{p}^{2}}=\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} \mathrm{~A}}=\cos \mathrm{A}[/math]
আবার, [math]\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{q}=\mathrm{B} \therefore \mathrm{q}=\sin \mathrm{B}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{1-\mathrm{q}^{2}}=\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} \mathrm{~B}}=\cos \mathrm{B}[/math]
এবং [math]\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{r}=\mathrm{C} \because \mathrm{r}=\sin \mathrm{C}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{1-r^{2}}=\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} C}=\cos C[/math]
তাহলে, [math]\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B}+\mathrm{C}=\pi[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=p \sqrt{1-p^{2}}+q \sqrt{1-q^{2}}+r \sqrt{1-r^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin A \cos A+\sin B \cos B+\sin C \cos C \\=\frac{1}{2}(2 \sin A \cos A+2 \sin B \cos B+2 \sin C \cos C) \\=\frac{1}{2}(\sin 2 A+\sin 2 B+2 \sin C \cos C) \\=\frac{1}{2}\{2 \sin (A+B) \cos (A-B)+2 \sin C \cos C\} \\=\frac{1}{2}\{2 \sin C \cos (A-B)+2 \sin C \cos C\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math][\because \sin (A+B)=\sin (\pi-C)=\sin C][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \sin C[\cos (A-B)+\cos C] \\=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \sin C[\cos (A-B)-\cos (A+B)]\end{array}[/math]
 [math][\because \cos C=\cos \{(\pi-(A+B)\}=-\cos (A+B)][/math]
 [math]=\sin C \cdot 2 \sin A \sin B=2 \sin A \sin B \sin C[/math]
 [math]=2 \mathrm{pqr}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ (দেখানো হলো) [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]A=\cos x-\cos 2 x[/math]
 এবং [math]R=1-\cos x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\frac{A}{R}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\cos x-\cos 2 x}{1-\cos x}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x-\cos 2 x=1-\cos x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x+\cos x=1+\cos 2 x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \cos x=2 \cos ^{2} x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{2} x-\cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos x(\cos x-1)=0[/math]
হয় [math]\cos x=0[/math]
 অথবা [math]\cos x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=(2 \mathrm{n}+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=2 n \pi,(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 যেখানে [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
n = 0 হলে [math]\mathrm{x}=0, \frac{\pi}{2} ; \mathrm{n}=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-2 \pi, \frac{-\pi}{2}[/math]
n = 1 হলে [math]x=2 \pi, \frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্দিষ্ট ব্যবধির মধ্যে সমাধান [math]x=\frac{\pi}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক ধরা যাক [math]\theta=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}[/math], তাহলে [math]\tan\theta=\frac{1}{3}[/math] এবং [math]\sin2\theta=\frac{2\tan\theta}{1+\tan^2\theta}=\frac{2\cdot\frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}}=\frac{3}{5}[/math]; তাই [math]2\theta=\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{5}[/math] এবং [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{3}=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{-1}\frac{3}{5}[/math].

খ ধরি [math]\alpha=\sin^{-1}p,\;\beta=\sin^{-1}q,\;\gamma=\sin^{-1}r[/math] এবং [math]\alpha+\beta+\gamma=\pi[/math]; তাহলে [math]p\sqrt{1-p^2}=\tfrac{1}{2}\sin2\alpha[/math] ইত্যাদি, ফলে বামের সমষ্টি [math]p\sqrt{1-p^2}+q\sqrt{1-q^2}+r\sqrt{1-r^2}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\sin2\alpha+\sin2\beta+\sin2\gamma)[/math]. কিন্তু [math]\alpha+\beta+\gamma=\pi[/math] হলে পরিচিত ত্রিকোণমিতিক পরিচয় থেকে [math]\sin2\alpha+\sin2\beta+\sin2\gamma=4\sin\alpha\sin\beta\sin\gamma=4pqr[/math], তাই সমীকরণটি [math]=2pqr[/math] হয়।

গ সমাধান করতে চাই [math]\dfrac{A}{R}=1[/math] যেখানে [math]A=\cos x-\cos2x,\;R=1-\cos x[/math]; মানে [math]\cos x-\cos2x=1-\cos x[/math] যা সরল করলে [math]2\cos x-\cos2x-1=0[/math] এবং [math]\cos2x=2\cos^2x-1[/math] ব্যবহার করে পাওয়া যায় [math]2\cos x(1-\cos x)=0[/math]; দেওয়া শর্তে [math]0<x<\pi[/math] এ [math]\cos x=1[/math] যায় না, তাই [math]\cos x=0\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}[/math]।

Question #147 (ID: 442414)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
দৃশ্যকল্প-১: [math]P=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{3}\right), Q=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{2}\right)[/math]

দৃশ্যকল্প-২: [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math].

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।
খ. দৃশ্যকল্প-১ এ [math]P+Q=\theta[/math] হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]4 x^{2}-12 x y \cos \theta+9 y^{2}=36 \sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
গ. দৃশ্যকল্প-২ এ [math](0,2 \pi)[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]f(x)+f(2 x)+f(3 x)=1+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)+f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান কর।


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত রাশি [math]=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=1-\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2}-1+\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\right\}^{2} \\=1-\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}-1+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}=1-\frac{1}{3}-1+\frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{6} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]P=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{3}\right)[/math]
 এবং [math]Q=\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{2}\right)[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে, [math]P+Q=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{3}\right)+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{2}\right)=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{x y}{3.2}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{3^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{2^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{x y}{6}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{9}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\right)}=\cos \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{x y}{6}-\cos \theta=\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{9}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}+\cos ^{2} \theta-2 \cdot \frac{x y}{6} \cdot \cos \theta=\left(1-\frac{x^{2}}{9}\right)\left(1-\frac{y^{2}}{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}+\cos ^{2} \theta-\frac{x y}{3} \cos \theta=1-\frac{x^{2}}{9}-\frac{y^{2}}{4}+\frac{x^{2} y^{2}}{36}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{x^{2}}{9}+\frac{y^{2}}{4}-\frac{x y}{3} \cos \theta=1-\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{4 x^{2}+9 y^{2}-12 x y \cos \theta}{36}=\sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore 4 x^{2}-12 x y \cos \theta+9 y^{2}=36 \sin ^{2} \theta[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 গদেওয়া আছে, [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(2 x)=\sin 2 x ; f(3 x)=\sin 3 x[/math]
;[math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)=\cos x[/math]
এবং [math]f\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-2 x\right)=\cos 2 x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\sin x+\sin 2 x+\sin 3 x=1+\cos x+\cos 2 x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 3 x+\sin x+\sin 2 x=1+\cos 2 x+\cos x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 2 x \cos x+\sin 2 x=2 \cos ^{2} x+\cos x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 2 x(2 \cos x+1)-\cos x(2 \cos x+1)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math](2 \cos x+1)(\sin 2 x-\cos x)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math](2 \cos x+1)(2 \sin x \cos x-\cos x) = 0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math](2 \cos x+1) \cos x(2 \sin x-1)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos x(2 \cos x+1)(2 \sin x-1)=0[/math]
হয় [math]\cos x=0 \quad \therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা [math]2 \cos x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x=\frac{-1}{2}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
অথবা [math]2 \sin x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3},(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
যখন n এর মান শূন্য বা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা।n = 0 হলে, [math]x= \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
n = 1 হলে, [math]x=\frac{8 \pi}{3}, \frac{4 \pi}{3}, \frac{3 \pi}{2}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
n = 2 হলে, [math]x=\frac{14 \pi}{3}, \frac{10 \pi}{3}, \frac{5 \pi}{2}, \frac{13 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore 0[math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \frac{4 \pi}{3}, \frac{3 \pi}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:
ক [math]\sin^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\alpha[/math] ধরলে [math]\sin\alpha=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}},\ \cos^{2}\alpha=1-\frac{1}{3}=\frac{2}{3}[/math]; এবং [math]\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\beta[/math] হলে [math]\sin^{2}\beta=1-\frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]; তাই পার্থক্যটি [math]\frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{6}[/math]।

খ [math]\cos P=\frac{x}{3},\ \cos Q=\frac{y}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\sin(P+Q)=\sin\theta=\sin P\cos Q+\cos P\sin Q[/math] থেকে গুনফলগুলোকে 6 দিয়ে গুণ করলে [math]6\sin\theta=3y\sin P+2x\sin Q[/math]; উভয় পাশে বর্গ করলে ও [math]\sin^{2}P=1-\frac{x^{2}}{9},\ \sin^{2}Q=1-\frac{y^{2}}{4},\ \sin P\sin Q=\frac{xy}{6}-\cos\theta[/math] প্রতিস্থাপন করলে সরলীকরণে পাওয়া যায় [math]4x^{2}-12xy\cos\theta+9y^{2}=36\sin^{2}\theta[/math]।

গ সমীকরণকে লেখা যায় [math]\sin x+\sin2x+\sin3x=1+\cos x+\cos2x[/math], ডান পাশের [math]\cos(\cdot)[/math] ব্যবহার করে সরল করলে [math](1+2\cos x)(\sin2x-\cos x)=0[/math]; তাই প্রথমটি দিলে [math]\cos x=-\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{2\pi}{3},\tfrac{4\pi}{3}[/math], দ্বিতীয়টি দিলে [math]\cos x=0\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{2},\tfrac{3\pi}{2}[/math] বা [math]\sin x=\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow x=\tfrac{\pi}{6},\tfrac{5\pi}{6}[/math] (সবই (0,2π) এর মধ্যে)।

Question #148 (ID: 442415)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\cos x-\cos 7 x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
খ. [math]f(\alpha)=\sin 4 \alpha[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান নির্ণয় কর।
গ. [math]\mathrm{g}\left(\pi \mathrm{g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\mathrm{x}\right)\right)=\mathrm{g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \mathrm{g}(\mathrm{x})\right)[/math] হলে দেখাও যে, [math]x= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কমনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta \quad \therefore \tan \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \Rightarrow 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
অর্থাৎ, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 ......(i)আবার, [math]\cos 2 \theta=\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{x}^{2}} \Rightarrow 2 \theta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{x}^{2}}[/math]
অর্থাৎ, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 ........(ii)(i) ও (ii) নং হতে আমরা পাই,[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}f(x)=\cos x-\cos 7 x \\\therefore f(\alpha)=\cos \alpha-\cos 7 \alpha\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(\alpha)=\sin 4 \alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \alpha-\cos 7 \alpha=\sin 4 \alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 4 \alpha \sin 3 \alpha-\sin 4 \alpha=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 4 \alpha(2 \sin 3 \alpha-1)=0[/math]
হয়, [math]\sin 4 \alpha=0[/math]
 অথবা, [math]2 \sin 3 \alpha-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]4 \alpha=\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
 [math]2 \sin 3 \alpha=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \alpha=\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 3 \alpha=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]3 \alpha=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \alpha=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\alpha=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{4}, \frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]
যখন n এর মান শূন্য অথবা অন্য যেকোনো পূর্ণ সংখ্যা৷ [math]\\[/math]
 গপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]g\left(\pi g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)\right)=g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi g(x)\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin \left(\pi \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)\right)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\pi \sin x\right)[\because g(x)=\sin x][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin (\pi \cos x)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x\right) \quad \therefore \pi \cos x=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x \pm \sin x=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos x \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin x=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos x \pm \sin \frac{\pi}{4} \sin x=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos \left(x \mp \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]x \mp \frac{\pi}{4}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
 (দেখানো হলো) [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক x = tan[math]\theta[/math] ধরলে [math]\frac{2x}{1+x^{2}}=\sin2\theta[/math] এবং [math]\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}=\cos2\theta[/math], অতএব উভয় পক্ষ সমান হয়ে [math]2\theta[/math] প্রদর্শন করে যা দিকনির্দেশক সীমা মানে একই অনুলিপি দিলে [math]\sin^{-1}\frac{2x}{1+x^{2}}=\cos^{-1}\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]।

খ [math]f(\alpha)=\cos\alpha-\cos7\alpha=-2\sin4\alpha\sin(-3\alpha)=2\sin4\alpha\sin3\alpha[/math]; সমীকরণ হল [math]2\sin4\alpha\sin3\alpha=\sin4\alpha[/math] তাই [math]\sin4\alpha(2\sin3\alpha-1)=0[/math], ফলে (i) [math]\sin4\alpha=0\Rightarrow \alpha=\dfrac{n\pi}{4}[/math] অথবা (ii) [math]\sin3\alpha=\tfrac{1}{2}\Rightarrow \alpha=\dfrac{\pi}{18}+ \dfrac{2k\pi}{3}\ অথবা [math]\alpha=\dfrac{5\pi}{18}+ \dfrac{2k\pi}{3}[/math]।

গ [math]g(x)=\sin x[/math] হলে সমীকরণ হয় [math]\sin(\pi\cos x)=\cos(\pi\sin x)[/math], অর্থাৎ [math]\sin(\pi\cos x)=\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\pi\sin x\big)[/math]. এর দুই শাখা দেয় [math]\sin x+\cos x=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math] অথবা [math]\cos x-\sin x=\tfrac{1}{2}[/math], এগুলি অনুবর্তীভাবে লেখা যায় [math]\sin\big(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\big)=\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math] অথবা [math]\cos\big(x+\tfrac{\pi}{4}\big)=\tfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math], ফলে সাধারণ সমাধান compact করে লেখা যায় [math]x=\pm\dfrac{\pi}{4}+\cos^{-1}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}[/math] (উপযুক্ত পর্যায়ক্রমিক যোগ করা যায়)।

Question #149 (ID: 442411)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
উদ্দীপক-১: [math]\cos x=\frac{p}{a}, \cos y=\frac{q}{b} \text {. }[/math]

উদ্দীপক-২: [math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]

Options:
ক. প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{29}}{5}[/math]
খ. উদ্দীপক-১ এর সাহায্যে, x + y = a হলে, প্রমাণ কর যে, [math]b^{2} p^{2}- \text{2abpq} \cos \alpha+a^{2} q^{2}=a^{2} b^{2} \sin ^{2} \alpha[/math]
গ. উদ্দীপক-২ এর সাহায্যে, সমাধান কর: [math]f(x)-\sqrt{1-\{\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}}=1,-2 \pi<\mathrm{x}<2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
ক
[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}+\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{29}}{5} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{2}{5} \\=\frac{\pi}{2}\left[\because \tan ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\right] \\\therefore \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{29}}{5}\end{array}[/math]
(প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 খউদ্দীপক-১ হতে পাই, [math]\cos x=\frac{p}{a}[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos y=\frac{q}{b}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{p}}{\mathrm{a}}[/math]
 এবং [math]y=\cos ^{-1} \frac{q}{b}[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে, [math]x+y=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{p}{a}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{q}{b}=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1}\left\{\frac{p q}{a b}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{p^{2}}{a^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{q^{2}}{b^{2}}\right)}\right\}=\alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}}-\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}\right)}=\cos \alpha[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}}-\cos \alpha=\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{p^{2} q^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}-\frac{2 p q}{a b} \cos \alpha+\cos ^{2} \alpha=1-\frac{p^{2}}{a^{2}}-\frac{q^{2}}{b^{2}}+\frac{p^{2} q^{2}}{a^{2} b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}+\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}-\frac{2 \mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}} \cos \alpha=1-\cos ^{2} \alpha[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}}{\mathrm{a}^{2}}-\frac{2 \mathrm{pq}}{\mathrm{ab}} \cos \alpha+\frac{\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{~b}^{2}}=\sin ^{2} \alpha[/math]
 [math]b^{2} p^{2}- \text{2abpq} \cos \alpha+a^{2} q^{2}=a^{2} b^{2} \sin ^{2} \alpha[/math]
. (প্রমাণিত) [math]\\[/math]
 গউদ্দীপক-2 হতে পাই, [math]f(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore f(x)=\sin x[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]f(\mathrm{x})-\sqrt{1-\{f(\mathrm{x})\}^{2}}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x-\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} x}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x-\sqrt{\cos ^{2} x}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x-\cos x=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}-\cos x \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
[ উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x \cdot \cos \frac{\pi}{4}-\cos x \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin \left(x-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}-\frac{\pi}{4}=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{4} \therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
যেখানে, n এর মান 0 অথবা যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা।n = 0 হলে, [math]x=0+(-1)^{0} \frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
n = 1 হলে [math]x=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}=\pi[/math]
n = -1 হলে [math]x=-\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}=-\pi[/math]
n = 2 হলে [math]x=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}=\frac{5 \pi}{2}[/math]
n = - 2 হলে [math]x=-2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{4}=-\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সীমার মাঝে x এর প্রাপ্ত মানসমূহ [math]\frac{\pi}{2}, \pi,-\pi, \frac{-3 \pi}{2} .[/math]
 কিন্তুএদের মধ্যে [math]\pi[/math]
 এবং [math]-\pi[/math]
 মানগুলো দ্বারা প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ,[math]\sin x-\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} x}=1[/math]
 সিদ্ধ হয় না।[math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান: [math]\frac{\pi}{2},-\frac{3 \pi}{2} \text { (Ans.) }[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা:

ক [math]\tan^{-1}\frac{2}{5}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\csc^{-1}\frac{\sqrt{29}}{5}[/math] সত্য, কারণ যদি [math]\phi=\tan^{-1}\frac{2}{5}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\tan\phi=\frac{2}{5}[/math] থেকে ত্রিভুজে কল্পনা করলে [math]\sin\phi=\frac{2}{\sqrt{29}},\;\cos\phi=\frac{5}{\sqrt{29}}[/math]; যদি [math]\theta=\csc^{-1}\frac{\sqrt{29}}{5}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\sin\theta=\frac{5}{\sqrt{29}}[/math], ফলে [math]\tan\bigl(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta\bigr)=\frac{2}{5}[/math] এবং কৌণিক রেঞ্জ বিবেচনায় [math]\phi=\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta[/math] হয়।

খ শুরু করে [math]\cos(x+y)=\cos x\cos y-\sin x\sin y[/math] এবং [math]\cos x=\frac{p}{a},\;\cos y=\frac{q}{b}[/math] ব্যবহার করলে যায়:  
[math]ab\cos\alpha=pq-\sqrt{(a^{2}-p^{2})(b^{2}-q^{2})}[/math]; দুই পাশে বর্গ করে ও সরিয়ে লিখলে সহজে পাওয়া যায়  
[math]b^{2}p^{2}-2abpq\cos\alpha+a^{2}q^{2}=a^{2}b^{2}\sin^{2}\alpha[/math]।

গ [math]f(x)=\sin x[/math] হলে সমীকরণ [math]\sin x-\sqrt{1-\sin^{2}x}=1[/math] বা [math]\sqrt{1-\sin^{2}x}=\sin x-1[/math]; বাম পাশ অইনবদ্ধ (≥0) হওয়ায় [math]\sin x\ge1[/math] ও [math]\sin x\le1[/math] থেকে [math]\sin x=1[/math]. তাই [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{2}+2k\pi[/math] এবং সীমায় (−2π,2π) ভেতরে সমাধানগুলো: [math]x=\tfrac{\pi}{2},\;x=-\tfrac{3\pi}{2}[/math]।

Question #158 (ID: 442464)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: CQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: 
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]g(x)=\cos x ; h(x)=\sin x[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\cos 2 \theta+\sin \theta=1[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান বের কর ।
খ. উদ্দীপকের আলোকে [math]\sqrt{3} g(\theta)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=1[/math] সমীকরণটি সমাধান কর। যখন [math]0<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
গ. [math]g\{\pi h(\theta)\}=h\{\pi g(\theta)\}[/math] হলে দেখাও যে , [math]\theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{7}[/math]


Correct Answer: 

Solution:
কপ্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]\cos 2 \theta+\sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta=1-\cos 2 \theta \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=2 \sin ^{2} \theta \quad\left[\because 1-\cos 2 \theta=2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta-\sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta(2 \sin \theta-1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]2 \sin \theta-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]
অথবা, [math]\sin \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi[/math]
 অথবা [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\\[/math]
 খদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \mathrm{g}(\theta)=\cos \theta \\\begin{aligned}\therefore \mathrm{g}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) & =\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right) \\& \Rightarrow \sin \theta\end{aligned}\end{array}[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]\sqrt{3} g(\theta)+g\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=1 ; 0গদেওয়া আছে, [math]g(x)=\cos x[/math]
 [math]\therefore g(\theta)=\cos \theta[/math]
এবং [math]h(x)=\sin x[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{h}(\theta)=\sin \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ[math]\begin{aligned}\mathrm{g}(\pi \mathrm{h}(\theta)) & =\mathrm{h}(\pi \mathrm{g}(\theta)) \\\Rightarrow\mathrm{g}(\pi \sin \theta) & =\mathrm{h}(\pi \cos \theta) \\\Rightarrow\cos (\pi \sin \theta) & =\sin (\pi \cos \theta)\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right)=\sin (\pi \cos \theta)\left[\because \sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \theta\right)=\cos \theta\right] \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta=\pi \cos \theta \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \pm \sin \theta=\cos \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2} \pm \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta \pm \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \pm \sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \theta \pm \frac{\pi}{4}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta= \pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{7}[/math]
[দেখানো হলো]
[Image]


Explanation:
ক সমীকরণে [math]\cos2\theta=1-2\sin^2\theta[/math] স্থাপন করলে [math]-2\sin^2\theta+\sin\theta=0\Rightarrow\sin\theta( -2\sin\theta+1)=0[/math], তাই [math]\sin\theta=0[/math] বা [math]\sin\theta=\tfrac12[/math]. সুতরাং [math]\theta=n\pi[/math] অথবা [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{6}+2n\pi,\ \tfrac{5\pi}{6}+2n\pi\ (n\in\mathbb{Z}).

খ এখানে [math]g(\theta)=\cos\theta,\ g(\tfrac{\pi}{2}-\theta)=\sin\theta[/math] হওয়ায় সমীকরণটি হয় [math]\sqrt{3}\cos\theta+\sin\theta=1[/math]; এটা রূপান্তর করলে [math]2\cos(\theta-\tfrac{\pi}{6})=1[/math], থেকে [math]\cos(\theta-\tfrac{\pi}{6})=\tfrac12[/math], তাই [math]\theta=\tfrac{\pi}{2},\ \tfrac{11\pi}{6} (যেহেতু [math]0গ শর্তাধীন সমীকরণে [math]\cos(\pi\sin\theta)=\sin(\pi\cos\theta)=\cos\{\pi(\tfrac12-\cos\theta)\}.

Sub-Questions:
